//===== Ragnarok Offline: companion-party-overhaul (was companion-life) ======
//= Companions talk like people who live in this world with you: greetings
//= by time away, reactions to towns/fields/dungeons and level-ups, combat
//= callouts with RO emotes, and conversations with each other when calm.
//=
//= How it works: a timer on each player (every 1.5 s) reads the party.
//= Companions are population-engine shells (char id 95000000 and up) and
//= real party members, so readparam/strcharinfo by char id and
//= unittalk/emotion by account id reach them. The engine drops script
//= events for shells, so everything is polled from the owner's side.
//=
//= Lines: .<category>_<personality>$ arrays, {t} = the speaker's title for
//= you, {a} = another companion's name. Personalities: 0 Rookie (Novice),
//= 1 Guardian (Swordman), 2 Scholar (Mage, Soul Linker), 3 Wanderer
//= (Archer, Bard, Dancer, Gunslinger), 4 Healer (Acolyte, Priest),
//= 5 Brawler (Monk, Taekwon, Star Gladiator), 6 Rogue (Thief, Ninja),
//= 7 Merchant.
//=
//= 1.1.0: daily life (own level-ups and job changes, resting, you going
//= quiet, time of day, long sessions) here; party-chat answers and battle
//= reactions are the engine's companion strategies (app 1.5.4), in
//= db/when/<setting>/population_strategy.yml, made by tools/make_strategy.ps1.
//= 1.2.0: memories (mod store, char scope, m.c<companion char id>.*) and
//= @clstat for the Companions window (client/index.js). Growth and the
//= fallen-companion return are npc/companion_growth.txt / companion_return.txt.
//===========================================================================
-	script	CompanionLife	-1,{
	end;

OnInit:
	.mod$ = "companion-party-overhaul";
	// 3.0.0: fewer settings. Everyday chatter = greetings, world reactions, daily
	// life and the purse talk; Combat callouts carry the emotes; Look around carries
	// the scouts' patrol; Sit down when you wait carries sitting with a favourite and
	// the restless sitting.
	.chatter = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "everyday_chatter", 1);
	.greet = .chatter;
	.world = .chatter;
	.callouts = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "combat_callouts", 1);
	.emotes = .callouts;
	.banter = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "banter_minutes", 4);
	.life = .chatter;
	.mem = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "memories", 1);
	.moments = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "moments", 1);	// 3.9.1 (L_MomentTry)
	.quiz = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "quiz", 12);	// 3.10.2: minutes between pop quizzes, 0 off (L_QuizStart)
	.purse = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "purse_per_level", 2);
	.purse_talk = .chatter;
	// 3.1.0: the party loot bag (setting loot_finds: 0 off, 1 low, 2 normal, 3
	// generous). A party kill can turn up one thing from that monster's own drop
	// list, at this share (per thousand) of its normal rate (L_LootTrack).
	setarray .@lsh, 0, 100, 250, 500;

	// pet foods (Pet Food, Monster's Feed, Falcon and PecoPeco Food).

	.near = 4;

	else if (callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "spacing_far", 0))

	// Titles: comma list, trimmed; each companion keeps one (char id mod n).
	deletearray .ttl$;
	.nt = 0;
	explode(.@raw$, callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "titles", "Boss,Captain,Leader"), ",");
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@raw$); .@i++) {
		.@s$ = .@raw$[.@i];
		while (getstrlen(.@s$) > 0 && charat(.@s$, 0) == " ")
			.@s$ = delchar(.@s$, 0);
		while (getstrlen(.@s$) > 0 && charat(.@s$, getstrlen(.@s$) - 1) == " ")
			.@s$ = delchar(.@s$, getstrlen(.@s$) - 1);
		if (.@s$ != "") {
			.ttl$[.nt] = .@s$;
			.nt++;
		}
	}
	if (!.nt) {
		.ttl$[0] = "Boss";
		.nt = 1;
	}

	// ---- Warp Portal (3.3.0, part of chat_orders): "take us back" in party chat. Out
	// hunting, an Acolyte-line companion opens a portal to your save point and remembers
	// the spot (any map: the user chose the exact spot over the memo rule); in town, the
	// same words open one back there. Real costs: a Blue Gemstone (setting warp_gem) and
	// 35/32/29/26 SP; blocked only on nowarp maps, like the skill. Caster lines are for
	// personalities 4 (Healer) and 5 (Brawler): only the Acolyte line casts it.
	.warp_gem = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "warp_gem", 1);
	// Maximize Power off after a fight (3.4.0, L_MaxPow): only with party_roles, whose
	// ct_maxpow plan keeps the engine from casting it straight back on.
	.maxpow = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "party_roles", 1);

	// ---- Pets (3.2.0, setting "pets"): the companions you allow (Bag tab, Pet) bring
	// the classic tameable pet of their class, as the lore has it. Lookalikes with ids
	// of their own (db/mob_db.yml 31201-31213, the real sprites via System/jobname.lub):
	// no drops, spells or EXP. Each is its companion's slave (UMOB_MASTERAID), so
	// rAthena's slave AI (mob.cpp mob_ai_sub_hard_slavemob) has it follow and take its
	// companion's target. Monsters see a player's slave as a friend, players can't hit
	// it, and UMOB_DMGIMMUNE covers the rest. The slave AI kills it when its companion
	// is gone (a warp or teleport, a fall), and L_PetTick puts it back. Every pet is as
	// strong as the next (setting pet_strength): attack 2 x its companion's level, one
	// hit per 1.5 s, accuracy with level.
	.pets = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "pets", 1);
	.pet_pow = cap_value(callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "pet_strength", 100), 0, 200);

	// 3.9.1: the expanded classes' own life stories (L_ClassStory), told before their
	// personality's: story 11 Gunslinger, 12 Ninja, 13 Taekwon and Star Gladiator, 14

	// second of the class. Written for anyone (no .arcsex). Super Novice has the Rookie's

	// Gunslinger: Einbroch smog, a father on the factory floor, and a gun built from scrap.
	setarray .arcx11$,
		"ADo you know what I saw the first night I left Einbroch, {a}?^BA monster?^AStars. I was sixteen, and I'd never seen one.^BNever?^AThe smoke from the factories covers the whole sky there. Day and night. You forget there's anything above it.^BThat's sad.^AIt was just home. I didn't know it was sad until I left.",
		"#AMy father worked the factory floor in Einbroch. Twenty years. He came home black with soot every night.^BWhat did he make there?^AParts for airships. 		"#AA boiler burst on the factory floor. Six men. My father was one of them.^B{a}... I'm so sorry.^AThe owners called it worker error. I stood at his gate for an hour.^BWhat happened?^AI went home. I don't know if that makes me a coward or the only good thing I've ever done.",
		"#AA letter came from Einbroch. From one of the men my father worked with.^BGood news?^AThe workers made a guild. They stopped the factories for a week until the owners listened. New rules. 		"#AMy father used to say: a good gun is one you never need to fire. Building it is the point. 	// 3.10.2: the pop quiz rides on the moment steps as this one (never picked by the
	// scan; L_QuizStart fills in the question, L_QuizAnswer takes your answer).
	.qz_k = .mo_n;
	callsub L_MoAdd, "quiz", -1, 0, 0, "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "";

	// ======================================================================
	// 3.10.2: pop quiz lines, by personality (0 Rookie, 1 Guardian, 2 Scholar,
	// 3 Wanderer, 4 Healer, 5 Brawler, 6 Rogue, 7 Merchant). {q} the question,
	// {Q} the same with a capital, {m} the monster, {g} a guess, {r} the right
	// answer, {a} the other companion, {t} your title. Frames (L_QuizStart):
	// qzf0 plain, qzf1 a monster near you, qzf2 after an argument (qzc1 the other's
	// guess, qzc2 theirs), qzf3 after a bet (qzb1/qzb2), qzf4 a letter, qzf5 the
	// golden question. Replies: qzok/qzno right/wrong, qzwin/qzsulk who guessed
	// right in an argument, qzboth neither, qzgok/qzgno golden, qzdon the
	// "double or nothing" offer, qzdq its question, qzpass you passed.
	// ======================================================================
	setarray .qz_en$, "Neutral", "Water", "Earth", "Fire", "Wind", "Poison", "Holy", "Shadow", "Ghost", "Undead";
	setarray .qz_rn$, "Formless", "Undead", "Brute", "Plant", "Insect", "Fish", "Demon", "Demi-Human", "Angel", "Dragon";
	setarray .qz_sn$, "Small", "Medium", "Large";
	setarray .qz_cn$, "Strong", "Normal", "Weak";
	setarray .qzf0_0$, "{t}! {t}! Quick one: {q}", "Ooh, {t}, I just thought of something. {Q}";

	setarray .qzpass_1$, "Wise.";
	setarray .qzpass_2$, "Prudent.";
	setarray .qzpass_3$, "Leaving them wanting more. Smart.";
	setarray .qzpass_4$, "Of course.";
	setarray .qzpass_5$, "Chicken! Ha!";
	setarray .qzpass_6$, "Smart. Quit while you're ahead.";
	setarray .qzpass_7$, "Taking your profit. Respect.";

	initnpctimer;	// the tick below
	end;

// The tick: one NPC timer for everyone, every 1500 ms. `addrid 0, 1` runs the
// tick for each player but skips anyone busy in another script (an NPC
// dialog). Before, each player had a timer of their own, and during a long
// dialog those queued up until the queue was full and were dropped ("event
// queue is full"), which stopped them for good.
OnTimer1500:
	initnpctimer;
	addrid 0, 1;
	if (getcharid(3) >= 95000000)
		end;	// an AI player, not a person
	goto OnTick;

OnPCLoginEvent:
	@cl_away = -1;
	if (CL_LAST)
		@cl_away = gettimetick(2) - CL_LAST;
	@cl_greet = .greet;
	@cl_login = gettimetick(2);
	@cl_map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	@cl_bnext = gettimetick(2) + 180 + rand(120);
	@cl_long = gettimetick(2) + 7200;	// a long-session line after 2 hours
	@cl_afk = 0;
	@cl_pcd = gettimetick(2) + 600;	// the first purse line after 10 minutes
	@cl_pgain = 0;
	@cl_wnext = gettimetick(2) + 30;
	end;

OnPCLogoutEvent:
	CL_LAST = gettimetick(2);
	if (@cl_fire$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_fire$;	// the campfire goes out with you
	if (@cl_wp$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_wp$;	// and an open Warp Portal closes
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < 6; .@s++) {
		if (getd("@cl_mo" + .@s + "$") != "")
			unloadnpc getd("@cl_mo" + .@s + "$");	// and a crying pet goes home
	}
	end;

OnPCBaseLvUpEvent:
	@cl_plvl = gettimetick(2);	// 3.9.1: "you got stronger again!" (L_MomentTry)
	if (!.world || gettimetick(2) < @cl_lvl_cd)
		end;
	@cl_lvl_cd = gettimetick(2) + 20;
	callsub L_Gather;
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	if (.@a >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "lvl", "", ET_CONGRATULATION;
	end;

OnPCDieEvent:
	@cl_pdead = gettimetick(2);	// 3.9.1: "that last fall... are you alright?" (L_MomentTry)
	callsub L_Gather;
	// 3.13.0: you fell in a solo: it's over, they step in (and a Priest can raise you).
	if (@cl_hu_solo) {
		if (CL_DEBUG)
			callsub L_Dbg, "solo over: you fell";
		callsub L_SoloEnd;
	}
	if (.mem) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
			modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".sawfall", 1);
	}
	if (!.callouts)
		end;
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
	if (.@a >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "odead", "", ET_CRY;
	end;

// Memories: your kills while they're with you, and the MVPs among them.
// (Only the kills that are yours: the server gives no script event for a
// companion's own last hit.)
OnNPCKillEvent:
	if (!@cl_n)
		end;
	.@mvp = (getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_MVPEXP) > 0);
	if (.@mvp) {
		// for the companions to talk about afterwards
		@cl_lastmvp = gettimetick(2);
		@cl_lastmvp$ = getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_NAME);
		// A gift to celebrate, now and then (a quarter of MVPs).
		if (.gifts && @cl_n && rand(100) < 25) {
			.@g = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@g >= 0)
				callsub L_Gift, .@g, "mvp";
		}
		// 3.9.0: every pet out cheers, and one companion says so.
		if (.pets) {
			callsub L_PetEmotes, ET_CONGRATULATION, 100;
			.@a = callsub(L_PetPick, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0 && .chatter && rand(100) < 60)
				callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "petmvp", "", ET_DELIGHT, 0, callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@a]);
		}
	}
	// The party purse: a share of each of your kills for every living
	// companion with you (the monster's drops go to you as always).
	if (.purse > 0) {
		.@alive = 0;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
				.@alive++;
		}
		.@add = .purse * getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_LV) * .@alive;
		if (.@add > 0 && modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) < 1000000000) {
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@add);
			@cl_pgain += .@add;
		}
	}
	if (!.mem)
		end;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
		// 3.0.1: counted for the Memories tab only; no more "100 monsters!" lines.
		modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 1);
		if (!.@mvp)
			continue;
		.@v = modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 1);
		if (.@v == 1) {
			// The one boss milestone left: the first, by name.
			modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_NAME);
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "mvpms", "", ET_CONGRATULATION, 1, getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_NAME);
		}
	}
	end;

OnTick:
	// 3.11.3: off the loop guard for the whole tick. The guard counts every jump in the run, so a
	// crowded MVP room plus a party wipe ran out of it ("infinity loop") and the rest of the tick
	// was skipped. Every loop below is a bounded for, and the while near the end shrinks its list.
	freeloop(1);
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	.@now = gettimetick(0);

	// A conversation in progress: one line per turn, 3.5 s apart.
	if (getarraysize(@cl_qa) && .@now >= @cl_qnext) {
		callsub L_SayAid, @cl_qa[0], @cl_qn$[0], @cl_qt$[0];
		@cl_said = @cl_qa[0];	// a sitter may turn to whoever spoke last
		@cl_saidt = .@now;
		if (@cl_qe[0])
			callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_qa[0], @cl_qe[0];
		deletearray @cl_qa[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_qn$[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_qt$[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_qe[0], 1;
		@cl_qnext = .@now + 3500;
		// 3.9.1: the last line of a conversation: a moment of quiet (L_ConvEnd).
		if (@cl_cvon && !getarraysize(@cl_qa))
			callsub L_ConvEnd, .@sec;
	}
	// 3.9.1: a heartfelt talk's closing beat: the one it was said to, a heart; now and
	// then someone who listened, a quiet smile.
	// (Two ticks apart: one emote on screen every 2.5 s, L_EmoteAid.)
	if (@cl_cbat && .@sec >= @cl_cbat) {
		@cl_cbat = 0;
		if (@cl_cbb && @cl_cbe)
			callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_cbb, @cl_cbe;
		if (@cl_cbc && @cl_cbk)
			@cl_cbat2 = .@sec + 3;
	}
	// The listener (L_ConvEnd): 1 smiles, 2 answers the one who shared, 3 teases the
	// pair, 4 teases a flirty pair, 5 calms an argument, 6 laughs, 7 laughs out loud.
	if (@cl_cbat2 && .@sec >= @cl_cbat2) {
		@cl_cbat2 = 0;
		.@li = inarray(@cl_aid, @cl_cbc);
		if (.@li >= 0) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@li] > 0) {
				.@two$ = @cl_cvna$ + " and " + @cl_cvnb$;
				if (@cl_cbk == 1)
					callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_cbc, ET_SMILE;
				else if (@cl_cbk == 2)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@li, "react", @cl_cvna$, 0;
				else if (@cl_cbk == 3)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@li, "tease", .@two$, 0;
				else if (@cl_cbk == 4)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@li, "ftease", .@two$, 0;
				else if (@cl_cbk == 5)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@li, "peace", .@two$, 0;
				else if (@cl_cbk == 6)
					callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_cbc, ET_KIK;
				else if (@cl_cbk == 7)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@li, "laugh", @cl_cvna$, ET_KIK;
			}
		}
	}

	callsub L_Gather;
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	if (.@map$ != @cl_map$) {
		// Out of town: a new trip. The Loot tab marks what's found from here on as new.
		if (callsub(L_MapKind, @cl_map$) == "town" && callsub(L_MapKind, .@map$) != "town")
			modstore_set "char", "lttrip", .@sec;
		// 3.10.1: a shopping ask only comes back from the field or a dungeon, not
		// from a shop's interior.
		.@was$ = callsub(L_MapKind, @cl_map$);
		@cl_shok = (.@was$ == "field" || .@was$ == "dungeon");
		@cl_map$ = .@map$;
		@cl_mapat = .@sec + 4;	// give companions time to arrive
		@cl_names$ = "";	// tell the client their names again (L_Gather)
		@cl_cast$ = "";	// and the portraits who's who
	}
	// Warp Portal (3.3.0): it opens once the caster has had time to cast, and closes
	// when its time is up or you've gone through (a dynamic NPC stays on the old map
	// after you leave it, so it's always taken away here).
	if (@cl_wpat && .@now >= @cl_wpat)
		callsub L_WarpOpen;
	if (@cl_wp$ != "" && .@now >= @cl_wpend)
		callsub L_WarpClose;
	if (@cl_greet && .@sec > @cl_login + 90)
		@cl_greet = 0;	// nobody came back with you: no greeting later
	// 3.9.0: pets crying beside their fallen companions (L_PetMourn).
	if (.pets)
		callsub L_PetMourn, .@sec;
	if (!@cl_n)
		end;

	// The colours you dyed them (every 3 s: the engine respawns them with the
	// colours it saved at recruit).
	if (.@sec >= @cl_dyenext) {
		@cl_dyenext = .@sec + 3;
		callsub L_Dyes;
	}

	// The loot bag: monsters beaten round the party (L_LootTrack).
	if (.loot_share > 0)
		callsub L_LootTrack;

	// 3.2.0: the pets of the companions you allowed (L_PetTick).
	if (.pets)
		callsub L_PetTick, .@sec;
	// 3.9.0: "Can we keep it?" (L_PetAsk). Bonds come from Memories, so it needs those.
	if (.pets && .mem)
		callsub L_PetAsk, .@sec;
	// 3.9.1: "hey boss" moments (L_MomentTry; 3.10.2: and the pop quiz). Bond tiers come from Memories.
	if ((.moments || .quiz) && .mem)
		callsub L_MomentTry, .@sec;
	// 3.10.1: the shopping ask, and the trip once they're out (L_ShopTick).
	if (@cl_shs || cl_shop$ != "")
		callsub L_ShopTick, .@sec;
	// 3.9.1: a story invite nobody answered, or a fight broke out: it waits (L_StoryEnd).
	if (@cl_stc) {
		if (.@sec >= @cl_stt || @cl_incombat)
			callsub L_StoryEnd, 1;
	}

	// A companion we haven't seen this session: newly hired or drafted.
	// The ones that come back with you at login are marked quietly during
	// the first 20 s.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (getd("@cl_k_" + @cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		setd "@cl_k_" + @cl_aid[.@i], 1;
		if (@cl_ready && .greet && @cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "gnew", "", ET_DELIGHT;
		if (.mem)
			callsub L_MemSeen, .@i;
	}
	if (.@sec > @cl_login + 20)
		@cl_ready = 1;

	// Login greeting, by how long you were away.
	if (@cl_greet) {
		@cl_greet = 0;
		if (@cl_away < 0 || @cl_away >= 43200)
			.@cat$ = "gdays";
		else if (@cl_away < 1800)
			.@cat$ = "gshort";
		else
			.@cat$ = "ghours";
		if (@cl_away < 0)
			.@cat$ = "ghours";	// first login with the mod: no idea, assume a rest
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0) {
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, .@cat$, "", ET_DELIGHT;
			if (@cl_n > 1 && rand(100) < 60) {
				.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
				if (.@b >= 0)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, .@cat$, "", 0;
			}
		}
	}

	// You in danger.
	.@ohp = Hp;
	.@hurt = 0;
	if (@cl_ohp > 0 && .@ohp < @cl_ohp) {
		.@hurt = 1;
		@cl_hurt_aid = getcharid(3);	// the last one hurt: a Healer sits by them
		@cl_hurt_t = gettimetick(2);
	}
	@cl_ohp = .@ohp;
	if (.callouts && .@hurt && .@ohp > 0 && .@ohp * 100 / MaxHp < 30 && .@sec >= @cl_olow_cd) {
		@cl_olow_cd = .@sec + 45;
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "olow", "", ET_SURPRISE;
	}

	// Each companion: falls, revivals, big hits, low HP and SP.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		.@hp = @cl_hp[.@i];
		.@mhp = @cl_mhp[.@i];
		.@prev = getd("@cl_ph_" + .@aid) - 1;	// stored +1, so -1 = not seen yet
		setd "@cl_ph_" + .@aid, .@hp + 1;
		if (.@prev < 0 || .@mhp <= 0)
			continue;
		if (.@hp < .@prev) {
			.@hurt = 1;
			@cl_hurt_aid = .@aid;
			@cl_hurt_t = .@sec;
		}
		if (.@hp <= 0) {
			if (.@prev > 0 && .mem)
				modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".fell", 1);
			if (.@prev > 0 && .callouts) {
				.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
				if (.@b >= 0)
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@b, "down", @cl_nm$[.@i], ET_CRY;
			}
			continue;
		}
		if (.@prev == 0) {
			if (.callouts)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "revive", "", ET_THANKS;
			continue;
		}
		if (!.callouts || .@hp >= .@prev)
			continue;	// the rest only while taking damage
		.@pct = .@hp * 100 / .@mhp;
		if ((.@prev - .@hp) * 100 / .@mhp >= 35 && .@sec >= getd("@cl_sc_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_sc_" + .@aid, .@sec + 60;
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "scared", "", ET_PROFUSELY_SWEAT;
		} else if (.@pct < 15) {
			callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_HELP;
		} else if (.@pct < 35 && .@sec >= getd("@cl_lo_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_lo_" + .@aid, .@sec + 45;
			if (rand(100) < 50 && .@sec >= @cl_talk)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "low", "", ET_HPTIME;
			else
				callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_HPTIME;
		}
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@msp = @cl_msp[.@i];
		if (!.callouts || !@cl_incombat || .@msp <= 0 || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_sp[.@i] * 100 / .@msp >= 10 || .@sec < getd("@cl_spc_" + @cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		setd "@cl_spc_" + @cl_aid[.@i], .@sec + 120;
		if (rand(100) < 50 && .@sec >= @cl_talk)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "sp", "", ET_SPTIME;
		else
			callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_SPTIME;
	}

	// Their own milestones: a new job (which comes before a level line, a
	// rebirth lowers the level), or a level-up, with a word from another.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		.@pl = getd("@cl_lv_" + .@aid);
		.@pc = getd("@cl_cl_" + .@aid) - 1;	// stored +1: Novice is class 0
		setd "@cl_lv_" + .@aid, @cl_lv[.@i];
		setd "@cl_cl_" + .@aid, @cl_cls[.@i] + 1;
		if (!.world || .@pl <= 0 || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (.@pc >= 0 && .@pc != @cl_cls[.@i]) {
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "jobme", jobname(@cl_cls[.@i]), ET_CONGRATULATION;
			.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
			if (.@b >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, "lvcg", @cl_nm$[.@i], ET_CONGRATULATION;	// (ET_CLAP is no constant: it gave no emote)
		} else if (@cl_lv[.@i] > .@pl && .@sec >= getd("@cl_lvcd_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_lvcd_" + .@aid, .@sec + 30;
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "lvme", "", ET_CONGRATULATION;
			if (rand(100) < 15)
				callsub L_Gift, .@i, "lvl";
			.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
			if (.@b >= 0 && rand(100) < 60)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, "lvcg", @cl_nm$[.@i], 0;
		}
	}

	if (.life) {
		// Resting: out of a fight the engine sits a companion down below its "Rest
		// below" mark (SP or HP) and stands it up once both reach "until" (the Battle
		// tab). 3.10.2: by each one's own marks (L_RoamRead; 30/95 until first read).
		// 0 = never, as while any of yours roams (L_RoamRest); and not while out shopping.
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || @cl_mhp[.@i] <= 0)
				continue;
			.@hpp = @cl_hp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_mhp[.@i];
			.@spp = 100;
			if (@cl_msp[.@i] > 0)
				.@spp = @cl_sp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_msp[.@i];
			.@below = 30;
			.@until = 95;
			.@j = inarray(@cl_rsn$, @cl_nm$[.@i]);
			if (.@j >= 0) {
				.@below = @cl_rsb[.@j];
				.@until = @cl_rsu[.@j];
			}
			if (compare("|" + cl_shop$, "|" + @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|"))
				.@below = 0;	// out shopping (cl_shop$ "A|B|")
			if (.@below <= 0) {
				setd "@cl_rst_" + .@aid, 0;
				continue;
			}
			if (!getd("@cl_rst_" + .@aid)) {
				if (!@cl_incombat && (.@spp < .@below || .@hpp < .@below)) {
					setd "@cl_rst_" + .@aid, 1;
					if (.@sec >= getd("@cl_rcd_" + .@aid) && .@sec >= @cl_talk && rand(100) < 60 && callsub(L_Calm)) {
						setd "@cl_rcd_" + .@aid, .@sec + 300;
						callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "rest", "", 0;
					}
				}
			} else if (.@spp >= .@until && .@hpp >= .@until) {
				setd "@cl_rst_" + .@aid, 0;
				if (!@cl_incombat && .@sec >= @cl_talk && rand(100) < 50 && callsub(L_Calm))
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "ready", "", ET_OK;
			}
		}

		// You going quiet for 5 minutes, and coming back.
		.@idle = checkidle();
		if (!@cl_afk && .@idle >= 300 && callsub(L_Calm)) {
			@cl_afk = 1;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "afk", "", ET_QUESTION;
		} else if (@cl_afk && .@idle < 5) {
			@cl_afk = 0;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "back", "", ET_DELIGHT;
		}

		// Two hours out: someone (a healer first) suggests a break.
		if (@cl_long && .@sec >= @cl_long && !@cl_incombat && callsub(L_Calm)) {
			@cl_long = .@sec + 7200;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "long", "", ET_SWEAT;
		}
	}

	// Into a fight, and out of it.
	if (.@hurt) {
		if (!@cl_incombat) {
			@cl_incombat = 1;
			if (.callouts && .@sec >= @cl_fight_cd && rand(100) < 35) {
				@cl_fight_cd = .@sec + 90;
				.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
				if (.@a >= 0)
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "fight", "", ET_GO;
			}
			// 3.2.0: the pets perk up, and now and then one's companion calls to it.
			if (.pets) {
				callsub L_PetEmotes, ET_SURPRISE, 60;
				if (.callouts && .@sec >= @cl_pfight_cd && rand(100) < 25) {
					.@a = callsub(L_PetPick, -1);
					if (.@a >= 0) {
						@cl_pfight_cd = .@sec + 120;
						callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "petfight", "", 0, 0, callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@a]);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		@cl_combat_until = .@sec + 10;
	} else if (@cl_incombat && .@sec > @cl_combat_until) {
		@cl_incombat = 0;
		@cl_peace = .@sec;
		if (.callouts && .@sec >= @cl_win_cd && rand(100) < 30) {
			@cl_win_cd = .@sec + 120;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "win", "", ET_OK;
		}
	}

	if (.maxpow)
		callsub L_MaxPow, .@sec;

	// 3.8.0: potions and food, the user's rules (L_Drink).
	if (.drink_hp > 0)
		callsub L_Drink, .@sec;
	// 3.8.1: gear you gave that waits for the engine to take back the old piece.
	if (getarraysize(@cl_pwl))
		callsub L_WearLater;

	// Arriving somewhere.
	if (@cl_mapat && .@sec >= @cl_mapat && !@cl_incombat) {
		@cl_mapat = 0;
		if (.mem)
			callsub L_MemMap, .@map$;
		// Back in town: what the trip turned up, then the shopping lists.
		if (callsub(L_MapKind, .@map$) == "town") {
			callsub L_LootHaul;
			// 3.10.1: Ask waits for a calm moment (L_ShopTick); otherwise as before.
			if (.shop == 2 && @cl_shok && .@sec >= @cl_shnext && cl_shop$ == ""
					&& getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") > 0) {
				@cl_shs = 1;
				@cl_sht = .@sec + 60;
				@cl_shmap$ = .@map$;
			} else if (.shop)
				callsub L_Restock, 0;
		}
		@cl_shok = 0;
		if (.world && .@sec >= @cl_map_cd && rand(100) < 70 && callsub(L_Calm)) {
			.@cat$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@map$);
			if (.@cat$ != "") {
				.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
				if (.@a >= 0) {
					@cl_map_cd = .@sec + 90;
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, .@cat$, "", 0;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// 3.12.4: a word when the weather turns (Day & Night 1.4.0).
	if (.world && $@dn_wx_on)
		callsub L_Weather, .@sec, .@map$;

	// Calm: a conversation, or a thought out loud when there's only one.
	// A party of four or more (you and three companions) talks more often, and
	// more as a group (L_Conversation).
	if (.banter > 0 && !@cl_incombat && callsub(L_Calm) && .@sec >= @cl_bnext && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 20) {
		@cl_bnext = .@sec + .banter * 60 * (70 + rand(61)) / 100;
		if (@cl_n >= 3)
			@cl_bnext = .@sec + .banter * 60 * (70 + rand(61)) * 60 / 10000;
		// Late at night or early in the morning, sometimes a word about the hour
		// instead, at most once an hour. 3.0.1: the game's hour when the day-night
		// mod runs its clock ($@dn_on, night 21-5), else your own clock.
		// 3.9.1: whether it's a thought out loud, decided first. Was "&& callsub(L_Thought)"
		// in the else-if below: rAthena reads both sides of &&, so a thought was queued
		// at EVERY turn, roll or not, and then a conversation was queued right after it
		// (the off-beat line before a talk, and the "did you know"s far too often).
		.@thought = (.mem && .@sec >= @cl_soft && rand(100) < 30 - 15 * (@cl_n >= 3));
		.@lh = gettime(DT_HOUR);	// the server keeps your time zone (app 1.5.5)
		.@nf = 22;
		if ($@dn_on) {
			.@lh = $@dn_min / 60;
			.@nf = 21;
		}
		.@tod$ = "";
		if (.@lh >= .@nf || .@lh < 5)
			.@tod$ = "night";
		else if (.@lh >= 6 && .@lh < 10)
			.@tod$ = "morning";
		if (.life && .@tod$ != "" && .@sec >= @cl_tod_cd && rand(100) < 40) {
			@cl_tod_cd = .@sec + 3600;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, .@tod$, "", 0;
		} else if (.@thought) {
			callsub L_Thought;	// a thought out loud: lore, a small moment, an easter egg
		} else if (@cl_n >= 2) {
			// 3.9.1: now and then a chapter of someone's life story (L_ArcTry). Nested:
			// && reads both sides, and L_ArcTry queues lines.
			.@told = 0;
			if (.mem && rand(100) < 30)
				.@told = callsub(L_ArcTry, 0);
			if (!.@told) {
				// 3.9.1: no more Merchant first 40% of the time (it put the Whitesmith in
				// half of all talks); the partner leans to whom they know least.
				.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
				.@b = -1;
				if (.@a >= 0)
					.@b = callsub(L_PickFriend, .@a);
				if (.@a >= 0 && .@b >= 0)
					callsub L_Conversation, .@a, .@b, .@map$, .@tod$;
			}
		} else if (rand(100) < 70) {
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, "idle", "", 0;
		}
	}

	// The party purse: now and then someone (a merchant first) says how much
	// went in since the last time.
	if (.purse > 0 && .purse_talk && @cl_pgain >= 1000 && .@sec >= @cl_pcd && !@cl_incombat && !getarraysize(@cl_qa)) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
		if (.@a >= 0) {
			@cl_pcd = .@sec + 900;
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "purse", "", ET_MONEY, @cl_pgain, "";
			@cl_pgain = 0;
		}
	}

	// Gifts (setting gifts): once a minute in a quiet moment, each companion here
	// has a small chance, bigger the closer it is to you (about once in 3 hours
	// for a new one, twice as often for a close one). Not in the first 10 minutes.
	if (.gifts && !@cl_incombat && callsub(L_Calm) && .@sec >= @cl_gnext && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 15 && .@sec >= @cl_login + 600) {
		@cl_gnext = .@sec + 60;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			.@bd = callsub(L_PlayerBond, .@i);
			if (.@bd > 40)
				.@bd = 40;
			if (rand(4500) < .@bd + 10) {
				callsub L_Gift, .@i, "";
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	// 3.2.0: pets between fights: a happy emote now and then (sleepy once the party
	// has sat a while), and every few minutes a word about them (L_PetLife).
	if (.pets && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10)
		callsub L_PetLife, .@sec;

	// Campfire nights (setting campfire): the fire's tick, or after 5 minutes of you
	// standing still with no fight, they light one themselves (once per stop, and not
	// within 20 minutes of the last camp). 3.7.0: was an offer you had to say yes to.
	if (.camp) {
		if (@cl_camp)
			callsub L_CampTick, .@sec;
		else if (!CL_STANDBY && .@sec - @cl_still >= 300 && @cl_cofferd != @cl_still && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 30
				&& .@sec >= @cl_clast + 1200 && !getarraysize(@cl_qa)) {
			@cl_cofferd = @cl_still;
			if (callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1) >= 0)	// somebody here and standing
				callsub L_CampStart, 1;
		}
	}

	// Bonds (3.7.0, part of Memories): every minute together counts, a new tier is
	// announced, and the tier's max HP/SP bonus is kept up (6 minutes, renewed here).
	if (.mem && .@sec >= @cl_bmnext) {
		@cl_bmnext = .@sec + 60;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
			modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 1);
			.@bt = callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@i]));
			setd "@cl_bt_" + @cl_cid[.@i], .@bt;	// 3.9.1: for L_Title
			if (.@bt > modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".tier", 1)) {
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".tier", .@bt;
				dispbottom "[Companions] Your bond with " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " grew: " + .bt_name$[.@bt] + " (" + .bt_perk$[.@bt] + ").", 0xFFCC66;
				if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "bondup", "", ET_THROB;
			}
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
				callsub L_BondBuff, .@i, .@bt;
		}
	}

	// Companions roaming the map (app setting Companion roaming): they hunt on
	// their own, so the strolls, patrol laps and sitting below leave them be.
	// The engine saves the choice in its table at once; read every 5 s.
	if (.@sec >= @cl_rmnext) {
		@cl_rmnext = .@sec + 5;
		callsub L_RoamRead;
		// Sent off or recalled from the window (no chat line): no rest while roaming,
		// the thresholds back once none roams.
		callsub L_RoamRest;
		// 2.5.7: the idle Hold and Sit rules (spacing_*, idle_sit) let go of a companion
		// with the harmless Entry_Queue_Apply_Delay status, as for the strolls. Every
		// roamer keeps it (8 s, renewed every 5 s), so one that is roaming is never held
		// or sat down beside you: after a server restart and with the window's Roam
		// button too, where no chat line flips the rules' own roaming switch. 2.5.9: the
		// roam rules (db/when/roam_together) asked for it too. 3.0.0: they ask for a status
		// of their own, Entry_Queue_Notify_Admission_Time_Out, that only roamers get, so a
		// stroll near you no longer looks like roaming to them.
		// 3.7.0: the Orders tab's "Alone" (CL_ROAMALONE) leaves out that second status, so
		// the group rules let them roam each on their own.
		if (.roamhelp) {
			for (.@ri = 0; .@ri < @cl_n; .@ri++) {
				if (@cl_hp[.@ri] <= 0 || !callsub(L_Roams, .@ri))
					continue;
				sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@ri];
				// 3.10.1: and shoppers, who scatter round the town (L_ShopStart).
				// 3.11.0: and MVP hunters, who each fly about on their own (L_HuntTick).
				// 3.12.0: and anyone on Stand By, or the group rules would walk them together.
				if (!CL_ROAMALONE && !CL_HUNT && !CL_STANDBY && !callsub(L_Shopping, .@ri))
					sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_NOTIFY_ADMISSION_TIME_OUT, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@ri];
			}
		}
		// A new companion that starts unequipped gets its starter kit.
		if (.kit)
			callsub L_KitCheck;
	}
	// 3.12.0: Stand By (L_StandTick), or 3.11.0: MVP hunts and Fly Wing hops while they roam (L_HuntTick).
	if (CL_STANDBY)
		callsub L_StandTick, .@sec;
	else if (getarraysize(@cl_rm$) || @cl_hu_g || @cl_hu_lead)
		callsub L_HuntTick, .@sec;
	if (@cl_hu_k)
		callsub L_HuntMark;	// 3.12.7: a find you put off stays on the minimap
	if (@cl_hu_solo)
		callsub L_SoloTick, .@sec;	// 3.13.0: Stand by, I'll solo

	// Looking around: while you stand still and nothing is fighting, every
	// 12-30 s one companion strolls a few steps to another spot near you.
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (.@ox != @cl_ox || .@oy != @cl_oy) {
		@cl_ox = .@ox;
		@cl_oy = .@oy;
		@cl_still = .@sec;
		// 3.0.0: the engine stands a sitter up at your every step. The idle_sit rules
		// wait for this marker (Reuse_Limit_H: it only blocks one cash item companions
		// never use) to run out, so a few steps get them up once, and they sit again
		// about 8 s after you stop, not after every step.
		if (.idlesit && !@cl_incombat) {
			for (.@si = 0; .@si < @cl_n; .@si++) {
				if (@cl_hp[.@si] > 0 && !callsub(L_Roams, .@si))
					sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_H, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@si];
			}
		}
		// 3.9.1: you walked on: a scout on watch lets go and follows (the lookout Hold).
		for (.@si = 0; .@si < @cl_n; .@si++) {
			if (@cl_ar[.@si] == 3)
				// sc_end can't clear Reuse_Limit_F (NoClearbuff + NoForcedEnd): a 1 ms
			// restart replaces its timer instead.
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_F, 1, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@si];
		}
	}
	// Not one that already went to sit by its favourite; the scouts (Wanderers:
	// archers, gunslingers) twice as often, they keep checking the perimeter,
	// unless they walk their patrol laps (scout_patrol) instead.
	if (.look && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_still >= 4 && .@sec >= @cl_wnext && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		@cl_wnext = .@sec + 12 + rand(19);
		.@w = 0;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) == @cl_still || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			if (.patrol && .idlesit && @cl_ar[.@i] == 3)
				continue;	// on its lookout round instead (needs idle_sit, 3.9.1)
			.@wc[.@w] = .@i;
			.@w++;
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3) {
				.@wc[.@w] = .@i;
				.@w++;
			}
		}
		if (.@w > 0) {
			.@a = .@wc[0];
			if (.@w > 1)
				.@a = .@wc[rand(.@w)];
			callsub L_Wander, .@a, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
		}
	}

	// Scout patrol (setting scout_patrol): while you stand still and all is
	// quiet, each scout does a lookout round (L_Patrol), then sits with the rest.
	// 3.9.1: was a lap of short legs every 3 s, stopped at 165 s. It needs idle_sit:
	// that file's Hold keeps a scout at its lookout spot.
	if (.patrol && .idlesit && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_still >= 4 && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] != 3 || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) == @cl_still || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			callsub L_Patrol, .@i, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
		}
	}

	// Restless sitting (setting restless_sitting): once they've sat a while,
	// every 1.5-4 minutes one gets up: it turns to look at you, at whoever
	// spoke, or about, or it moves to a new spot, then the idle_sit rule sits
	// it down again (it waits out the same marker as the strolls).
	if (.restless && .idlesit && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		if (@cl_rstill != @cl_still) {
			@cl_rstill = @cl_still;
			@cl_rnext = @cl_still + 120 + rand(91);	// the first a minute or so after they sit
		}
		if (.@sec >= @cl_rnext) {
			@cl_rnext = .@sec + 90 + rand(151);
			callsub L_Restless, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
		}
	}

	// The last step of a walk that should end facing someone (L_FaceWalk).
	// Dropped if a fight started or you moved off meanwhile.
	while (getarraysize(@cl_fa) && .@now >= @cl_ft[0]) {
		if (!@cl_incombat && @cl_fs[0] == @cl_still && unitexists(@cl_fa[0])) {
			unitstopwalk @cl_fa[0], USW_FORCE_STOP;
			unitwalk @cl_fa[0], @cl_fx[0], @cl_fy[0];
		}
		deletearray @cl_fa[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_fx[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_fy[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_ft[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_fs[0], 1;
	}

	// Sitting with their favourite (setting sit_with_favourite): a little before
	// the idle_sit rules sit them (after a minute of you standing still, three
	// for the scouts), each walks over to the one it is most comfortable with,
	// one every 1.5 s. Bonds: L_Fav.
	if (.sitfav && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		.@still = .@sec - @cl_still;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) == @cl_still || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3 && .patrol)
				continue;	// 3.9.1: a scout on lookout duty keeps its rounds (L_Patrol)
			.@thr = 45;
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3)
				.@thr = 165;
			if (.@still < .@thr)
				continue;
			setd "@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i], @cl_still;
			callsub L_SitBy, .@i, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
			break;
		}
	}
	// The emote once one has got there: one a tick.
	if (getarraysize(@cl_pea) && .@sec >= @cl_pet[0]) {
		if (.emotes && unitexists(@cl_pea[0]))
			emotion @cl_pee[0], @cl_pea[0];
		deletearray @cl_pea[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_pee[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_pet[0], 1;
	}
	end;

// Roaming orders from party chat (client/index.js): "@clroam <request> on|off
// <what you said>". Runs the engine's "@companion roam" for the companions
// named in the line (all of them when none is), and one whose state it changed
// answers in its own words. Answers OK|<changed> or ERR|<why>.
// 3.10.0, the Roster tab: "@clroster <request> <action> ...", the app's Companions
// window in ours. Every change is the engine's own @companion command, so its rules
// (party size, the saved list) stay the engine's; we add the hiring rules on top.
//   list                   M|your tier|level|zeny|fee|first is free|roaming on|hire mode,
//                          C|name|class|sex|lv|job lv|in party|favourite|role|roaming|
//                          homunculus (-1 none)|rebirth (-1 no, 0 not yet, 1 ready)|class name,
//                          F|job|lowest level (learned on a hire)
//   hire <job> <m|f|r>     a new companion at your level (tier 1, 2, or transcendent
//                          once you are), for the fee; the first one is free
//   summon|bench|remove <name>, fav on|off <name>, hom on|off <name>,
//   roam on|off <name>, reb novice|trans <name>, stats <name> (3.10.2)
// Answers OK|text or ERR|text. The role is a party-chat line (client/index.js).
OnCmdRoster:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	.@me = getcharid(0);
	.@tier$ = callsub(L_RsTier);
	.@mode = getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_hire");
	if (.@act$ == "list") {
		query_sql "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@me, .@cnt;
		.@free = (!modstore_get("char", "rs.hired", 0) && !.@cnt);
		.@out$ = "M|" + .@tier$ + "|" + BaseLevel + "|" + Zeny + "|" + callsub(L_RsFee, .@tier$) + "|" + .@free
			+ "|" + (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") > 0) + "|" + .@mode;
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@n = query_sql("SELECT name, job_id, sex, base_level, job_level, favorite, duty, roam, IFNULL(hom_enabled, 1) FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = "
			+ .@me + " ORDER BY favorite DESC, id", .@qn$, .@qj, .@qs, .@ql, .@qjl, .@qf, .@qd, .@qr, .@qh);
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@n; .@i++) {
			.@cls = .@qj[.@i];
			.@sx = .@qs[.@i];
			.@lv = .@ql[.@i];
			.@jl = .@qjl[.@i];
			.@ix = inarray(@clb_nm$, .@qn$[.@i]);
			.@on = (.@ix >= 0);
			if (.@on) {
				.@c = @clb_cid[.@ix];
				.@cls = readparam(Class, .@c);
				.@sx = readparam(Sex, .@c);
				.@lv = readparam(BaseLevel, .@c);
				.@jl = readparam(JobLevel, .@c);
			}
			.@hom = -1;
			if (.@cls == Job_Alchemist || .@cls == Job_Creator || .@cls == Job_Baby_Alchemist)
				.@hom = (.@qh[.@i] != 0);
			.@reb = -1;
			if (.@cls >= Job_Knight && .@cls <= Job_Crusader2) {	// a 2nd job (mounts too)
				.@reb = 0;
				if (.@lv >= 99 && .@jl >= 50)
					.@reb = 1;
			}
			.@out$ += "~C|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@qn$[.@i]) + "|" + .@cls + "|" + .@sx + "|" + .@lv + "|" + .@jl + "|" + .@on
				+ "|" + .@qf[.@i] + "|" + .@qd[.@i] + "|" + .@qr[.@i] + "|" + .@hom + "|" + .@reb + "|" + jobname(.@cls);
		}
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize($cpo_hfj$); .@i++)
			.@out$ += "~F|" + $cpo_hfj$[.@i] + "|" + $cpo_hfv[.@i];
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "hire") {
		.@job$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[2];
		.@sx$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[3];
		.@ok$ = "";
		if (.@tier$ == "1")
			.@ok$ = .rs_t1$;
		else if (.@tier$ == "2")
			.@ok$ = .rs_t2$;
		else if (.@tier$ == "t")
			.@ok$ = .rs_t2$ + .rs_tt$;
		if (.@ok$ == "" || .@job$ == "" || !compare(.@ok$, " " + .@job$ + " ")) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|You can only hire someone of your own class tier.";
			end;
		}
		if (.@mode == 2) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Your hiring rules say companions are hired from a Companion Recruiter in town (app Settings, Population).";
			end;
		}
		.@k = inarray($cpo_hfj$, .@job$);
		if (.@k >= 0) {
			if ($cpo_hfv[.@k] > BaseLevel) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|A " + .@job$ + " can't join below Lv " + $cpo_hfv[.@k] + ". Come back then!";
				end;
			}
		}
		query_sql "SELECT COUNT(*), IFNULL(MAX(id), 0) FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@me, .@cnt, .@last;
		.@fee = 0;
		if (!.@mode) {	// with the app's own fee on (mode 1), the engine charges it
			if (modstore_get("char", "rs.hired", 0) || .@cnt)
				.@fee = callsub(L_RsFee, .@tier$) * BaseLevel;
		}
		if (Zeny < .@fee) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Hiring costs " + .@fee + " z. You have " + Zeny + " z.";
			end;
		}
		// Bard and Clown are men, Dancer and Gypsy women: Random picks one of the pair.
		if (.@sx$ == "r") {
			if (.@job$ == "Bard" || .@job$ == "Dancer")
				.@job$ = (rand(2) ? "Bard" : "Dancer");
			else if (.@job$ == "Clown" || .@job$ == "Gypsy")
				.@job$ = (rand(2) ? "Clown" : "Gypsy");
		}
		if (.@job$ == "Bard" || .@job$ == "Clown")
			.@sx$ = "m";
		else if (.@job$ == "Dancer" || .@job$ == "Gypsy")
			.@sx$ = "f";
		.@cmd$ = "@companion draft " + .@job$;
		if (.@sx$ == "m" || .@sx$ == "f")
			.@cmd$ += " " + .@sx$;
		atcommand .@cmd$;
		sleep2 800;
		.@nid = 0;
		query_sql "SELECT id, name, base_level FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@me + " AND id > " + .@last
			+ " ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1", .@nid, .@nnm$, .@nlv;
		if (!.@nid) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Nobody answered your call. Is your party full?";
			end;
		}
		// Never above you: a class that can't come that low goes again, and is
		// remembered (for everyone) so the Hire page locks it until you get there.
		if (.@nlv > BaseLevel + 2) {
			atcommand "@companion remove " + .@nnm$;
			.@k = inarray($cpo_hfj$, .@job$);
			if (.@k < 0)
				.@k = getarraysize($cpo_hfj$);
			$cpo_hfj$[.@k] = .@job$;
			$cpo_hfv[.@k] = .@nlv;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|A " + .@job$ + " can't join below Lv " + .@nlv + ". Come back then!";
			end;
		}
		if (.@fee > 0)
			Zeny -= .@fee;
		modstore_set "char", "rs.hired", 1;
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nnm$);
		if (.@ix >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "rshire", "", ET_SMILE;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@nnm$) + "|" + .@nlv + "|" + .@fee;
		end;
	}
	// The rest name a companion of yours: "<action> <name>", or "<action> <on|off|...> <name>".
	.@arg$ = "";
	.@from = 2;
	if (.@act$ == "fav" || .@act$ == "hom" || .@act$ == "roam" || .@act$ == "reb") {
		.@arg$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[2];
		.@from = 3;
	}
	.@nm$ = "";
	for (.@k = .@from; .@k < .@atcmd_numparameters; .@k++) {
		if (.@nm$ != "")
			.@nm$ += " ";
		.@nm$ += .@atcmd_parameters$[.@k];
	}
	.@has = 0;
	if (.@nm$ != "")
		query_sql "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@me + " AND name = '" + escape_sql(.@nm$) + "'", .@has;
	if (!.@has) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|No companion named " + .@nm$ + ".";
		end;
	}
	// 3.10.2: the Stats fold. In your party: their points, and the fight numbers with
	// gear and buffs counted (readparam on the companion; the gear part of each point
	// can't be read). Resting: the points saved with them. Answers S|in party|str|agi|
	// vit|int|dex|luk, then for one in your party |hit|flee|flee2|def|def2|mdef|mdef2|crit|aspd.
	if (.@act$ == "stats") {
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@ix = inarray(@clb_nm$, .@nm$);
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			.@c = @clb_cid[.@ix];
			setarray .@p, bStr, bAgi, bVit, bInt, bDex, bLuk, bHit, bFlee, bFlee2, bDef, bDef2, bMdef, bMdef2, bCritical, bAspd;
			.@out$ = "S|1";
			for (.@k = 0; .@k < getarraysize(.@p); .@k++)
				.@out$ += "|" + readparam(.@p[.@k], .@c);
		} else {
			query_sql "SELECT str_, agi_, vit_, intl_, dex_, luk_ FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@me
				+ " AND name = '" + escape_sql(.@nm$) + "' LIMIT 1", .@s1, .@s2, .@s3, .@s4, .@s5, .@s6;
			.@out$ = "S|0|" + .@s1 + "|" + .@s2 + "|" + .@s3 + "|" + .@s4 + "|" + .@s5 + "|" + .@s6;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "summon") {
		atcommand "@companion summon " + .@nm$;
		sleep2 1500;
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nm$);
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@ix] > 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "rshi", "", ET_SMILE;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@nm$ + " joins the party.";
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "bench") {
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nm$);
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@ix] > 0) {
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "rsbye", "", ET_SMILE;
				sleep2 1500;
			}
		}
		atcommand "@companion dismiss " + .@nm$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@nm$ + " is resting now.";
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "remove") {
		atcommand "@companion remove " + .@nm$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|You parted ways with " + .@nm$ + ".";
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "fav") {
		if (.@arg$ == "on")
			atcommand "@companion favorite " + .@nm$;
		else
			atcommand "@companion unfavorite " + .@nm$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "hom") {
		if (.@arg$ != "on")
			.@arg$ = "off";
		atcommand "@companion homunculus " + .@nm$ + " " + .@arg$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@nm$ + "'s homunculus is " + .@arg$ + ".";
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "roam") {
		if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|roaming off";
			end;
		}
		if (.@arg$ != "on")
			.@arg$ = "off";
		atcommand "@companion roam " + .@nm$ + " " + .@arg$;
		callsub L_RoamRead;
		callsub L_RoamRest;
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nm$);
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@ix] > 0) {
				if (.@arg$ == "on")
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "roam", "", ET_GO;
				else
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "back", "", ET_SMILE;
			}
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@arg$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "reb") {
		if (.@arg$ != "novice" && .@arg$ != "trans") {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Rebirth as what?";
			end;
		}
		atcommand "@companion rebirth " + .@nm$ + " " + .@arg$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@nm$ + " starts a new life.";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|unknown";
	end;

// 3.10.0: your class tier for hiring: "1" (Novice and 1st jobs, High ones too), "2",
// "t" (transcendent 2nd), "3" or "4" (not offered by the Roster's Hire page).
L_RsTier:
	.@ea = eaclass(Class);
	if (.@ea & EAJL_FOURTH)
		return "4";
	if (.@ea & EAJL_THIRD)
		return "3";
	if (.@ea & EAJL_2) {
		if (.@ea & EAJL_UPPER)
			return "t";
		return "2";
	}
	return "1";

// The hiring fee per your base level for a tier.
L_RsFee:
	if (getarg(0) == "1")
		return .rs_fee1;
	if (getarg(0) == "2")
		return .rs_fee2;
	return .rs_feet;

OnCmdRoam:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@on = (.@atcmd_parameters$[1] == "on");
	// The Orders tab's buttons say "tab" and work with chat orders off too (3.7.0).
	if (!.orders && .@atcmd_parameters$[2] != "tab") {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|chat orders off";
		end;
	}
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") <= 0) {
		dispbottom "Companion roaming is off. Turn it on in the app's Settings (Population).";
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|roaming off";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_StandOff;
	// 3.10.1: "come back" (or the window's Recall) during a shopping trip calls them
	// all back by Fly Wing (L_ShopEnd).
	if (!.@on && cl_shop$ != "") {
		callsub L_ShopEnd, 1;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|0";
		end;
	}
	.@said$ = " ";
	for (.@k = 2; .@k < .@atcmd_numparameters; .@k++)
		.@said$ += .@atcmd_parameters$[.@k] + " ";
	.@c = callsub(L_RoamOrder, .@on, .@said$, (.@on ? "roam" : "back"), 0);
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@c;
	end;

// 3.11.0 (was OnCmdRoam's own): roaming on (getarg(0) 1) or off for the companions named
// in getarg(1) (the line, padded with spaces), else all of them. One of those it changed
// answers with a getarg(2) line; getarg(3) 1: one of the named answers even when none
// changed. Returns how many it changed.
L_RoamOrder:
	.@on = getarg(0);
	// The line without punctuation, so " name " finds a whole name.
	.@said$ = getarg(1);
	setarray .@pun$, ",", ".", "!", "?", ":", ";";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < getarraysize(.@pun$); .@k++)
		.@said$ = replacestr(.@said$, .@pun$[.@k], " ");
	callsub L_BagParty;
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	.@n = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @clb_n; .@i++) {
		if (compare(.@said$, " " + @clb_nm$[.@i] + " ")) {
			.@who$[.@n] = @clb_nm$[.@i];
			.@n++;
		}
	}
	if (!.@n) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @clb_n; .@i++)
			.@who$[.@i] = @clb_nm$[.@i];
		.@n = @clb_n;
	}
	.@arg$ = " off";
	if (.@on)
		.@arg$ = " on";
	.@c = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		if ((inarray(@cl_rm$, .@who$[.@k]) >= 0) == .@on)
			continue;	// already roaming / already following
		atcommand "@companion roam " + .@who$[.@k] + .@arg$;
		.@chg$[.@c] = .@who$[.@k];
		.@c++;
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	// One of them answers, if it's here and standing.
	.@cn = 0;
	if (.@c) {
		copyarray .@cand$[0], .@chg$[0], .@c;
		.@cn = .@c;
	} else if (getarg(3)) {
		copyarray .@cand$[0], .@who$[0], .@n;
		.@cn = .@n;
	}
	.@s = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@cn; .@k++) {
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@cand$[.@k]);
		if (.@ix < 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_hp[.@ix] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@sp[.@s] = .@ix;
		.@s++;
	}
	if (.@s) {
		.@ix = .@sp[0];
		if (.@s > 1)
			.@ix = .@sp[rand(.@s)];
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, getarg(2), "", (.@on ? ET_GO : ET_SMILE);
	}
	return .@c;

// 3.11.0: what roaming is for, "@clhunt <request> normal|mvp task" from the Orders tab's Task
// card (3.12.0: one click sets it and sends them; char var CL_HUNT: 0 everything, 1 the MVP
// only; L_HuntTick). From party chat ("hunt the MVP", "hunt everything") the rest of the line
// follows, and the companions named in it (else all) go.
OnCmdHunt:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@a$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	// 3.13.0: the find's answers (client/index.js): "engage" (Engage together) and "solo"
	// (Stand by, I'll solo). You Fly Wing beside the one who found it, everyone flies to you,
	// then L_HuntGo. No wing, or wings don't work here: walk to the red mark, they keep
	// waiting, and the answer holds for when you get there (L_HuntGather).
	if (.@a$ == "engage" || .@a$ == "solo") {
		if (!@cl_hu_g) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|over";
			end;
		}
		@cl_hu_mode = (.@a$ == "engage" ? 1 : 2);
		if (CL_DEBUG)
			callsub L_Dbg, "answer: " + (@cl_hu_mode == 1 ? "Engage together" : "Stand by, I'll solo") + " (" + (gettimetick(2) - @cl_hu_t) + " s after the find)";
		.@fi = inarray(@cl_aid[0], @cl_hu_f);
		getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
		if (.@fi < 0 || getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_hu_f) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@m$ || Hp <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|away";
			end;
		}
		.@dx = .@cx - .@x;
		.@dy = .@cy - .@y;
		.@why$ = "";
		if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) > 3) {
			if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOTELEPORT))
				.@why$ = "Fly Wings don't work on this map";
			else if (countitem(601) < 1)
				.@why$ = "You have no Fly Wing";
		}
		if (.@why$ != "") {
			if (CL_DEBUG)
				callsub L_Dbg, "answer: you walk (" + .@why$ + ")";
			dispbottom "[Companions] " + .@why$ + ": walk to the red mark. They wait, and "
				+ (@cl_hu_mode == 1 ? "everyone engages" : "they stand by for your solo") + " when you get there.", 0xFFCC66;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|walk";
			end;
		}
		.@tx = .@x;
		.@ty = .@y;
		if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) > 3) {
			// A free cell beside them, else their own (as @clgoto).
			.@tx = .@cx;
			.@ty = .@cy;
			for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8; .@k++) {
				.@ax = .@cx + rand(-2, 2);
				.@ay = .@cy + rand(-2, 2);
				if ((.@ax != .@cx || .@ay != .@cy) && checkcell(.@m$, .@ax, .@ay, cell_chkpass)) {
					.@tx = .@ax;
					.@ty = .@ay;
					break;
				}
			}
			delitem 601, 1;
			specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION;
			warp .@m$, .@tx, .@ty;
			specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION;
		}
		callsub L_HuntGo, .@tx, .@ty;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@a$;
		end;
	}
	// 3.13.0: "help me" in party chat during a solo: they all join in.
	if (.@a$ == "help") {
		if (!@cl_hu_solo) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|no solo";
			end;
		}
		if (CL_DEBUG)
			callsub L_Dbg, "solo over: you called for help";
		callsub L_SoloEnd;
		callsub L_HuntCharge;
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "mvphelp", @cl_hu_m$, ET_GO;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|help";
		end;
	}
	// 3.12.7: "Not now" on a find (client/index.js): all back to you and following, and the
	// mark stays on your minimap (L_HuntMark). Too late, the wait already over: nothing to do.
	// 3.13.0: the button is called Retreat now; it does the same.
	if (.@a$ == "skip") {
		if (@cl_hu_g) {
			if (CL_DEBUG)
				callsub L_Dbg, "answer: Retreat (" + (gettimetick(2) - @cl_hu_t) + " s after the find)";
			.@g = @cl_hu_g;
			callsub L_HuntEnd;
			callsub L_HuntRecall;
			@cl_hu_k = .@g;
			@cl_hu_km$ = strcharinfo(3);
			@cl_hu_kx = 0;
			@cl_hu_ky = 0;
			callsub L_HuntMark;
			dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_hu_m$ + " stays marked red on your minimap.", 0xFFCC66;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|0";
		end;
	}
	if (.@a$ != "normal" && .@a$ != "mvp") {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|normal or mvp";
		end;
	}
	.@task = (.@atcmd_parameters$[2] == "task");
	if (!.orders && !.@task) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|chat orders off";
		end;
	}
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") <= 0) {
		dispbottom "Companion roaming is off. Turn it on in the app's Settings (Population).";
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|roaming off";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_StandOff;
	CL_HUNT = (.@a$ == "mvp");
	@cl_hu_since = 0;
	.@said$ = " ";
	for (.@k = 2 + .@task; .@k < .@atcmd_numparameters; .@k++)
		.@said$ += .@atcmd_parameters$[.@k] + " ";
	.@c = callsub(L_RoamOrder, 1, .@said$, (CL_HUNT ? "huntmvp" : "roam"), CL_HUNT);
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@c;
	end;

// 3.13.2: the Debug recorder (troubleshooting only; nothing leaves your PC). CL_DEBUG (kept
// through a relog): 0 off, 1 on, 2 on + each new line in your chat. It records decisions,
// not ticks: the last 50 in @cl_dbg$ (a repeat counts up "(xN)"), each new one also in the
// map log as "[CPO debug]". Today it covers MVP finds, the answers and solos (L_Dbg calls).
OnCmdDebug:
	.@a$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	if (.@a$ == "on" || .@a$ == "live") {
		CL_DEBUG = (.@a$ == "live" ? 2 : 1);
		dispbottom "[CPO debug] Recorder on" + (CL_DEBUG == 2 ? ", new lines show here as they happen" : "") + ". @cldebug show to read it, @cldebug off to stop.", 0x99CCFF;
		callsub L_Dbg, "recorder on (" + .@a$ + ") on " + strcharinfo(3);
		end;
	}
	if (.@a$ == "off") {
		if (CL_DEBUG)
			callsub L_Dbg, "recorder off";
		CL_DEBUG = 0;
		dispbottom "[CPO debug] Recorder off. What it caught stays until you log out (@cldebug show).", 0x99CCFF;
		end;
	}
	if (.@a$ == "clear") {
		deletearray @cl_dbg$;
		@cl_dbg_last$ = "";
		@cl_dbg_x = 0;
		dispbottom "[CPO debug] Cleared.", 0x99CCFF;
		end;
	}
	if (.@a$ == "show") {
		.@n = getarraysize(@cl_dbg$);
		.@k = 15;
		if (.@atcmd_numparameters > 1)
			.@k = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[1]);
		if (.@k < 1 || .@k > 50)
			.@k = 50;
		if (!.@n) {
			dispbottom "[CPO debug] Nothing recorded yet" + (CL_DEBUG ? "." : ": the recorder is off (@cldebug on).") , 0x99CCFF;
			end;
		}
		.@s = (.@n > .@k ? .@n - .@k : 0);
		dispbottom "[CPO debug] Last " + (.@n - .@s) + " of " + .@n + ":", 0x99CCFF;
		for (.@i = .@s; .@i < .@n; .@i++)
			dispbottom "  " + @cl_dbg$[.@i], 0x99CCFF;
		end;
	}
	.@st$ = "off";
	if (CL_DEBUG == 2)
		.@st$ = "on (live)";
	else if (CL_DEBUG)
		.@st$ = "on";
	dispbottom "[CPO debug] Recorder is " + .@st$ + ", " + getarraysize(@cl_dbg$) + " lines.", 0x99CCFF;
	dispbottom "  @cldebug on | live (also shows here) | off | show [n] | clear", 0x99CCFF;
	end;

// 3.12.0: Stand By, "@clstand <request> task" from the Task card, or the rest of the line from
// party chat ("stand by", "wait here"; the companions named in it, else all). Only a roamer may
// stray from you (a follower gets warped back), so they roam, and Reuse_Limit_B (ct_hunt_look)
// holds them where they are: no walking and no new fights, only defending themselves.
OnCmdStand:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@task = (.@atcmd_parameters$[1] == "task");
	if (!.orders && !.@task) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|chat orders off";
		end;
	}
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") <= 0) {
		dispbottom "Companion roaming is off. Turn it on in the app's Settings (Population).";
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|roaming off";
		end;
	}
	if (cl_shop$ != "")
		callsub L_ShopEnd, 1;
	if (@cl_hu_g)
		callsub L_HuntEnd;
	@cl_hu_lead = 0;
	CL_STANDBY = 1;
	@cl_st_map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	.@said$ = " ";
	for (.@k = 1 + .@task; .@k < .@atcmd_numparameters; .@k++)
		.@said$ += .@atcmd_parameters$[.@k] + " ";
	.@c = callsub(L_RoamOrder, 1, .@said$, "standby", 1);
	callsub L_Gather;
	@cl_st_next = 0;
	callsub L_StandTick, gettimetick(2);
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@c;
	end;

// Each tick on Stand By: the hold renewed every 3 s (15 s long, so an NPC dialog, which skips
// the tick, doesn't free them). Nobody roaming any more (the camp, the app's window): it's
// over. You left the map: the engine brought them along, so they follow you again.
L_StandTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	if (!getarraysize(@cl_rm$)) {
		callsub L_StandOff;
		return;
	}
	.@m$ = strcharinfo(3);
	if (@cl_st_map$ == "")
		@cl_st_map$ = .@m$;	// a relog
	if (.@m$ != @cl_st_map$) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(@cl_rm$); .@i++)
			atcommand "@companion roam " + @cl_rm$[.@i] + " off";
		callsub L_RoamRead;
		callsub L_RoamRest;
		callsub L_StandOff;
		dispbottom "[Companions] You left the map, so they follow you again.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	if (.@sec < @cl_st_next)
		return;
	@cl_st_next = .@sec + 3;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0 && callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_B, 15000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	}
	return;

// Stand By over: the marks can't be ended, so a 1 ms restart clears them.
L_StandOff:
	if (!CL_STANDBY)
		return;
	CL_STANDBY = 0;
	@cl_st_map$ = "";
	callsub L_Gather;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_B, 1, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	return;

// 3.11.0: "@clgoto <request> <char id>": a right-click on a party frame (client/frames.js)
// (3.11.0-3.12.7 also the found-it bubble). You Fly Wing to that companion's side: one Fly
// Wing from your bag, on this map only, not where wings don't work.
OnCmdGoto:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[1]);
	callsub L_Gather;
	.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	.@here = 0;
	if (.@i >= 0) {
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) == 0 && .@cm$ == .@m$)
			.@here = 1;
	}
	if (!.@here) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] They aren't on this map with you.", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|away";
		end;
	}
	.@nm$ = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	.@dx = .@cx - .@x;
	.@dy = .@cy - .@y;
	if (Hp <= 0) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|dead";
		end;
	}
	if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) <= 3) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + .@nm$ + " is right beside you.", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|near";
		end;
	}
	if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOTELEPORT)) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] Fly Wings don't work on this map.", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|noteleport";
		end;
	}
	if (countitem(601) < 1) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] You need a Fly Wing to fly to " + .@nm$ + ".", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|wing";
		end;
	}
	// A free cell beside them, else their own.
	.@tx = .@cx;
	.@ty = .@cy;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8; .@k++) {
		.@ax = .@cx + rand(-2, 2);
		.@ay = .@cy + rand(-2, 2);
		if ((.@ax != .@cx || .@ay != .@cy) && checkcell(.@m$, .@ax, .@ay, cell_chkpass)) {
			.@tx = .@ax;
			.@ty = .@ay;
			break;
		}
	}
	delitem 601, 1;
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@nm$;
	specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION;
	warp .@m$, .@tx, .@ty;
	specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION;
	end;

// Campfire nights (setting campfire, 3.0.0): "@clcamp <request> on|off|yes" from
// your party-chat lines (client/index.js): "make camp", "time to go", or yes when a
// companion offered to light one.
OnCmdCamp:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	if (!.camp) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|campfire off";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (.@act$ == "off") {
		if (@cl_camp)
			callsub L_CampEnd, 1;
	} else if (.@act$ == "on") {
		if (!@cl_camp)
			callsub L_CampStart, 0;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @cl_camp;
	end;

// Warp Portal (3.3.0): "@clwarp <request>" from your party-chat line "take us back"
// (client/index.js). In a field or dungeon: a portal to your save point, and this spot
// is remembered (CL_WPM$/CL_WPX/CL_WPY). Anywhere else (towns, indoors): a portal back
// to that spot. The caster walks over and casts; the portal itself opens in the tick
// (L_WarpOpen), which is also when the gem and SP are paid.
OnCmdWarp:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	if (!.orders && .@atcmd_parameters$[1] != "tab") {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|chat orders off";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (!@cl_n) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's nobody here to open a portal.", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|nobody";
		end;
	}
	if (@cl_wpat || @cl_wp$ != "") {
		dispbottom "[Companions] The portal is already open. Step in!", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|open";
		end;
	}
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	.@kind$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@m$);
	.@home = (.@kind$ == "field" || .@kind$ == "dungeon");
	// The caster: a living Acolyte-line companion who knows the skill, the one with the
	// most SP. (The engine grants a companion its whole job tree, so every Acolyte,
	// Priest and Monk has Warp Portal Lv 4.) Skill names aren't constants: 27 = AL_WARP.
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@ix = -1;
	.@lv = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_ACOLYTE)
			continue;
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (!attachrid(.@aid))
			continue;
		.@k = getskilllv(27);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@k < 1)
			continue;
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@ix])
				continue;
		}
		.@ix = .@i;
		.@lv = .@k;
	}
	if (.@ix < 0) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "warpnone", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|no caster";
		end;
	}
	if (.@home) {
		.@dm$ = getsavepoint(0);
		.@dx = getsavepoint(1);
		.@dy = getsavepoint(2);
	} else {
		// Lv 1 only reaches the save point; a memo point needs Lv 2.
		if (CL_WPM$ == "" || .@lv < 2) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpnowhere", "", ET_QUESTION;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|nowhere";
			end;
		}
		.@dm$ = CL_WPM$;
		.@dx = CL_WPX;
		.@dy = CL_WPY;
	}
	if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOWARP)) {
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpno", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|nowarp";
		end;
	}
	if (@cl_sp[.@ix] < 38 - 3 * .@lv) {
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpsp", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|sp";
		end;
	}
	if (.warp_gem) {
		if (callsub(L_CampFind, "717") == "") {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpgem", "", ET_SWEAT;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|gem";
			end;
		}
	}
	@cl_wpc = @cl_aid[.@ix];
	@cl_wpcn$ = @cl_nm$[.@ix];
	@cl_wplv = .@lv;
	@cl_wphome = .@home;
	@cl_wpfm$ = .@m$;
	@cl_wpfx = .@x;
	@cl_wpfy = .@y;
	@cl_wpdm$ = .@dm$;
	@cl_wpdx = .@dx;
	@cl_wpdy = .@dy;
	@cl_wpat = gettimetick(0) + 1500;	// a walk over and the 1 s cast
	stand @cl_nm$[.@ix];	// a sitter can't walk (campfire lesson)
	callsub L_GiftWalk, .@ix;
	if (.@home)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpgo", "", ET_GO;
	else
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpback", "", ET_GO;
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @cl_wpcn$;
	end;

// The portal opens: checked again (you may have left, the caster may have fallen or
// spent the SP), paid, and placed next to you (CpoWarpPortal, below; only you see it).
L_WarpOpen:
	@cl_wpat = 0;
	if (strcharinfo(3) != @cl_wpfm$)
		return;	// you left before it opened
	.@ix = inarray(@cl_aid[0], @cl_wpc);
	if (.@ix < 0) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] The portal fizzled: " + @cl_wpcn$ + " couldn't finish the cast.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_hp[.@ix] <= 0) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] The portal fizzled: " + @cl_wpcn$ + " couldn't finish the cast.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	.@cost = 38 - 3 * @cl_wplv;	// 35/32/29/26 SP at Lv 1-4
	if (@cl_sp[.@ix] < .@cost) {
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpsp", "", ET_SWEAT;
		return;
	}
	.@gem$ = "";
	if (.warp_gem) {
		.@gem$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "717");
		if (.@gem$ == "") {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpgem", "", ET_SWEAT;
			return;
		}
	}
	@cl_wp$ = duplicate_dynamic("CpoWarpPortal");
	if (@cl_wp$ == "") {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's no room for a portal here.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	// A floating source NPC is the invisible JT_FAKENPC, and so is its copy (never sent
	// to the client). 111 = the hidden NPC: the client knows it but draws no body, so
	// it carries the skill's own look, EF_PORTAL2 (what the client draws for a real
	// UNT_WARPPORTAL: a turning blue ring, looping, and light columns for 25 s). Both
	// are attached to the NPC and go with it when the tick unloads it. 3.3.1: was 45,
	// the map's ordinary portal.
	setnpcdisplay @cl_wp$, "Warp Portal", 111;
	specialeffect EF_PORTAL2, AREA, @cl_wp$;
	if (.@gem$ != "")
		callsub L_CampUse, .@gem$;
	heal 0, -.@cost, @cl_cid[.@ix];
	@cl_wpend = gettimetick(0) + @cl_wplv * 5000;	// 5/10/15/20 s, as the skill
	if (@cl_wphome) {
		CL_WPM$ = @cl_wpfm$;
		CL_WPX = @cl_wpfx;
		CL_WPY = @cl_wpfy;
		.@to$ = callsub(L_TownName, @cl_wpdm$);
		if (.@to$ == "")
			.@to$ = "your save point";
	} else
		.@to$ = "where you left off";
	.@paid$ = "";
	if (.@gem$ != "")
		.@paid$ = ", one Blue Gemstone";
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_wpcn$ + " opened a Warp Portal to " + .@to$ + " (" + .@cost + " SP" + .@paid$ + "). Step in within " + (@cl_wplv * 5) + " seconds.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

L_WarpClose:
	if (@cl_wp$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_wp$;
	@cl_wp$ = "";
	@cl_wpend = 0;
	return;

// The Orders tab (3.7.0, client/index.js): "@clorder <request> <action>".
//   state     OK|companions here|portal caster or ""|why no portal or ""|portal home (1)
//             or back (0)|appraiser or ""|why no appraisal or ""|unidentified items|
//             camp lit|roamers|roam alone|roaming on (app setting)|campfire on|
//             smith or ""|why no repair or ""|broken items (3.10.2)|
//             hunt (CL_HUNT)|Fly Wings in their bags|"<name> found <MVP>" or "" (3.11.0)|
//             on Stand By (3.12.0)|on a shopping trip (3.12.7)
//   appraise  the Merchant-line companion with the most SP identifies your items,
//             10 SP each (Item Appraisal); fewer if its SP runs out
//   repair    3.10.2: the Blacksmith/Whitesmith with the most SP fixes broken gear, yours
//             first, then the party's, 30 SP each (Weapon Repair's cost; no materials)
//   group / alone   the roam style, kept on your character (CL_ROAMALONE)
OnCmdOrder:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (.@act$ == "group" || .@act$ == "alone") {
		CL_ROAMALONE = (.@act$ == "alone");
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + CL_ROAMALONE;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "appraise") {
		.@ix = callsub(L_Appraiser);
		if (.@ix < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|No Merchant in the party.";
			end;
		}
		.@n = identifyall(false);
		if (.@n <= 0) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appnone", "", ET_QUESTION;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Nothing to appraise.";
			end;
		}
		.@can = @cl_sp[.@ix] / 10;
		if (.@can <= 0) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appnosp", "", ET_SWEAT;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @cl_nm$[.@ix] + " is out of SP.";
			end;
		}
		stand @cl_nm$[.@ix];	// a sitter can't walk
		callsub L_GiftWalk, .@ix;
		if (.@can >= .@n) {
			identifyall(true);
			.@done = .@n;
		} else {
			// Not enough SP for all: one by one, each given back identified (cards,
			// options, refine and binding kept, as the Bag tab's take does).
			getinventorylist;
			.@done = 0;
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < @inventorylist_count && .@done < .@can; .@j++) {
				if (@inventorylist_identify[.@j])
					continue;
				.@id = @inventorylist_id[.@j];
				.@amt = @inventorylist_amount[.@j];
				setarray .@oid, @inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id3[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id5[.@j];
				setarray .@oval, @inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value3[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value5[.@j];
				setarray .@opar, @inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@j];
				delitemidx @inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@amt;
				if (@inventorylist_bound[.@j])
					getitembound3 .@id, .@amt, 1, @inventorylist_refine[.@j], @inventorylist_attribute[.@j], @inventorylist_card1[.@j], @inventorylist_card2[.@j], @inventorylist_card3[.@j], @inventorylist_card4[.@j], @inventorylist_bound[.@j], .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
				else
					getitem3 .@id, .@amt, 1, @inventorylist_refine[.@j], @inventorylist_attribute[.@j], @inventorylist_card1[.@j], @inventorylist_card2[.@j], @inventorylist_card3[.@j], @inventorylist_card4[.@j], .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
				.@done++;
			}
		}
		heal 0, -(.@done * 10), @cl_cid[.@ix];
		.@left = .@n - .@done;
		if (.@left > 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appnosp", "", ET_SWEAT;
		else
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appraise", "", ET_OK;
		.@msg$ = @cl_nm$[.@ix] + " appraised " + .@done + " item" + (.@done == 1 ? "" : "s") + " (" + (.@done * 10) + " SP).";
		if (.@left > 0)
			.@msg$ += " " + .@left + " still unknown: ask again when their SP is back.";
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + .@msg$, 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "repair") {
		.@ix = callsub(L_Smith);
		if (.@ix < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|No Blacksmith in the party.";
			end;
		}
		.@n = callsub(L_Broken);
		if (.@n <= 0) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "repnone", "", ET_QUESTION;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Nothing is broken.";
			end;
		}
		.@can = @cl_sp[.@ix] / 30;
		if (.@can <= 0) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "repnosp", "", ET_SWEAT;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @cl_nm$[.@ix] + " is out of SP.";
			end;
		}
		stand @cl_nm$[.@ix];
		callsub L_GiftWalk, .@ix;
		// yours first, then each companion's; one at a time (repair 1 = the first broken one)
		.@done = 0;
		for (.@p = -1; .@p < @cl_n && .@done < .@can; .@p++) {
			.@who = getcharid(0);	// (not ?: — rAthena reads both sides, and @cl_cid[-1] ends the script)
			if (.@p >= 0)
				.@who = @cl_cid[.@p];
			while (.@done < .@can && getbrokenid(1, .@who)) {
				repair 1, .@who;
				.@done++;
			}
		}
		heal 0, -(.@done * 30), @cl_cid[.@ix];
		specialeffect2 EF_REPAIRWEAPON;
		.@left = .@n - .@done;
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, (.@left > 0 ? "repnosp" : "repair"), "", (.@left > 0 ? ET_SWEAT : ET_OK);
		.@msg$ = @cl_nm$[.@ix] + " repaired " + .@done + " item" + (.@done == 1 ? "" : "s") + " (" + (.@done * 30) + " SP).";
		if (.@left > 0)
			.@msg$ += " " + .@left + " still broken: ask again when their SP is back.";
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + .@msg$, 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// state
	.@alive = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			.@alive++;
	}
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	.@kind$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@m$);
	.@home = (.@kind$ == "field" || .@kind$ == "dungeon");
	// The portal: the same checks as OnCmdWarp, said in a few words.
	.@wc$ = "";
	.@why$ = "";
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@wix = -1;
	.@wlv = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_ACOLYTE)
			continue;
		if (!attachrid(@cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		.@k = getskilllv(27);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@k < 1)
			continue;
		if (.@wix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@wix])
				continue;
		}
		.@wix = .@i;
		.@wlv = .@k;
	}
	if (.@wix < 0)
		.@why$ = "No Acolyte in the party";
	else {
		.@wc$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@wix]);
		if (@cl_wpat || @cl_wp$ != "")
			.@why$ = "The portal is open";
		else if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOWARP))
			.@why$ = "No portals on this map";
		else if (!.@home && (CL_WPM$ == "" || .@wlv < 2))
			.@why$ = "Nowhere to go back to yet";
		else if (@cl_sp[.@wix] < 38 - 3 * .@wlv)
			.@why$ = @cl_nm$[.@wix] + " needs SP";
		else if (.warp_gem) {
			if (callsub(L_CampFind, "717") == "")
				.@why$ = "No Blue Gemstone";
		}
	}
	// The appraiser.
	.@ac$ = "";
	.@awhy$ = "";
	.@un = identifyall(false);
	.@aix = callsub(L_Appraiser);
	if (.@aix < 0)
		.@awhy$ = "No Merchant in the party";
	else {
		.@ac$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@aix]);
		if (.@un <= 0)
			.@awhy$ = "Nothing to appraise";
		else if (@cl_sp[.@aix] < 10)
			.@awhy$ = @cl_nm$[.@aix] + " needs SP";
	}
	// 3.10.2: the smith (Repair).
	.@rc$ = "";
	.@rwhy$ = "";
	.@br = callsub(L_Broken);
	.@rix = callsub(L_Smith);
	if (.@rix < 0)
		.@rwhy$ = "No Blacksmith in the party";
	else {
		.@rc$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@rix]);
		if (.@br <= 0)
			.@rwhy$ = "Nothing is broken";
		else if (@cl_sp[.@rix] < 30)
			.@rwhy$ = @cl_nm$[.@rix] + " needs SP";
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	// 3.11.0: the hunt, the Fly Wings in their bags, and who found what (while you're awaited).
	.@wings = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
		.@wings += countitem(601, @cl_aid[.@i]);
	.@found$ = "";
	if (@cl_hu_g) {
		.@fi = inarray(@cl_aid[0], @cl_hu_f);
		if (.@fi >= 0)
			.@found$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@fi] + " found " + @cl_hu_m$);
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@alive + "|" + .@wc$ + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@why$) + "|" + .@home + "|" + .@ac$ + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@awhy$)
		+ "|" + max(0, .@un) + "|" + @cl_camp + "|" + getarraysize(@cl_rm$) + "|" + CL_ROAMALONE
		+ "|" + (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") > 0) + "|" + .camp
		+ "|" + .@rc$ + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@rwhy$) + "|" + .@br
		+ "|" + CL_HUNT + "|" + .@wings + "|" + .@found$ + "|" + CL_STANDBY + "|" + (cl_shop$ != "");
	end;

// 3.10.2: the living Blacksmith or Whitesmith here with the most SP; -1 if none.
L_Smith:
	.@ix = -1;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@c = @cl_cls[.@i];
		if (.@c != Job_Blacksmith && .@c != Job_Whitesmith && .@c != Job_Baby_Blacksmith)
			continue;
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@ix])
				continue;
		}
		.@ix = .@i;
	}
	return .@ix;

// 3.10.2: how many broken items you and your companions here carry (getbrokenid counts
// from 1; up to 30 each).
L_Broken:
	.@n = 0;
	for (.@p = -1; .@p < @cl_n; .@p++) {
		.@who = getcharid(0);	// (not ?: — both sides are read)
		if (.@p >= 0)
			.@who = @cl_cid[.@p];
		for (.@k = 1; .@k <= 30 && getbrokenid(.@k, .@who); .@k++)
			.@n++;
	}
	return .@n;

// The living Merchant-line companion here who knows Item Appraisal (40), the one with
// the most SP; -1 if none.
L_Appraiser:
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@ix = -1;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_MERCHANT)
			continue;
		if (!attachrid(@cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		.@k = getskilllv(40);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@k < 1)
			continue;
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@ix])
				continue;
		}
		.@ix = .@i;
	}
	return .@ix;

// Maximize Power (3.4.0): the engine turns it on but never off, and it drains SP and stops
// SP regen while on. Off once no monster is within 9 cells of the smith for two ticks
// (about 3 s), or nobody in the party was hit for 20 s (a fight's @cl_combat_until is
// the last hit + 10; not in the first 20 s after it went on, before the first hit), or
// under 25% SP. party_roles' ct_maxpow plan bans the engine's recast until monsters
// attack again and SP is back to 40%. getarg(0) = now (s). @cl_mpon_<aid> = when seen on.
L_MaxPow:
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !getstatus(SC_MAXIMIZEPOWER, 0, @cl_cid[.@i])) {
			setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, 0;
			setd "@cl_mpon_" + .@aid, 0;
			continue;
		}
		.@on = getd("@cl_mpon_" + .@aid);
		if (!.@on) {
			.@on = getarg(0);
			setd "@cl_mpon_" + .@aid, .@on;
		}
		.@off = (@cl_msp[.@i] > 0 && @cl_sp[.@i] * 100 < @cl_msp[.@i] * 25);
		if (!.@off && getarg(0) - .@on >= 20 && getarg(0) > @cl_combat_until + 10)
			.@off = 1;
		if (!.@off) {
			if (getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0)
				continue;
			if (getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@m$, .@x - 9, .@y - 9, .@x + 9, .@y + 9) > 0) {
				setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, 0;
				continue;
			}
			.@q = getd("@cl_mpq_" + .@aid) + 1;
			setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, .@q;
			if (.@q < 2)
				continue;
		}
		sc_end SC_MAXIMIZEPOWER, .@aid;
		setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, 0;
		setd "@cl_mpon_" + .@aid, 0;
	}
	return;

// Light the fire: a bonfire next to you (a dynamic NPC: only you see it, and it
