// goes when you leave the map), roamers called back, everyone to a seat round it.
L_CampStart:
	if (!@cl_n) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's nobody here to share a fire with.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_incombat) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] Not while we're fighting!", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(@cl_rm$); .@i++)
		atcommand "@companion roam " + @cl_rm$[.@i] + " off";
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	if (@cl_fire$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_fire$;
	@cl_fire$ = duplicate_dynamic("CampFire");
	if (@cl_fire$ == "") {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's no room for a fire here.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	// A floating source NPC ("-") is always the invisible JT_FAKENPC in rAthena, and the
	// copy inherits its class, so give the copy its bonfire look here (mod id 19420 =
	// 4_BONFIRE, System/jobname.lub).
	setnpcdisplay @cl_fire$, "Campfire", 19420;
	getmapxy(.@fm$, .@fx, .@fy, BL_NPC, @cl_fire$);
	@cl_cfm$ = .@fm$;
	@cl_cfx = .@fx;
	@cl_cfy = .@fy;
	dispbottom "@@event dnfire " + .@fm$ + " " + .@fx + " " + .@fy;	// 3.12.5: Day & Night's glow, in the dark
	@cl_camp = 1;
	@cl_cout = 0;
	@cl_cstart = gettimetick(2);
	@cl_cmeal = 0;
	@cl_crest = 0;
	// 3.9.1: talk and little actions by the fire follow your pace (banter_minutes), twice as often.
	@cl_cnext = @cl_cstart + callsub(L_CampGap);
	@cl_cact = @cl_cstart + callsub(L_CampGap);
	// Seats round the fire for everyone; scouts stand watch at theirs for two minutes
	// before they sit (their marker waits, L_CampTick). Riders get off: a mounted
	// character can't be drawn sitting (Brizion on his Peco looked like he stood), and
	// they get back on in L_CampEnd (the engine remounts them on a map change anyway).
	@cl_crid$ = ";";
	callsub L_CampSeat;
	@cl_cseatst = @cl_still;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0 && checkriding(@cl_cid[.@i])) {
			setriding 0, @cl_cid[.@i];
			@cl_crid$ += @cl_aid[.@i] + ":" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ";";
		}
	}
	// getarg(0) 1: lit on their own while you stood still (3.7.0).
	.@auto = getarg(0, 0);
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
	if (.@a >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, (.@auto ? "campauto" : "camplit"), "", ET_DELIGHT;
	if (.@auto)
		dispbottom "[Companions] You stood still a while, so they made camp. Walk away, say \"time to go\" in party chat, or click the fire to put it out.", 0xFFCC66;
	else
		dispbottom "[Companions] Campfire lit. Say \"time to go\" in party chat (or click the fire) to put it out.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

// Seats in an even circle: the 16 cells two out from the fire, in clockwise order.
// You hold one place (the ring cell in your direction from the fire); the companions
// are spread at equal steps round the rest, so with five of them and you it's six
// evenly spaced places. A blocked or taken seat shifts to the next free cell. Each seat
// then goes to the nearest companion still without one (short walks), who walks up to
// end facing the fire. Seats handed out are kept in @cl_cseat$ (";x,y;x,y;").
L_CampSeat:
	.@fm$ = @cl_cfm$;
	.@fx = @cl_cfx;
	.@fy = @cl_cfy;
	setarray .@sx, 2, 2, 2, 1, 0, -1, -2, -2, -2, -2, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 2;
	setarray .@sy, 0, 1, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 1, 0, -1, -2, -2, -2, -2, -2, -1;
	setarray .@sl, 200, 224, 283, 224, 200, 224, 283, 224, 200, 224, 283, 224, 200, 224, 283, 224;	// length x100
	// Who sits: the living companions on this map.
	.@m = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@fm$)
			continue;
		.@ci[.@m] = .@i;
		.@cx_[.@m] = .@cx;
		.@cy_[.@m] = .@cy;
		.@m++;
	}
	if (!.@m)
		return;
	// Your place on the ring: the seat pointing most your way from the fire.
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	.@dx = .@ox - .@fx;
	.@dy = .@oy - .@fy;
	.@p = 0;
	if (.@dx != 0 || .@dy != 0) {
		.@bs = -99999;
		for (.@s = 0; .@s < 16; .@s++) {
			.@sc = (.@sx[.@s] * .@dx + .@sy[.@s] * .@dy) * 1000 / .@sl[.@s];
			if (.@sc > .@bs) {
				.@bs = .@sc;
				.@p = .@s;
			}
		}
	}
	@cl_cseat$ = ";" + (.@fx + .@sx[.@p]) + "," + (.@fy + .@sy[.@p]) + ";";	// yours
	setarray .@try, 0, 1, -1, 2, -2, 3, -3;
	for (.@k = 1; .@k <= .@m; .@k++) {
		.@t = (.@p + (.@k * 16 + (.@m + 1) / 2) / (.@m + 1)) % 16;
		// The seat itself, or the nearest free cell beside it on the ring.
		.@seat = -1;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < 7 && .@seat < 0; .@j++) {
			.@s = (.@t + .@try[.@j] + 16) % 16;
			.@gx = .@fx + .@sx[.@s];
			.@gy = .@fy + .@sy[.@s];
			if (.@gx == .@ox && .@gy == .@oy)
				continue;
			if (compare(@cl_cseat$, ";" + .@gx + "," + .@gy + ";"))
				continue;
			if (!checkcell(.@fm$, .@gx, .@gy, cell_chkpass))
				continue;
			.@seat = .@s;
		}
		if (.@seat < 0)
			continue;
		.@gx = .@fx + .@sx[.@seat];
		.@gy = .@fy + .@sy[.@seat];
		// The nearest companion still standing about.
		.@w = -1;
		.@bd = 999;
		for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@m; .@q++) {
			if (.@got[.@q])
				continue;
			.@d = max(.@cx_[.@q] - .@gx, .@gx - .@cx_[.@q], .@cy_[.@q] - .@gy, .@gy - .@cy_[.@q]);
			if (.@d < .@bd) {
				.@bd = .@d;
				.@w = .@q;
			}
		}
		if (.@w < 0)
			break;
		.@got[.@w] = 1;
		@cl_cseat$ += .@gx + "," + .@gy + ";";
		.@i = .@ci[.@w];
		// One already sitting (they sit after a minute of waiting) can't walk: up first,
		// or it stayed where it sat, on the fire itself sometimes.
		stand @cl_nm$[.@i];
		callsub L_FaceWalk, @cl_aid[.@i], .@fm$, .@cx_[.@w], .@cy_[.@w], .@gx, .@gy, .@fx, .@fy, 6000;
	}
	return;

// Put it out. getarg(0): 1 when you said so (someone answers), 0 when you walked
// off or left the map. Long enough by the fire: Well Rested for everyone.
L_CampEnd:
	if (@cl_fire$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_fire$;
	@cl_fire$ = "";
	dispbottom "@@event dnfire off";
	@cl_camp = 0;
	@cl_clast = gettimetick(2);
	// The riders back on their mounts.
	if (@cl_crid$ != "" && @cl_crid$ != ";") {
		explode(.@rd$, @cl_crid$, ";");
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@rd$); .@i++) {
			if (.@rd$[.@i] == "")
				continue;
			explode(.@ac$, .@rd$[.@i], ":");
			if (isloggedin(atoi(.@ac$[0]), atoi(.@ac$[1])))
				setriding 1, atoi(.@ac$[1]);
		}
	}
	@cl_crid$ = "";
	.@min = (gettimetick(2) - @cl_cstart) / 60;
	.@dur = 0;
	if (.@min >= 15)
		.@dur = 3600;
	else if (.@min >= 5)
		.@dur = 1800;
	if (.@dur) {
		// A camp of 5+ minutes is a night to remember (Bonds, 3.7.0).
		if (.mem) {
			for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
				modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".camps", 1);
		}
		bonus_script "bonus2 bExpAddRace,RC_ALL,10; bonus bHPrecovRate,30; bonus bSPrecovRate,30;", .@dur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, EFST_CASH_PLUSEXP;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
				bonus_script "bonus bHPrecovRate,30; bonus bSPrecovRate,30;", .@dur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, EFST_CASH_PLUSEXP, @cl_cid[.@i];
		}
		dispbottom "[Companions] Well Rested: +10% base EXP and faster HP and SP recovery for " + (.@dur / 60) + " minutes.", 0xFFCC66;
	}
	if (getarg(0)) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "campout", "", ET_OK;
	}
	return;

// Every tick while the fire burns.
L_CampTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	// You chose "Put out the fire" at the fire (CampFire marks it; the dialog is shut by
	// now, since this tick skips anyone in a dialog).
	if (@cl_cout) {
		@cl_cout = 0;
		callsub L_CampEnd, 1;
		return;
	}
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (.@om$ != @cl_cfm$ || max(.@ox - @cl_cfx, @cl_cfx - .@ox, .@oy - @cl_cfy, @cl_cfy - .@oy) > 12) {
		callsub L_CampEnd, 0;
		return;
	}
	// You moved about the camp: the game's follow pulled them into formation behind you,
	// off their seats. Two seconds after you settle, a fresh even ring from where you
	// stand now.
	if (@cl_cseatst != @cl_still && .@sec - @cl_still >= 2 && !@cl_incombat) {
		@cl_cseatst = @cl_still;
		callsub L_CampSeat;
	}
	// The marker that sits them round the fire (db/when/campfire, Reuse_Limit_G: it
	// only blocks one cash item companions never use), renewed every 4 s. The scouts
	// stand watch at their seats for two minutes, then sit too. (No effect
	// on the fire: EF_TORCH keeps burning once started, and repeating it stacked into
	// a huge glow; the bonfire sprite is the fire.)
	if (.@sec >= @cl_cmk) {
		@cl_cmk = .@sec + 4;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
				continue;
			// Scouts too: their two-minute watch, held at their seat, is in
			// db/when/campfire (ct_camp_watch).
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_G, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
		}
	}
	if (@cl_incombat)
		return;
	if (!@cl_cmeal && .@sec - @cl_cstart >= 60) {
		@cl_cmeal = 1;
		callsub L_CampCook;
		return;
	}
	.@min = (.@sec - @cl_cstart) / 60;
	if (@cl_crest < 1 && .@min >= 5) {
		@cl_crest = 1;
		dispbottom "[Companions] The fire's warmth sinks in. You'll leave Well Rested (30 minutes).", 0xFFCC66;
	} else if (@cl_crest < 2 && .@min >= 15) {
		@cl_crest = 2;
		dispbottom "[Companions] A proper rest. You'll leave Well Rested (1 hour).", 0xFFCC66;
	}
	// 3.9.1: and not in the quiet after a talk.
	if (!callsub(L_Calm))
		return;
	// 0 minutes between conversations: a quiet fire (the meal and Well Rested still come).
	if (.banter <= 0)
		return;
	if (.@sec >= @cl_cnext) {
		@cl_cnext = .@sec + callsub(L_CampGap);
		callsub L_CampTalk;
		return;
	}
	// No poking the fire right after a heartfelt talk (the mood, L_ConvEnd).
	if (.@sec >= @cl_cact && .@sec >= @cl_soft) {
		@cl_cact = .@sec + callsub(L_CampGap);
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, "campact", "", 0;
	}
	return;

// Seconds to the next talk or little action by the fire: twice as often as on the road
// (half of banter_minutes, 30% either way; a party of four or more 40% sooner). 3.9.1:
// before, the fire kept its own minute whatever the setting.
L_CampGap:
	.@g = max(1, .banter) * 30 * (70 + rand(61)) / 100;
	if (@cl_n >= 3)
		.@g = .@g * 60 / 100;
	return .@g;

// A talk by the fire: two of them, sometimes three, at night sometimes the night
// ones; one companion alone muses. Bonds grow twice as fast here.
L_CampTalk:
	if (@cl_n < 2) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, "campsolo", "", 0;
		return;
	}
	// 3.9.1: by the fire, the life stories come out (half the talks when one is due;
	// the deepest chapters only here).
	if (.mem && rand(100) < 50) {
		if (callsub(L_ArcTry, 1))
			return;
	}
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	.@b = -1;
	if (.@a >= 0)
		.@b = callsub(L_PickFriend, .@a);	// 3.9.1: every pair its turn
	if (.@a < 0 || .@b < 0)
		return;
	.@c = -1;
	if (@cl_n >= 3 && rand(100) < 30) {
		.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
		if (.@x >= 0 && .@x != .@b)
			.@c = .@x;
	}
	.@lh = gettime(DT_HOUR);
	if ($@dn_on)
		.@lh = $@dn_min / 60;	// the game's hour, with the day-night mod
	.@cat$ = "camp";
	if (.@c >= 0)
		.@cat$ = "camp3";
	else if ((.@lh >= 21 || .@lh < 5) && rand(100) < 40)
		.@cat$ = "campn";
	// 3.9.1: right after a heartfelt talk, the quiet night kind (L_ConvEnd).
	if (.@c < 0 && gettimetick(2) < @cl_soft)
		.@cat$ = "campn";
	.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, .@cat$);
	if (.@line$ == "")
		return;
	if (.@cat$ == "campn")
		@cl_cvsoft = 1;
	callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@b;
	callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@b;
	if (.@c >= 0) {
		callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@c;
		callsub L_Bump, .@b, .@c;
	}
	callsub L_QueueConv, .@a, .@b, .@c, .@line$, "", "", -1;
	return;

// The meal: the cook (a Merchant, else a Healer, else anyone) uses what's in the
// bags, theirs first, then yours. A buff for everyone, 30 minutes.
L_CampCook:
	// 3.8.5: nested, not &&: rAthena reads both sides, and @cl_ar[-1] ends the script.
	.@k = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
	if (.@k >= 0) {
		if (@cl_ar[.@k] != 7) {
			.@h = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
			if (.@h >= 0) {
				if (@cl_ar[.@h] == 4)
					.@k = .@h;
			}
		}
	}
	if (.@k < 0)
		return;
	.@meal = 0;
	.@m$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "517,12102");
	.@v$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "515,516,535,581,622");
	if (.@m$ != "" && .@v$ != "") {
		.@meal = 1;
		.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@m$) + " and " + callsub(L_CampUse, .@v$);
	} else {
		.@f$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "579,544");
		if (.@f$ != "") {
			.@meal = 2;
			.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@f$);
		} else {
			.@r$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "512,513,514,578,568");
			.@w$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "518,519,548");
			if (.@r$ != "" && .@w$ != "") {
				.@meal = 3;
				.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@r$) + " and " + callsub(L_CampUse, .@w$);
			} else {
				.@any$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "517,12102,515,516,535,581,622,579,544,512,513,514,578,568,518,519,548");
				if (.@any$ != "") {
					.@meal = 4;
					.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@any$);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if (!.@meal) {
		callsub L_QueueIdx, .@k, "campnofood", "", ET_SWEAT;
		return;
	}
	setarray .@mn$, "", "Hunter's Stew", "Grilled Fish", "Sweet Tart", "Simple Soup";
	setarray .@bs$, "", "bonus bBaseAtk,15; bonus bHit,10;", "bonus bMaxHPrate,8; bonus bHPrecovRate,20;",
		"bonus bMaxSPrate,8; bonus bSPrecovRate,30;", "bonus bHPrecovRate,20; bonus bSPrecovRate,20;";
	setarray .@fx$, "", "+15 ATK, +10 HIT", "+8% max HP, faster HP recovery", "+8% max SP, faster SP recovery", "faster HP and SP recovery";
	// The client's food icon closest to the meal (each icon has the game's own fixed
	// tooltip, so the gold line above says what the meal really does).
	setarray .@ic, 0, EFST_FOOD_STR, EFST_FOOD_VIT, EFST_FOOD_INT, EFST_FOOD_VIT;
	// Bonds (3.7.0): a Trusted (or closer) cook's meal lasts 15 minutes longer.
	.@mdur = 1800;
	if (.mem) {
		if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@k])) >= 3)
			.@mdur = 2700;
	}
	bonus_script .@bs$[.@meal], .@mdur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, .@ic[.@meal];
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			bonus_script .@bs$[.@meal], .@mdur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, .@ic[.@meal], @cl_cid[.@i];
	}
	.@t$ = replacestr(callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, "cook" + .@meal, 0), .@k, ""), "{i}", .@mn$[.@meal]);
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@k];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@k];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = ET_DELIGHT;
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@k] + " cooked " + .@mn$[.@meal] + " (" + .@used$ + "): " + .@fx$[.@meal] + " for everyone, " + (.@mdur / 60) + " minutes.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

// "<account id>:<item id>" of the first bag (companions', then yours) holding one of
// getarg(0) ("id,id,..."), or "".
L_CampFind:
	explode(.@ids$, getarg(0), ",");
	.@n = getarraysize(.@ids$);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			if (countitem(atoi(.@ids$[.@j]), @cl_aid[.@i]) > 0)
				return @cl_aid[.@i] + ":" + .@ids$[.@j];
		}
	}
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
		if (countitem(atoi(.@ids$[.@j])) > 0)
			return getcharid(3) + ":" + .@ids$[.@j];
	}
	return "";

// Takes the one L_CampFind found; returns its name.
L_CampUse:
	explode(.@p$, getarg(0), ":");
	.@id = atoi(.@p$[1]);
	if (atoi(.@p$[0]) == getcharid(3))
		delitem .@id, 1;
	else
		delitem .@id, 1, atoi(.@p$[0]);
	return getitemname(.@id);

// The Companions status window: "@clstat <request> list". One record per
// companion in the party (on this map or not), fields split by |, records by ~:
// name|job|base lv|job lv|hp|max hp|sp|max sp|state|map|days|kills|mvps|fell|
// places|met map|your level then|first mvp|your falls they saw|closest to
// ("you", a companion's name, or "" when it isn't here)
// state: ok, rest, dead, away (another map), off (not here at all).
OnCmdStat:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@out$ = "";
	.@pid = getcharid(1);
	// 3.9.1: "@clstat <request> hud": the party frames (client/frames.js), asked every
	// couple of seconds, so only what they draw and nothing from the store:
	// char id|name|class|sex|base lv|hp|max hp|sp|max sp|state|pet|on watch|ailment|weight %|shopping
	// (state as for list; pet: the kind when it's out; on watch: 1 on a lookout round;
	// 3.10.1 ailment: the worst one by the frames' name, weight in whole %, shopping 1 on a trip).
	if (.@atcmd_parameters$[1] == "hud") {
		setarray .@sc[0], SC_STONE, SC_STONEWAIT, SC_FREEZE, SC_STUN, SC_SLEEP, SC_CURSE, SC_SILENCE, SC_BLIND, SC_POISON, SC_DPOISON, SC_BLEEDING;
		setarray .@scn$[0], "stone", "stone", "frozen", "stun", "sleep", "curse", "silence", "blind", "poison", "poison", "bleed";
		.@scs = getarraysize(.@sc);
		if (.@pid) {
			.@me = getcharid(3);
			.@mymap$ = strcharinfo(3);
			getpartymember .@pid, 1;
			.@cnt = $@partymembercount;
			copyarray .@cid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@cnt;
			getpartymember .@pid, 2;
			copyarray .@aid[0], $@partymemberaid[0], .@cnt;
			getpartymember .@pid, 0;
			copyarray .@nm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@cnt;
			for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
				if (.@cid[.@i] < 95000000 || .@aid[.@i] == .@me)
					continue;
				.@c = .@cid[.@i];
				.@a = .@aid[.@i];
				.@hp = readparam(Hp, .@c);
				.@r$ = .@c + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@nm$[.@i]);
				if (.@hp < 0)
					.@r$ += "|0|0|0|0|0|0|0|off||0||0|0";
				else {
					.@cls = readparam(Class, .@c);
					.@st$ = "ok";
					if (.@hp == 0)
						.@st$ = "dead";
					else if (strcharinfo(3, .@c) != .@mymap$)
						.@st$ = "away";
					else if (getd("@cl_rst_" + .@a))
						.@st$ = "rest";
					.@pet$ = "";
					if (getd("@cl_pg_" + .@a)) {
						if (unitexists(getd("@cl_pg_" + .@a)))
							.@pet$ = .pet_nm$[callsub(L_PetOf, .@cls)];
					}
					.@watch = 0;
					if (.patrol && .idlesit && callsub(L_Arch, .@cls) == 3) {
						if (getd("@cl_ps_" + .@a) == @cl_still && getd("@cl_pdn_" + .@a) != @cl_still)
							.@watch = 1;
					}
					.@r$ += "|" + .@cls + "|" + readparam(Sex, .@c) + "|" + readparam(BaseLevel, .@c)
						+ "|" + .@hp + "|" + readparam(MaxHp, .@c) + "|" + readparam(Sp, .@c) + "|" + readparam(MaxSp, .@c)
						+ "|" + .@st$ + "|" + .@pet$ + "|" + .@watch;
					.@ail$ = "";
					if (.@hp > 0) {
						for (.@s = 0; .@s < .@scs; .@s++) {
							if (getstatus(.@sc[.@s], 0, .@c)) {
								.@ail$ = .@scn$[.@s];
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					.@mw = readparam(MaxWeight, .@c);
					.@wt = 0;   // (no ?: here: both sides run, and /0 would end the script)
					if (.@mw > 0)
						.@wt = readparam(Weight, .@c) * 100 / .@mw;
					.@shop = cl_shop$ != "" && compare("|" + cl_shop$, "|" + .@nm$[.@i] + "|");
					.@r$ += "|" + .@ail$ + "|" + .@wt + "|" + .@shop;
				}
				.@out$ += (.@out$ == "" ? "" : "~") + .@r$;
			}
		}
		.@len = getstrlen(.@out$);
		.@n = max(1, (.@len + 179) / 180);
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
			.@part$ = "";
			if (.@len > 0)
				.@part$ = substr(.@out$, .@k * 180, min(.@len, (.@k + 1) * 180) - 1);
			dispbottom "@@reply " + .@req$ + " " + (.@k + 1) + "/" + .@n + " " + .@part$;
		}
		end;
	}
	if (.@pid) {
		.@me = getcharid(3);
		.@mymap$ = strcharinfo(3);
		getpartymember .@pid, 1;
		.@cnt = $@partymembercount;
		copyarray .@cid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@cnt;
		getpartymember .@pid, 2;
		copyarray .@aid[0], $@partymemberaid[0], .@cnt;
		getpartymember .@pid, 0;
		copyarray .@nm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@cnt;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
			if (.@cid[.@i] < 95000000 || .@aid[.@i] == .@me)
				continue;
			.@c = .@cid[.@i];
			.@hp = readparam(Hp, .@c);
			.@r$ = callsub(L_Clean, .@nm$[.@i]);
			if (.@hp < 0) {
				.@r$ += "||0|0|0|0|0|0|off|";
			} else {
				.@map$ = strcharinfo(3, .@c);
				.@st$ = "ok";
				if (.@hp == 0)
					.@st$ = "dead";
				else if (.@map$ != .@mymap$)
					.@st$ = "away";
				else if (getd("@cl_rst_" + .@aid[.@i]))
					.@st$ = "rest";
				.@r$ += "|" + jobname(readparam(Class, .@c)) + "|" + readparam(BaseLevel, .@c) + "|" + readparam(JobLevel, .@c)
					+ "|" + .@hp + "|" + readparam(MaxHp, .@c) + "|" + readparam(Sp, .@c) + "|" + readparam(MaxSp, .@c)
					+ "|" + .@st$ + "|" + .@map$;
			}
			.@p$ = "m.c" + .@c;
			.@met = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", 0);
			.@days = 0;
			if (.@met > 0)
				.@days = (gettimetick(2) - .@met) / 86400;
			.@metmap$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".metmap", "");
			.@fmvp$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", "");
			.@r$ += "|" + .@days + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 0) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 0)
				+ "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".fell", 0) + "|" + modstore_count("char", .@p$ + ".maps")
				+ "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@metmap$) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".metlv", 0)
				+ "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@fmvp$) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".sawfall", 0);
			// Who it's closest to (L_Fav), when it's here with you.
			.@ix = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
			.@fav$ = "";
			if (.@ix >= 0) {	// (two ifs: && reads both sides)
				if (@cl_hp[.@ix] > 0) {
					.@fx = callsub(L_Fav, .@ix);
					.@fav$ = "you";
					if (.@fx >= 0)
						.@fav$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@fx]);
				}
			}
			.@r$ += "|" + .@fav$;
			// 3.7.0, Bonds: |points|tier|camps|gifts
			.@pt = callsub(L_BondPts, .@c);
			.@r$ += "|" + .@pt + "|" + callsub(L_BondTier, .@pt) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".camps", 0) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".gifts", 0);
			// 3.9.1: |char id (the Journal asks by it)|chapters told
			.@r$ += "|" + .@c + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arc", 0);
			if (.@out$ != "")
				.@out$ += "~";
			.@out$ += .@r$;
		}
	}
	.@len = getstrlen(.@out$);
	.@n = max(1, (.@len + 179) / 180);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@part$ = "";
		if (.@len > 0)
			.@part$ = substr(.@out$, .@k * 180, min(.@len, (.@k + 1) * 180) - 1);
		dispbottom "@@reply " + .@req$ + " " + (.@k + 1) + "/" + .@n + " " + .@part$;
	}
	end;

// The Companions window's Bag tab: "@clbag <request> <action>".
//   list                      P|purse|inventory on|zeny per level, then per
//                             companion C|char id|name|job|outfit colour|min|
//                             max|hair colour|min|max, and per stack in
//                             its bag I|char id|index|item id|amount|worn|
//                             refine|cards|options|bound|rental|name (cards
//                             "id,id", options "id:value,id:value")
//                             (records split by ~); worn is
//                             the equip slots (0 = in the bag), and only gear
//                             you gave is listed, never its own
//   take <char id> <index> <amount>   that stack (or part of it) to you
//   gearback <char id> <equip slots>  worn gear you gave, back to you
//   dye <char id> cloth|hair <colour> outfit or hair colour, kept for good
//   withdraw <zeny> / deposit <zeny>  the party purse
// 3.1.0: the C record ends with |weight|max weight (tenths, as the game counts),
//   and P with |in town (1/0); per restock entry R|char id|item id|keep|name.
//   rsset <char id> <item id> <keep>  a companion's restock list (0 = off the list);
//                                     in town it shops at once
//   lootlist                  P|purse|trip start|Overcharge %|finds on (1/0), then
//                             per item L|item id|amount|found at|sell price|card (1/0)|name
//   ltake <item id> <amount>  out of the party bag, to you
//   lsell <item id> <amount>  sold, the zeny into the purse
//   lsellall                  everything but cards sold, the zeny into the purse
// Actions answer OK|text or ERR|text. A companion's bag is its own (the
// engine's companion inventory); the purse is the party's (mod store, char
// scope, "purse"). The bag is read with getinventorylist <char id>, which
// leaves its @inventorylist_* on the companion; getvar reads them from there.
// 3.9.1: "@clmoment <request> <char id> <moment> <answer 1-3>": your answer to a
// "hey boss" moment (L_MomentTry). Kept (m.c<id>.mo<moment>), counted for the bond
// (.moments), and the companion replies. Your name: callname 1 yes / 2 keep the
// title. A story choice: its "later" line in about 30 minutes together.
OnCmdMoment:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[1]);
	.@k = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
	.@ch = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
	callsub L_Gather;
	.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
	if (.@c != @cl_mopc || .@k != @cl_mopid || .@ch < 1 || .@ch > 3 || .@i < 0 || .@k < 0 || .@k >= .mo_n) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	.@p$ = "m.c" + .@c;
	// 3.10.2: a pop quiz answer isn't kept as a moment (L_QuizAnswer).
	if (.@k == .qz_k) {
		callsub L_QuizAnswer, .@i, .@ch, .@sec;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	if (.mo_p[.@k] < 0 && .mo_kind$[.@k] != "check") {
		modstore_set "char", "mop" + .@k, .@ch;	// asked for the whole party
		modstore_set "char", "mopw" + .@k, .@c;	// by whom (the Journal)
	} else
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".mo" + .@k, .@ch;
	modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".moments", 1);
	if (.mo_rep[.@k]) {
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".moat" + .@k, .@sec;
		@cl_mochk = .@sec + 3600;
	}
	.@r$ = .mo_r1$[.@k];
	if (.@ch == 2)
		.@r$ = .mo_r2$[.@k];
	else if (.@ch == 3)
		.@r$ = .mo_r3$[.@k];
	.@em = ET_SMILE;
	.@kd$ = .mo_kind$[.@k];
	if (.@kd$ == "name") {
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".callname", (.@ch == 1 ? 1 : 2);
		if (.@ch == 1)
			.@em = ET_THROB;
	} else if (compare(.@kd$, "story")) {
		.@f$ = .mo_x1$[.@k];
		if (.@ch == 2)
			.@f$ = .mo_x2$[.@k];
		if (.@f$ != "") {
			modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".mofu", modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) + 30;
			modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".mofut", .@f$;
		}
	}
	if (.@r$ != "")
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, .@r$, .@em;
	@cl_mos = 0;	// the box closed itself
	callsub L_MomentEnd, 0;
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
	end;

// 3.9.1: "@clstory <request> yes|no": your answer to a story invite (L_ArcTry).
OnCmdStory:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	if (!@cl_stc) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_Gather;
	.@a = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_stc);
	.@b = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_stl);
	.@ok = 0;
	if (.@atcmd_parameters$[1] == "yes" && .@a >= 0 && .@b >= 0) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@a] > 0 && @cl_hp[.@b] > 0)
			.@ok = 1;
	}
	if (.@ok) {
		.@ch = @cl_stch;
		@cl_stc = 0;
		callsub L_StoryTell, .@a, .@b, .@ch;
	} else
		callsub L_StoryEnd, 0;
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
	end;

// 3.9.1: "@cljournal <request> <char id>": the Journal in the Bonds tab. Records,
// split by ~: J|chapters told; C|chapter|title|who heard it|turns ("A:text^B:text");
// L|chapter|title|when it comes; X|your answer in their story|what came of it ("" while
// it hasn't come yet); M|question|your answer (moments it asked you).
OnCmdJournal:
	freeloop(1);
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[1]);
	callsub L_BagParty;
	.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
	if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
		end;
	}
	.@nm$ = @clb_nm$[.@k];
	.@ar = callsub(L_Arch, readparam(Class, .@c));
	.@p$ = "m.c" + .@c;
	.@told = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arc", 0);
	.@sv = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".story", 1);	// which story (L_StoryVar)
	if (!modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".story") && callsub(L_ClassStory, readparam(Class, .@c)))
		.@sv = callsub(L_ClassStory, readparam(Class, .@c));	// not begun: most likely their class's
	.@ttl$ = .ttl$[.@c % .nt];
	if (modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".callname", 0) == 1)
		.@ttl$ = strcharinfo(0);
	.@out$ = "J|" + .@told;
	.@from = 1;
	if (!.@told && callsub(L_NoStory, readparam(Class, .@c))) {
		.@out$ += "~L|1||once they've chosen a class";
		.@from = 6;	// no chapters to list yet
	}
	for (.@ch = .@from; .@ch <= 5; .@ch++) {
		.@title$ = callsub(L_ArcText, .@ar, .@sv, .@ch, 1);
		if (.@ch > .@told) {
			.@when$ = "at the " + .bt_name$[.@ch] + " bond";
			if (.@ch >= 4)
				.@when$ += ", by a campfire";
			.@out$ += "~L|" + .@ch + "|" + .@title$ + "|" + .@when$;
			continue;
		}
		.@who$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arcl" + .@ch, "");
		.@hear$ = .@who$;
		if (.@hear$ == "")
			.@hear$ = "a friend";
		.@line$ = callsub(L_ArcText, .@ar, .@sv, .@ch, 0);
		if (charat(.@line$, 0) == "#")
			.@line$ = delchar(.@line$, 0);
		explode(.@turn$, .@line$, "^");
		.@tx$ = "";
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < getarraysize(.@turn$); .@j++) {
			.@s$ = charat(.@turn$[.@j], 0);
			.@other$ = .@hear$;
			if (.@s$ == "B")
				.@other$ = .@nm$;
			.@say$ = callsub(L_JText, delchar(.@turn$[.@j], 0), .@ttl$, .@other$);
			.@tx$ += (.@j ? "^" : "") + .@s$ + ":" + replacestr(callsub(L_Clean, .@say$), "^", "");
		}
		.@out$ += "~C|" + .@ch + "|" + .@title$ + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@hear$) + "|" + .@tx$;
	}
	// Their story choice, and the moments it asked you.
	for (.@m = 0; .@m < .mo_n; .@m++) {
		.@kd$ = .mo_kind$[.@m];
		.@ans = 0;
		.@own = 0;
		if (compare(.@kd$, "story")) {
			if (callsub(L_MoStory, .@kd$) != .@sv)
				continue;	// another story's choice
			.@own = (.@sv >= 11);
			.@kd$ = "story";
		}
		if (.mo_p[.@m] >= 0) {
			if (!.@own && .mo_p[.@m] != .@ar)
				continue;
			.@ans = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mo" + .@m, 0);
		} else if (.@kd$ == "check") {
			.@ans = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mo" + .@m, 0);
		} else {
			if (modstore_get("char", "mopw" + .@m, 0) != .@c)
				continue;
			.@ans = modstore_get("char", "mop" + .@m, 0);
		}
		if (.@ans < 1 || .@ans > 3)
			continue;
		.@lab$ = getd(".mo_o" + .@ans + "$[" + .@m + "]");
		if (.@kd$ == "story") {
			.@aft$ = "";
			if (!modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".mofu"))	// told you already
				.@aft$ = getd(".mo_x" + .@ans + "$[" + .@m + "]");
			.@out$ += "~X|" + callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_JText, .@lab$, .@ttl$, "")) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_JText, .@aft$, .@ttl$, ""));
			continue;
		}
		.@out$ += "~M|" + callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_JText, .mo_q$[.@m], .@ttl$, "")) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_JText, .@lab$, .@ttl$, ""));
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
	end;

OnCmdBag:
	// A full party's bags (arrows, potions, gear: 30+ stacks, each read field by
	// field) is more than the loop guard allows; it stopped the list half way
	// ("infinity loop"), so the window kept showing an old list. It ends with
	// this run.
	freeloop(1);
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	.@inv = getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_inventory");
	if (.@act$ == "list") {
		// 3.8.4: "list <cid> <have>": the bag, restock and gear of the companion
		// whose name tab is picked only (the first one when none is); the others
		// get their C line for the tabs. The whole party every 3 s made the game
		// choppy. When the window already has this answer (have 1) and nothing
		// changed, it gets "SAME" and doesn't redraw.
		.@pick = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		// 3.9.0: a window from before 3.8.4 (a cached copy after an update) sends no
		// companion at all: it gets every companion's bag, as it used to, instead of
		// only the first one's (Ekado's report: the others' gear looked gone).
		.@all = (.@atcmd_numparameters < 3);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		if (inarray(@clb_cid, .@pick) < 0)
			.@pick = @clb_cid[0];
		.@out$ = "P|" + modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + "|" + .@inv + "|" + .purse + "|" + (callsub(L_MapKind, strcharinfo(3)) == "town") + "|" + .@pick;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @clb_n; .@i++) {
			.@c = @clb_cid[.@i];
			.@cls = readparam(Class, .@c);
			.@fx = 0;
			if (checkriding(.@c))
				.@fx += 32;
			if (checkfalcon(.@c))
				.@fx += 16;
			.@out$ += "~C|" + .@c + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, @clb_nm$[.@i]) + "|" + jobname(.@cls)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@c) + "|" + getbattleflag("min_cloth_color") + "|" + callsub(L_ClothMax, .@cls)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@c) + "|" + getbattleflag("min_hair_color") + "|" + getbattleflag("max_hair_color")
				+ "|" + readparam(Weight, .@c) + "|" + readparam(MaxWeight, .@c)
				// 3.5.0: the look, for the Bag tab's character (the client's api.screens.stage):
				// class, sex, hair style, then the view ids the game itself sends: weapon,
				// shield, lower/top/middle headgear, garment; and the mount/falcon bits
				// (OPTION_RIDING 0x20, OPTION_FALCON 0x10).
				+ "|" + .@cls + "|" + readparam(Sex, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_HAIR, .@c)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_WEAPON, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_SHIELD, .@c)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_HEAD_BOTTOM, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_HEAD_TOP, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_HEAD_MID, .@c)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_ROBE, .@c) + "|" + .@fx
				// 3.9.0: its pet (fields 22-25): out 1/0 (-1 with the pets setting off), its
				// name, kept or not (L_PetState), and what it is.
				+ "|" + (.pets ? callsub(L_PetOn, .@c) : -1) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_PetName, .@c))
				+ "|" + callsub(L_PetState, .@c) + "|" + .pet_nm$[callsub(L_PetOf, .@cls)];
			if (.@c != .@pick && !.@all)
				continue;
			// Its restock list.
			.@rn = modstore_keys("char", "rs.c" + .@c, .@rids$);
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@rn; .@j++) {
				.@rid = atoi(.@rids$[.@j]);
				.@out$ += "~R|" + .@c + "|" + .@rid + "|" + modstore_get("char", "rs.c" + .@c + "." + .@rid, 0) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, getitemname(.@rid));
			}
			// The slots worn with gear you gave (the engine's given_mask). 3.5.0: its own
			// starting gear is listed too, marked own (the Bag tab draws every slot like the
			// Equip window); it can't be taken.
			.@gm = 0;
			if (query_sql("SELECT given_mask FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0)
					+ " AND name = '" + escape_sql(@clb_nm$[.@i]) + "' LIMIT 1", .@gmv) > 0)
				.@gm = .@gmv;
			// Gear you dropped on it in the Bag tab (our own record), and the pieces
			// of its own that came off for it (in its bag, not yours to take).
			.@gm = .@gm | callsub(L_DgMask, .@c);
			.@own$ = callsub(L_DgOwn, .@c);
			getinventorylist .@c;
			.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
				.@eq = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c);
				.@mine = 0;
				if (.@eq && .@eq != EQP_AMMO && !(.@eq & .@gm))
					.@mine = 1;	// its own gear
				.@id = getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c);
				if (!.@eq && compare(.@own$, "," + .@id + ","))
					continue;	// its own piece, waiting to go back on
				// Cards in its slots (card ids, not a forged or brewed item's maker),
				// as "id,id"; the window shows their names.
				.@cards$ = "";
				.@c1 = getvar(@inventorylist_card1[.@j], .@c);
				if (.@c1 != 255 && .@c1 != 254) {
					setarray .@cv, .@c1, getvar(@inventorylist_card2[.@j], .@c),
						getvar(@inventorylist_card3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_card4[.@j], .@c);
					for (.@s = 0; .@s < 4; .@s++) {
						if (.@cv[.@s] > 1000 && .@cv[.@s] < 100000000)
							.@cards$ += (.@cards$ == "" ? "" : ",") + .@cv[.@s];
					}
				}
				// Random options (Affix Forge and the like) as "id:value,id:value";
				// the window turns them into "STR +3".
				setarray .@ov, getvar(@inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value3[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id5[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value5[.@j], .@c);
				.@opts$ = "";
				for (.@s = 0; .@s < 10; .@s += 2) {
					if (.@ov[.@s] > 0)
						.@opts$ += (.@opts$ == "" ? "" : ",") + .@ov[.@s] + ":" + .@ov[.@s + 1];
				}
				.@out$ += "~I|" + .@c + "|" + getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@c) + "|" + .@id
					+ "|" + getvar(@inventorylist_amount[.@j], .@c) + "|" + .@eq
					+ "|" + getvar(@inventorylist_refine[.@j], .@c) + "|" + .@cards$ + "|" + .@opts$
					+ "|" + (getvar(@inventorylist_bound[.@j], .@c) > 0) + "|" + (getvar(@inventorylist_expire[.@j], .@c) > 0)
					+ "|" + .@mine + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, getitemname(.@id));
			}
		}
		if (atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]) == 1 && .@out$ == @clb_last$) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "SAME";
			end;
		}
		@clb_last$ = .@out$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "take") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@idx = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		.@amt = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]);
		if (!.@inv) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Turn on Settings > Population > Companion inventory first. Without it a companion's potions and arrows are free and refill by themselves, so there's nothing of yours in its bag.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		getinventorylist .@c;
		.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
			if (getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@c) == .@idx)
				break;
		}
		if (.@j >= .@cnt) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That item isn't in the bag any more.";
			end;
		}
		.@id = getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c);
		.@have = getvar(@inventorylist_amount[.@j], .@c);
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c) != 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " is wearing that. Worn gear comes back through the app's Companions window (Gear).";
			end;
		}
		// Rentals stay: a copy would come back without its time limit.
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_expire[.@j], .@c)) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That one is a rental, it can't be handed over.";
			end;
		}
		if (compare(callsub(L_DgOwn, .@c), "," + .@id + ",")) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That's " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + "'s own, it goes back on when you take back what you gave.";
			end;
		}
		.@amt = min(max(.@amt, 1), .@have);
		if (!checkweight(.@id, .@amt)) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|You can't carry that much.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_ToYou, .@c, .@j, .@amt;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Took " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " from " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + ".";
		end;
	}
	// Test build (2.3.0): "give <char id> <index> <item id> <amount>", an item
	// dragged from your inventory onto a companion in the Bag tab. It goes into
	// its bag, with refine, cards, random options and bound type. Gear it can
	// wear it puts on at once; the engine's given_mask is only set by a real
	// trade, so that slot is "from you" in our own record (mod store, char
	// scope, dg.c<char id>: "<slots>,<item id>,<its own item id>" entries split
	// by ";"), which gearback below reads.
	if (.@act$ == "give") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@idx = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		.@want = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]);
		.@amt = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[5]);
		if (!.@inv) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Turn on Settings > Population > Companion inventory first; without it a companion has no bag of its own.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		.@aid = 0;
		if (.@k >= 0)
			.@aid = getcharid(3, @clb_nm$[.@k]);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0 || .@aid <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't here.";
			end;
		}
		.@nm$ = @clb_nm$[.@k];
		// Your stack: the index the window saw, or else the first loose stack of it.
		getinventorylist;
		.@j = -1;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @inventorylist_count; .@i++) {
			if (@inventorylist_id[.@i] != .@want || @inventorylist_equip[.@i])
				continue;
			if (@inventorylist_idx[.@i] == .@idx) {
				.@j = .@i;
				break;
			}
			if (.@j < 0)
				.@j = .@i;
		}
		if (.@j < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That isn't in your inventory (take it off first if you wear it).";
			end;
		}
		if (@inventorylist_expire[.@j]) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Rentals can't be handed over.";
			end;
		}
		.@id = .@want;
		.@idx = @inventorylist_idx[.@j];
		// (Copied out first: min() given an array element reads the whole rest of
		// the array, so it came out as the smallest stack after this one.)
		.@have = @inventorylist_amount[.@j];
		.@amt = min(max(.@amt, 1), .@have);
		.@iw = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_WEIGHT);
		.@room = readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) - readparam(Weight, .@c);
		if (.@iw * .@amt > .@room) {
			// How many would fit, and the weight in the game's units (tenths).
			.@fit = 0;
			if (.@iw > 0 && .@room > 0)
				.@fit = .@room / .@iw;
			.@wt$ = " (weight " + (readparam(Weight, .@c) / 10) + " / " + (readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) / 10) + ")";
			if (.@fit > 0)
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + .@nm$ + " can only carry " + .@fit + " more " + getitemname(.@id) + .@wt$ + ". Take something heavy back first, or give fewer.";
			else
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + .@nm$ + "'s bag is full" + .@wt$ + ". Take something heavy back first.";
			end;
		}
		.@ident = @inventorylist_identify[.@j];
		.@ref = @inventorylist_refine[.@j];
		.@attr = @inventorylist_attribute[.@j];
		.@c1 = @inventorylist_card1[.@j];
		.@c2 = @inventorylist_card2[.@j];
		.@c3 = @inventorylist_card3[.@j];
		.@c4 = @inventorylist_card4[.@j];
		.@bound = @inventorylist_bound[.@j];
		setarray .@oid, @inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id3[.@j],
			@inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id5[.@j];
		setarray .@oval, @inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value3[.@j],
			@inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value5[.@j];
		setarray .@opar, @inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@j],
			@inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@j];
		// Into its bag first, then out of yours.
		if (.@bound)
			getitembound3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@bound, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar, .@aid;
		else
			getitem3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar, .@aid;
		delitemidx .@idx, .@amt;
		.@msg$ = "Gave " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " to " + .@nm$ + ".";
		// Gear: it puts it on, as a trade does. Whatever you gave for that slot
		// before comes back to you first (a trade swaps it the same way).
		.@loc = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_LOCATIONS);
		.@type = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_TYPE);
		if ((.@type == IT_WEAPON || .@type == IT_ARMOR) && .@loc > 0 && .@amt == 1 && .@ident) {
			.@gm = 0;
			if (query_sql("SELECT given_mask FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0)
					+ " AND name = '" + escape_sql(.@nm$) + "' LIMIT 1", .@gmv) > 0)
				.@gm = .@gmv;
			.@dm = callsub(L_DgMask, .@c);
			// The slot. An accessory with a side free just goes on (rAthena fills
			// the right when it's empty, else the left) and nothing comes off. With
			// both sides worn it replaces the companion's own piece, else the left
			// one. (EQP_ACC_RL: a plain EQP_ACC is no constant here, just an empty
			// variable, so every ring was taken for "both sides" and sent the worn
			// one back, 2.4.0.)
			.@t = .@loc;
			// 3.9.0: dual wield (app 1.7.3: equip takes a position). A dagger,
			// one-handed sword or one-handed axe for an Assassin whose right hand
			// holds a weapon and whose left hand is free goes in the left hand.
			// (A katar fills both hands, so the left isn't free then.)
			if (.@loc == EQP_HAND_R && (eaclass(readparam(Class, .@c)) & EAJ_UPPERMASK) == (EAJ_THIEF | EAJL_2_1)) {
				.@sub = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_SUBTYPE);
				if ((.@sub == W_DAGGER || .@sub == W_1HSWORD || .@sub == W_1HAXE) && getequipid(EQI_HAND_R, .@c) > 0 && getequipid(EQI_HAND_L, .@c) <= 0)
					.@t = EQP_HAND_L;
			}
			if (.@loc == EQP_ACC_RL) {
				if (getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) <= 0 || getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) <= 0)
					.@t = 0;
				// 3.8.2: one side already wears this very item: the other side, so
				// a second Skull Ring makes a pair instead of replacing the first.
				else if (getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) == .@id && getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) != .@id)
					.@t = EQP_ACC_R;
				else if (getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) == .@id && getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) != .@id)
					.@t = EQP_ACC_L;
				else if (((.@gm | .@dm) & EQP_ACC_L) && !((.@gm | .@dm) & EQP_ACC_R))
					.@t = EQP_ACC_R;
				else
					.@t = EQP_ACC_L;
			}
			.@ok = 1;
			.@extra$ = "";
			if (.@t & .@dm) {
				.@back$ = callsub(L_DgBack, .@c, .@nm$, .@t & .@dm);
				if (substr(.@back$, 0, 2) == "ER") {
					.@ok = 0;
					.@msg$ += " It's in the bag: " + substr(.@back$, 4, getstrlen(.@back$) - 1);
				} else if (.@back$ != "") {
					.@extra$ += " " + substr(.@back$, 3, getstrlen(.@back$) - 1);
				}
			}
			if (.@ok && (.@t & .@gm)) {
				// Traded gear: the engine's own way back (as the Take back button).
				setarray .@bit, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_SHOES, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID;
				setarray .@word$, "headlow", "weapon", "garment", "accl", "armor", "shield", "shoes", "accr", "headtop", "headmid";
				.@slots$ = "";
				for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@bit); .@s++) {
					if (.@t & .@gm & .@bit[.@s])
						.@slots$ += " " + .@word$[.@s];
				}
				if (.@t & .@gm & (EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW | EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT))
					.@slots$ += " costume";
				if (.@slots$ != "") {
					atcommand "@companion gear " + .@nm$ + .@slots$;
					.@extra$ += " What you gave for that slot went back to you.";
					// 3.8.1: the engine does that a moment later, not now, and takes
					// back whatever the slot holds by then: the new piece, if it went
					// on at once (a player's report, 2 Skull Rings: the new one came
					// straight back). So it goes on once the engine is done (L_WearLater).
					.@later = 1;
				}
			}
			if (.@ok && .@later) {
				if (!getd("@cl_pw_" + .@c))
					@cl_pwl[getarraysize(@cl_pwl)] = .@c;
				setd "@cl_pw_" + .@c, .@id;
				setd "@cl_pwt_" + .@c, .@t;
				setd "@cl_pwx_" + .@c, gettimetick(0) + 10000;
				setd "@cl_pwn_" + .@c + "$", .@nm$;
				.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " is taking the old one off first: the " + getitemname(.@id) + " goes on in a moment." + .@extra$;
			} else if (.@ok)
				.@msg$ = callsub(L_WearNow, .@c, .@nm$, .@id, .@t, .@aid, .@extra$, .@msg$);
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// 3.2.0: "pet <char id> <1|0>", the Bag tab's Pet switch: the companion brings its
	// class's pet, or sends it home. Kept in the store (pet.c<char id>).
	if (.@act$ == "pet") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@on = (atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]) > 0);
		if (!.pets) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Pets are off in this mod's settings (Party behaviour > Companion pets).";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		// 3.9.0: only a pet you let them keep.
		if (!callsub(L_PetKept, .@c)) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " hasn't got a pet of their own yet.";
			end;
		}
		if (.@on)
			modstore_set "char", "pet.c" + .@c, 1;
		else
			modstore_delete "char", "pet.c" + .@c;
		setd "@cl_po_" + .@c, .@on + 1;
		.@pn$ = callsub(L_PetName, .@c);
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
		if (.@i >= 0) {
			.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
			if (.@on) {
				setd "@cl_qpw_" + .@aid, 0;	// at the next tick
				if (.chatter)
					callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "petshow", "", ET_DELIGHT, 0, .@pn$;
			} else {
				.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + .@aid);
				callsub L_PetHide, .@g;
				setd "@cl_pg_" + .@aid, 0;
				if (.chatter)
					callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "petoff", "", 0, 0, .@pn$;
			}
		}
		if (.@on)
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + "'s " + .@pn$ + " comes along" + (.@i >= 0 ? "." : " once they're back with you.");
		else
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + "'s " + .@pn$ + " goes home.";
		end;
	}
	// 3.9.0: "keep <char id> yes|later": the answer to "Can we keep it?" (L_PetAsk), or
	// the Bag tab's "Let them keep it" (yes). Yes: the pet is theirs and comes along
	// (the window then asks for a name, "petname ... new"). Not now: asked once, the
	// second ask waits an hour of play together; asked twice, it stops asking.
	if (.@act$ == "keep") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@yes = (.@atcmd_parameters$[3] == "yes");
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (!.pets || .@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
		.@sp$ = .pet_nm$[callsub(L_PetOf, readparam(Class, .@c))];
		if (.@yes) {
			modstore_set "char", "pk.c" + .@c, 1;
			modstore_set "char", "pet.c" + .@c, 1;
			setd "@cl_po_" + .@c, 0;	// read again
			if (@cl_akc == .@c) {
				@cl_akc = 0;	// its pet stays: it's theirs now
				@cl_aks = 0;
				@cl_aknext = gettimetick(2) + 600;
			}
			setd "@cl_pv_" + .@c, 0;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@sp$;
			end;
		}
		if (@cl_akc != .@c) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
			end;
		}
		.@n = modstore_get("char", "pa.c" + .@c, 0) + 1;
		modstore_set "char", "pa.c" + .@c, .@n;
		if (.@n < 2)
			modstore_set "char", "pat.c" + .@c, modstore_get("char", "m.c" + .@c + ".mins", 0) + 60;
		if (.@i >= 0 && .chatter)
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, (.@n >= 2 ? "plast" : "plater"), "", (.@n >= 2 ? ET_SWEAT : ET_OK), 0, .@sp$;
		@cl_aks = 0;	// the box closed itself
		callsub L_PetAskEnd, gettimetick(2) + 600;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	// 3.9.0: "petname <char id> new|re <name ...>": the name you give a kept pet, up to 23
	// letters ("" = what it is). New: right after the yes, with its companion's welcome.
	// The pet steps out and back in to wear it (the game keeps the old name on screen).
	if (.@act$ == "petname") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@new = (.@atcmd_parameters$[3] == "new");
		.@nm$ = "";
		for (.@k = 4; .@k < .@atcmd_numparameters; .@k++)
			.@nm$ += (.@nm$ != "" ? " " : "") + .@atcmd_parameters$[.@k];
		.@nm$ = replacestr(replacestr(replacestr(.@nm$, "|", ""), "~", ""), "^", "");
		if (getstrlen(.@nm$) > 23)
			.@nm$ = substr(.@nm$, 0, 22);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (!.pets || .@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		if (!callsub(L_PetKept, .@c)) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " hasn't got a pet of their own yet.";
			end;
		}
		.@sp$ = .pet_nm$[callsub(L_PetOf, readparam(Class, .@c))];
		if (.@nm$ == "" || .@nm$ == .@sp$)
			modstore_delete "char", "pn.c" + .@c;
		else
			modstore_set "char", "pn.c" + .@c, .@nm$;
		.@pn$ = callsub(L_PetName, .@c);
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
		if (.@i >= 0) {
			.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
			callsub L_PetHide, getd("@cl_pg_" + .@aid);
			setd "@cl_pg_" + .@aid, 0;
			setd "@cl_qpw_" + .@aid, 0;
			if (.@new && .chatter)
				callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "pwel", "", ET_THROB, 0, .@pn$;
		}
		if (.@new)
			dispbottom "[Companions] " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " and " + .@pn$ + " are together now. Rename " + .@pn$ + " any time in the Companions window, Bag tab.", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + "'s pet is called " + .@pn$ + ".";
		end;
	}
	// 3.2.0: "show me your pets" in party chat (client/index.js): the companions with
	// a pet out show it off, up to four of them.
	if (.@act$ == "petshow") {
		if (!.pets) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|pets off";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_Gather;
		.@n = 0;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n && .@n < 4; .@i++) {
			.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@i]);
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !.@g || !unitexists(.@g))
				continue;
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "petshow", "", ET_DELIGHT, 0, callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@i]);
			if (.emotes)
				emotion ET_LUV, .@g;
			.@n++;
		}
		if (!.@n)
			dispbottom "[Companions] None of your companions here has a pet out. Allow one in the Companions window, Bag tab (Pet).", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@n;
		end;
	}
	// Dye: "dye <char id> cloth|hair <colour>". Shown at once (setlook), and kept
	// in the mod store (char scope, dye.c<char id>.cl / .hc), because the engine
	// saves a companion's colours only when it is recruited; L_Dyes puts them
	// back on whenever the engine respawns it with its old ones.
	if (.@act$ == "dye") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@kind$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[3];
		.@v = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0 || readparam(Hp, .@c) < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't here.";
			end;
		}
		if (.@kind$ == "cloth") {
			.@v = cap_value(.@v, getbattleflag("min_cloth_color"), callsub(L_ClothMax, readparam(Class, .@c)));
			setlook LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
			modstore_set "char", "dye.c" + .@c + ".cl", .@v;
		} else if (.@kind$ == "hair") {
			.@v = cap_value(.@v, getbattleflag("min_hair_color"), getbattleflag("max_hair_color"));
			setlook LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
			modstore_set "char", "dye.c" + .@c + ".hc", .@v;
		} else {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Unknown dye.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	// Worn gear you gave: the engine's own "@companion gear <name> <slots>" (the
	// app's Gear tab sends the same), so it goes back to you its usual way and
	// the companion puts its own piece back on. Costume slots go back together.
	if (.@act$ == "gearback") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@eq = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0 || .@eq <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		// Gear you dropped on it in the Bag tab (test build 2.3.0): our own record,
		// not the engine's. Off, back to you, and its own piece back on.
		.@back$ = callsub(L_DgBack, .@c, @clb_nm$[.@k], .@eq);
		if (.@back$ != "") {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@back$;
			end;
		}
		setarray .@bit, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_SHOES, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID;
		setarray .@word$, "headlow", "weapon", "garment", "accl", "armor", "shield", "shoes", "accr", "headtop", "headmid";
		.@slots$ = "";
		for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@bit); .@s++) {
			if (.@eq & .@bit[.@s])
				.@slots$ += " " + .@word$[.@s];
		}
		if (.@eq & (EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW | EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT))
			.@slots$ += " costume";
		if (.@eq & (EQP_SHADOW_ARMOR | EQP_SHADOW_WEAPON | EQP_SHADOW_SHIELD | EQP_SHADOW_SHOES | EQP_SHADOW_ACC_R | EQP_SHADOW_ACC_L))
			.@slots$ += " shadow";
		if (.@slots$ == "") {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Not a slot that can be handed back.";
			end;
		}
		atcommand "@companion gear " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + .@slots$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Asked " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " to hand it back (see your chat).";
		end;
	}
	// 3.1.0: a companion's restock list. An item dragged onto its Restock row
	// (or a changed amount): keep this many; 0 takes it off the list.
	if (.@act$ == "rsset") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@id = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		.@keep = cap_value(atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]), 0, 30000);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		.@nm$ = @clb_nm$[.@k];
		.@key$ = "rs.c" + .@c + "." + .@id;
		if (!.@keep) {
			modstore_delete "char", .@key$;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + getitemname(.@id) + " is off " + .@nm$ + "'s restock list.";
			end;
		}
		if (!compare(.rs_ok$, "," + .@id + ",")) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + getitemname(.@id) + " isn't sold in NPC shops, so it can't be restocked. Potions, Blue Gemstones, arrows, bullets and the like can" + (.@id == 715 || .@id == 716 ? " (Red and Yellow Gemstones too, with that setting on)." : ".");
			end;
		}
		if (!modstore_exists("char", .@key$) && modstore_count("char", "rs.c" + .@c) >= .rs_max) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + .@nm$ + "'s list is full (" + .rs_max + " items). Take one off first.";
			end;
		}
		modstore_set "char", .@key$, .@keep;
		.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " keeps " + .@keep + " " + getitemname(.@id) + ", restocked in town.";
		// 3.1.1: dropped from your inventory (index getarg 5): your stack goes in first,
		// up to the number to keep; the purse only buys what's still missing. (3.1.0
		// only bought from the purse, so your stack stayed and it looked doubled.)
		if (.@atcmd_numparameters > 5 && .@inv) {
			.@from = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[5]);
			.@aid = getcharid(3, .@nm$);
			.@need = 0;
			if (.@aid > 0)
				.@need = .@keep - countitem(.@id, .@aid);
			if (.@need > 0) {
				getinventorylist;
				.@j = -1;
				for (.@i = 0; .@i < @inventorylist_count; .@i++) {
					if (@inventorylist_id[.@i] != .@id || @inventorylist_equip[.@i] || @inventorylist_expire[.@i] || @inventorylist_bound[.@i])
						continue;
					if (@inventorylist_idx[.@i] == .@from) {
						.@j = .@i;
						break;
					}
					if (.@j < 0)
						.@j = .@i;
				}
				if (.@j >= 0) {
					.@have = @inventorylist_amount[.@j];
					.@n = min(.@need, .@have);
					.@iw = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_WEIGHT);
					if (.@iw > 0)
						.@n = min(.@n, (readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) - readparam(Weight, .@c)) / .@iw);
					if (.@n > 0) {
						getitem .@id, .@n, .@aid;
						delitemidx @inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@n;
						.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " keeps " + .@keep + " " + getitemname(.@id) + ": " + .@n + " from you now, the rest restocked in town.";
						if (.@n >= .@need)
							.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " keeps " + .@keep + " " + getitemname(.@id) + ": " + .@n + " from you now, topped up in town from here on.";
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (callsub(L_MapKind, strcharinfo(3)) == "town" && .@inv) {
			callsub L_Restock, .@c;
			if (@rs_msg$ != "")
				.@msg$ += " " + @rs_msg$;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// 3.1.0: the Loot tab. The party bag lives in the mod store (char scope:
	// loot.<item id> = how many, lootat.<item id> = when it was last found).
	if (.@act$ == "lootlist") {
		.@oc = callsub(L_Discount, 38);
		.@out$ = "P|" + modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + "|" + modstore_get("char", "lttrip", 0) + "|" + .@oc + "|" + (.loot_share > 0);
		.@n = modstore_keys("char", "loot", .@ids$);
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			.@id = atoi(.@ids$[.@j]);
			.@amt = modstore_get("char", "loot." + .@id, 0);
			if (.@amt <= 0)
				continue;
			.@out$ += "~L|" + .@id + "|" + .@amt + "|" + modstore_get("char", "lootat." + .@id, 0)
				+ "|" + (getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_SELL) * (100 + .@oc) / 100)
				+ "|" + (getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_TYPE) == IT_CARD) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, getitemname(.@id));
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "ltake" || .@act$ == "lsell") {
		.@id = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@have = modstore_get("char", "loot." + .@id, 0);
		.@amt = min(max(atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]), 1), .@have);
		if (.@have <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That isn't in the party bag any more.";
			end;
		}
		if (.@act$ == "ltake") {
			if (!checkweight(.@id, .@amt)) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|You can't carry that much.";
				end;
			}
			getitem .@id, .@amt;
			.@msg$ = "Took " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " from the party bag.";
		} else {
			.@oc = callsub(L_Discount, 38);
			.@z = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_SELL) * (100 + .@oc) / 100 * .@amt;
			if (modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + .@z > 1000000000) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|The purse is full (1,000,000,000 z). Take some zeny out first.";
				end;
			}
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@z);
			.@msg$ = "Sold " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " for " + callfunc("F_InsertComma", .@z) + " z, into the purse.";
			if (.@oc > 0)
				.@msg$ += " (" + @rs_who$ + "'s Overcharge +" + .@oc + "%)";
		}
		if (.@have - .@amt <= 0) {
			modstore_delete "char", "loot." + .@id;
			modstore_delete "char", "lootat." + .@id;
		} else
			modstore_inc("char", "loot." + .@id, -.@amt);
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// 3.10.1: Sell all. Everything in the party bag but cards (a rare card shouldn't go
	// by accident; they stay for Take or their own Sell).
	if (.@act$ == "lsellall") {
		.@oc = callsub(L_Discount, 38);
		.@n = modstore_keys("char", "loot", .@ids$);
		.@z = 0;
		.@k = 0;
		.@cards = 0;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			.@id = atoi(.@ids$[.@j]);
			.@amt = modstore_get("char", "loot." + .@id, 0);
			if (.@amt <= 0)
				continue;
			if (getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_TYPE) == IT_CARD) {
				.@cards++;
				continue;
			}
			.@z += getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_SELL) * (100 + .@oc) / 100 * .@amt;
			.@sid[.@k] = .@id;
			.@sam[.@k] = .@amt;
			.@k++;
		}
		if (!.@k) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Nothing to sell" + (.@cards ? ": only cards in the bag." : ".");
			end;
		}
		if (modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + .@z > 1000000000) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|The purse is full (1,000,000,000 z). Take some zeny out first.";
			end;
		}
		.@things = 0;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@k; .@j++) {
			modstore_delete "char", "loot." + .@sid[.@j];
			modstore_delete "char", "lootat." + .@sid[.@j];
			.@things += .@sam[.@j];
		}
		modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@z);
		.@msg$ = "Sold " + callfunc("F_InsertComma", .@things) + " thing" + (.@things == 1 ? "" : "s") + " for " + callfunc("F_InsertComma", .@z) + " z, into the purse.";
		if (.@oc > 0)
			.@msg$ += " (" + @rs_who$ + "'s Overcharge +" + .@oc + "%)";
		if (.@cards)
			.@msg$ += " Cards stay in the bag.";
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "withdraw" || .@act$ == "deposit") {
		.@z = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@p = modstore_get("char", "purse", 0);
		if (.@act$ == "withdraw") {
			.@z = min(.@z, .@p, 1000000000 - Zeny);
			if (.@z <= 0) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Nothing to take out.";
				end;
			}
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", -.@z);
			Zeny += .@z;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Took " + .@z + " zeny out of the purse.";
		} else {
			.@z = min(.@z, Zeny, 1000000000 - .@p);
			if (.@z <= 0) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Nothing to put in.";
				end;
			}
			Zeny -= .@z;
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@z);
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Put " + .@z + " zeny in the purse.";
		}
		end;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Unknown request.";
	end;

// The companions in your party that are loaded (on any map): @clb_n of them,
// char ids in @clb_cid, names in @clb_nm$ (character variables: a callsub
// has .@ variables of its own).
L_BagParty:
	@clb_n = 0;
	deletearray @clb_cid;
	deletearray @clb_nm$;
	.@pid = getcharid(1);
	if (!.@pid)
		return;
	getpartymember .@pid, 1;
	.@pc = $@partymembercount;
	copyarray .@pcid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@pc;
	getpartymember .@pid, 0;
	copyarray .@pnm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@pc;
	for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@pc; .@q++) {
		if (.@pcid[.@q] < 95000000 || readparam(Hp, .@pcid[.@q]) < 0)
			continue;
		@clb_cid[@clb_n] = .@pcid[.@q];
		@clb_nm$[@clb_n] = .@pnm$[.@q];
		@clb_n++;
	}
	return;

// 3.1.0, the loot bag: party kills. Scripts get no event for a companion's kill
// (the engine drops script events for its shells), so every tick this looks at the
// monsters within 9 cells of you or a companion. One seen last tick that is now
// gone, at 0 HP, or back at full HP far away (a map spawn keeps its id and comes
// back elsewhere) was beaten: a party kill, whoever landed it. Slaves don't count.
// Last tick's monsters: @lt_g (id), @lt_c (class), @lt_x/@lt_y, @lt_n of them.
L_LootTrack:
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	if (.@m$ != @lt_map$) {
		@lt_map$ = .@m$;
		@lt_n = 0;
	}
	if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOMOBLOOT)) {
		@lt_n = 0;
		return;
	}
	// Where the party stands (you first), and the box round all of you.
	.@px[0] = .@x;
	.@py[0] = .@y;
	.@np = 1;
	.@x1 = .@x - 10;
	.@x2 = .@x + 10;
	.@y1 = .@y - 10;
	.@y2 = .@y + 10;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@m$)
			continue;
		.@ddx = .@cx - .@x;
		.@ddy = .@cy - .@y;
		if (max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy) > 40)
			continue;	// far off on its own
		.@px[.@np] = .@cx;
		.@py[.@np] = .@cy;
		.@np++;
		.@x1 = min(.@x1, .@cx - 10);
		.@x2 = max(.@x2, .@cx + 10);
		.@y1 = min(.@y1, .@cy - 10);
		.@y2 = max(.@y2, .@cy + 10);
	}
	// Last tick's monsters: which were beaten?
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @lt_n; .@k++) {
		.@g = @lt_g[.@k];
		.@dead = 0;
		if (!unitexists(.@g))
			.@dead = 1;
		else {
			getunitdata .@g, .@d;
			.@hp = .@d[UMOB_HP];
			.@ddx = .@d[UMOB_X] - @lt_x[.@k];
			.@ddy = .@d[UMOB_Y] - @lt_y[.@k];
			if (.@hp <= 0)
				.@dead = 1;
			else if (.@hp >= .@d[UMOB_MAXHP] && max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy) > 15)
				.@dead = 1;	// beaten, and already back somewhere else
		}
		if (.@dead) {
			callsub L_LootKill, @lt_c[.@k], @lt_x[.@k], @lt_y[.@k];
			// Diablorok rolls its loot on kill events, which companions never send: hand it this one.
			if ($@diablorok_on && @dk_qn < 32) {
				@dk_qg[@dk_qn] = .@g;
				@dk_qc[@dk_qn] = @lt_c[.@k];
				@dk_qx[@dk_qn] = @lt_x[.@k];
				@dk_qy[@dk_qn] = @lt_y[.@k];
				@dk_qn++;
				if (@dk_qn == 1)
					addtimer 50, "Diablorok_Drops::OnPartyKill";
			}
		}
	}
	// This tick's monsters near one of you.
	@lt_n = 0;
	.@n = getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@m$, .@x1, .@y1, .@x2, .@y2, .@u);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n && @lt_n < 64; .@k++) {
		.@g = .@u[.@k];
		getunitdata .@g, .@d;
		if (.@d[UMOB_HP] <= 0 || .@d[UMOB_MASTERAID] > 0)
			continue;
		.@mx = .@d[UMOB_X];
		.@my = .@d[UMOB_Y];
		for (.@p = 0; .@p < .@np; .@p++) {
			.@ddx = .@mx - .@px[.@p];
			.@ddy = .@my - .@py[.@p];
			if (max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy) <= 9)
				break;
		}
		if (.@p >= .@np)
			continue;
		@lt_g[@lt_n] = .@g;
		@lt_c[@lt_n] = .@d[UMOB_CLASS];
		@lt_x[@lt_n] = .@mx;
		@lt_y[@lt_n] = .@my;
		@lt_n++;
	}
	return;

// A party kill of monster class getarg(0) at getarg(1),getarg(2): maybe one thing
// from its drop list into the party bag (mod store, char scope: loot.<item id> =
// how many, lootat.<item id> = when it was last found). Each drop is tried at
// .loot_share per thousand of its normal rate, from a random place in the list.
// Cards, gear and drops of 1% or rarer are rare finds: a line, and the name of
// the companion standing closest.
L_LootKill:
	.@cls = getarg(0);
	if (!getmobdrops(.@cls) || $@MobDrop_count < 1)
		return;
	.@c = $@MobDrop_count;
	copyarray .@it[0], $@MobDrop_item[0], .@c;
	copyarray .@rt[0], $@MobDrop_rate[0], .@c;
	.@s = 0;
	if (.@c > 1)
		.@s = rand(.@c);
	.@id = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@c; .@k++) {
		.@j = (.@s + .@k) % .@c;
		if (.@it[.@j] <= 0 || .@rt[.@j] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (rand(10000000) < .@rt[.@j] * .loot_share) {
			.@id = .@it[.@j];
			.@r = .@rt[.@j];
			break;
		}
	}
	if (!.@id)
		return;
	modstore_inc("char", "loot." + .@id, 1);
	modstore_set "char", "lootat." + .@id, gettimetick(2);
	@lt_found++;
	.@type = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_TYPE);
	if (.@type != IT_CARD && .@type != IT_WEAPON && .@type != IT_ARMOR && .@r > 100)
		return;	// a common find: it's in the Loot tab, nothing said
	// The best of the trip, for the summary in town: a card over anything else.
	if (@lt_best$ == "" || .@type == IT_CARD)
		@lt_best$ = getitemname(.@id);
	// Who found it: the companion standing closest to where it fell.
	.@b = -1;
	.@bd = 99;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0)
			continue;
		.@ddx = .@cx - getarg(1);
		.@ddy = .@cy - getarg(2);
		.@dd = max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy);
		if (.@dd < .@bd) {
			.@bd = .@dd;
			.@b = .@i;
		}
	}
	if (.@b < 0) {
		dispbottom "[Loot] Found: " + getitemname(.@id) + ". It's in the party bag (Companions window, Loot tab).", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	dispbottom "[Loot] " + @cl_nm$[.@b] + " found " + getitemname(.@id) + "! It's in the party bag (Loot tab).", 0xFFCC66;
	// A word about it, at most every 15 s (a card always).
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	if (.chatter && (.@type == IT_CARD || .@sec >= @lt_saycd)) {
		@lt_saycd = .@sec + 15;
		.@em = ET_DELIGHT;
		if (.@type == IT_CARD)
			.@em = ET_THROB;
		callsub L_QueueFmt, .@b, "found", "", .@em, 0, getitemname(.@id);
	}
	return;

// Back in town: what the trip turned up (@lt_found finds since the last town,
// @lt_best$ the best), told once.
L_LootHaul:
	if (@lt_found <= 0)
		return;
	.@what$ = @lt_found + " thing" + (@lt_found == 1 ? "" : "s");
	if (@lt_best$ != "")
		.@what$ += ", the best a " + @lt_best$;
	dispbottom "[Loot] This trip: " + .@what$ + ". See the Loot tab in the Companions window (Alt+N).", 0xFFCC66;
	if (.chatter && @lt_best$ != "") {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "haul", "", ET_MONEY, @lt_found, @lt_best$;
	}
	@lt_found = 0;
	@lt_best$ = "";
	return;

// 3.8.0: potions and food, the user's rules (setting drink_hp, default 50). The
// engine's own habit (only in a fight, under 40% HP or 20% SP, only potions, never
// over 50% weight) stays underneath; this comes first. Like a careful player:
// - only in a fight, or while you walk on (standing still after a fight they sit
//   and rest instead);
// - under drink_hp % HP: the healing item that best fits the wound (the smallest
//   that lifts it back over the line, cheapest of equals); a Priest or Acolyte
//   with SP gets 2 seconds to heal first, and Acolytes, Priests and Crusaders
//   heal themselves while their SP lasts;
// - under 25% HP: the strongest item at once; Yggdrasil Berry and Seed only
//   under 15%;
// - SP under 20%: the SP item that best fits, up to 30%;
// - bag weight never stops it; one item a second at most.
// getarg(0): the tick's second.
L_Drink:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@now = gettimetick(0);
	if (!@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_still > 3)
		return;	// standing still after a fight: they rest
	// Who heals now: 2 an Acolyte or Priest (heals others too), 1 a Crusader
	// (itself), with 15% SP or more. Monks leave Heal out of their builds.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@heal[.@i] = 0;
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || @cl_msp[.@i] <= 0 || @cl_sp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_msp[.@i] < 15)
			continue;
		.@ea = eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]);
		.@up = .@ea & EAJ_UPPERMASK;
		if (.@up == EAJ_PRIEST || ((.@ea & EAJ_BASEMASK) == EAJ_ACOLYTE && !(.@ea & EAJL_2)))
			.@heal[.@i] = 2;
		else if (.@up == EAJ_CRUSADER)
			.@heal[.@i] = 1;
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || @cl_mhp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@hpp = @cl_hp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_mhp[.@i];
		.@kind = 0;	// 1 HP, 2 SP
		if (.@hpp < .drink_hp) {
			.@kind = 1;
			if (.@hpp >= 25) {
				if (.@heal[.@i]) {
					.@kind = 0;	// it heals itself while its SP lasts
				} else {
					// A healer in the party gets 2 seconds first.
					.@hl = 0;
					for (.@j = 0; .@j < @cl_n; .@j++) {
						if (.@j != .@i && .@heal[.@j] == 2)
							.@hl = 1;
					}
					.@since = getd("@cl_dw_" + .@aid);
					if (!.@since) {
						setd "@cl_dw_" + .@aid, .@now;
						.@since = .@now;
					}
					if (.@hl && .@now - .@since < 2000)
						.@kind = 0;
				}
			}
		} else
			setd "@cl_dw_" + .@aid, 0;
		if (!.@kind && @cl_msp[.@i] > 0 && @cl_sp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_msp[.@i] < 20)
			.@kind = 2;
		if (!.@kind || .@now < getd("@cl_dn_" + .@aid))
			continue;
		if (.@kind == 1) {
			.@max = @cl_mhp[.@i];
			.@need = .@max * .drink_hp / 100 - @cl_hp[.@i] + 1;
			.@panic = (.@hpp < 25);
			.@ygg = (.@hpp < 15);
		} else {
			.@max = @cl_msp[.@i];
			.@need = .@max * 30 / 100 - @cl_sp[.@i] + 1;
			.@panic = 0;
			.@ygg = 0;
		}
		// The smallest item that does the job (the list is cheapest first, so a
		// tie keeps the cheaper), and the strongest one carried.
		.@best = -1;
		.@bv = 0;
		.@big = -1;
		.@bigv = 0;
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < .dk_n; .@k++) {
			.@v = .dk_hp[.@k];
			if (.@kind == 2)
				.@v = .dk_sp[.@k];
			if (.@v <= 0)
				continue;
			if (.dk_pct[.@k]) {
				if (!.@ygg)
					continue;
				.@v = .@max * .@v / 100;
			}
			if (countitem(.dk_id[.@k], .@aid) < 1)
				continue;
			if (.@v > .@bigv) {
				.@big = .@k;
				.@bigv = .@v;
			}
			if (.@v >= .@need && (.@best < 0 || .@v < .@bv)) {
				.@best = .@k;
				.@bv = .@v;
			}
		}
		.@use = .@best;
		if (.@panic || .@use < 0)
			.@use = .@big;
		if (.@use < 0)
			continue;
		.@id = .dk_id[.@use];
		delitem .@id, 1, .@aid;
		consumeitem .@id, @cl_cid[.@i];
		specialeffect2 EF_POTION1, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
		setd "@cl_dn_" + .@aid, .@now + 1000;
	}
	return;

// Restock in town: each companion here gets its list (mod store, char scope,
// rs.c<char id>.<item id> = how many to keep) topped up, bought at the NPC shop
// price from the party purse, less the party's best Discount. getarg(0): one
// companion's char id, or 0 for all. Needs the app's Companion inventory (without
// it their potions and arrows are free anyway). Returns the zeny spent; the
// summary is in @rs_msg$ (and in your chat box).
L_Restock:
	@rs_msg$ = "";
	if (!getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_inventory"))
		return 0;
	.@only = getarg(0);
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	callsub L_BagParty;
	.@cn = @clb_n;
	copyarray .@cc[0], @clb_cid[0], .@cn;
	copyarray .@cnm$[0], @clb_nm$[0], .@cn;
	.@disc = callsub(L_Discount, 37);
	.@dwho$ = @rs_who$;
	.@purse = modstore_get("char", "purse", 0);
	.@spent = 0;
	.@got$ = "";
	.@short$ = "";
	.@heavy$ = "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@cn; .@k++) {
		.@c = .@cc[.@k];
		if ((.@only && .@c != .@only) || strcharinfo(3, .@c) != .@map$)
			continue;
		.@aid = getcharid(3, .@cnm$[.@k]);
		if (.@aid <= 0)
			continue;
		.@n = modstore_keys("char", "rs.c" + .@c, .@ids$);
		.@mine$ = "";
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			.@id = atoi(.@ids$[.@j]);
			.@want = modstore_get("char", "rs.c" + .@c + "." + .@id, 0);
			.@need = .@want - countitem(.@id, .@aid);
			if (.@need <= 0)
				continue;
			.@price = max(1, getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_BUY) * (100 - .@disc) / 100);
			.@buy = min(.@need, .@purse / .@price);
			.@iw = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_WEIGHT);
			.@room = .@buy;
			if (.@iw > 0)
				.@room = (readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) - readparam(Weight, .@c)) / .@iw;
			if (.@room < .@buy) {
				.@buy = max(0, .@room);
				if (!compare(.@heavy$ + ",", ", " + .@cnm$[.@k] + ","))
					.@heavy$ += ", " + .@cnm$[.@k];
			}
			if (.@buy > 0) {
				getitem .@id, .@buy, .@aid;
				.@purse -= .@buy * .@price;
				.@spent += .@buy * .@price;
				.@mine$ += (.@mine$ == "" ? "" : ", ") + .@buy + " " + getitemname(.@id);
			}
			if (.@buy < .@need && .@room >= .@need)
				.@short$ += ", " + (.@need - .@buy) + " " + getitemname(.@id);
		}
		if (.@mine$ != "")
			.@got$ += (.@got$ == "" ? "" : "; ") + .@cnm$[.@k] + ": " + .@mine$;
	}
	if (.@spent > 0)
		modstore_inc("char", "purse", -.@spent);
	if (.@got$ != "") {
		@rs_msg$ = "Restocked " + .@got$ + " for " + callfunc("F_InsertComma", .@spent) + " z";
		if (.@disc > 0)
			@rs_msg$ += " (" + .@dwho$ + "'s Discount -" + .@disc + "%)";
		@rs_msg$ += ".";
	}
	if (.@short$ != "")
		@rs_msg$ += (@rs_msg$ == "" ? "" : " ") + "Not enough in the purse for" + substr(.@short$, 1, getstrlen(.@short$) - 1) + ".";
	if (.@heavy$ != "")
		@rs_msg$ += (@rs_msg$ == "" ? "" : " ") + "Too heavy to carry it all:" + substr(.@heavy$, 1, getstrlen(.@heavy$) - 1) + ".";
	if (@rs_msg$ != "")
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + @rs_msg$, 0xFFCC66;
	// A word from the one who bargained, else anyone. 3.10.1: getarg(1) 1 = no word
	// (a shopping trip: they say theirs when they're back).
	if (.@got$ != "" && .chatter && !getarg(1, 0)) {
		.@a = -1;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_nm$[.@i] == .@dwho$)
				.@a = .@i;
		}
		if (.@a < 0)
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "restock", "", ET_OK, .@spent, "";
	}
	return .@spent;

// The party's best Discount (skill getarg(0) = 37) or Overcharge (38) in percent:
// yours, or that of a Merchant-line companion in your party (its own skill level,
// read by attaching to it for a moment). @rs_who$ = whose it is.
L_Discount:
	.@sk = getarg(0);
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@best = getskilllv(.@sk);
	@rs_who$ = "";
	if (.@best > 0)
		@rs_who$ = strcharinfo(0);
	callsub L_BagParty;
	.@cn = @clb_n;
	copyarray .@cc[0], @clb_cid[0], .@cn;
	copyarray .@cnm$[0], @clb_nm$[0], .@cn;
	.@who$ = @rs_who$;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@cn; .@k++) {
		if ((eaclass(readparam(Class, .@cc[.@k])) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_MERCHANT)
			continue;
		.@aid = getcharid(3, .@cnm$[.@k]);
		if (.@aid <= 0 || !attachrid(.@aid))
			continue;
		.@lv = getskilllv(.@sk);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@lv > .@best) {
			.@best = .@lv;
			.@who$ = .@cnm$[.@k];
		}
	}
	@rs_who$ = .@who$;
	if (.@best <= 0)
		return 0;
	if (.@best >= 10)
		return 24;
	return 5 + 2 * .@best;	// 7% at level 1, +2% a level, 24% at 10

// The names of your companions set to roam (@cl_rm$), from the engine's table;
// none while the app's Companion roaming setting is off (they follow then).
// 3.10.2: the same read gives each one's rest marks (the Battle tab's "Rest below /
// until"; @cl_rsn$ names, @cl_rsb below, @cl_rsu until) for the resting icon.
L_RoamRead:
	deletearray @cl_rm$;
	deletearray @cl_rsn$;
	deletearray @cl_rsb;
	deletearray @cl_rsu;
	.@n = query_sql("SELECT name, roam, rest_below, rest_until FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0) + " AND active = 1",
		@cl_rsn$, .@rm, @cl_rsb, @cl_rsu);
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") > 0) {
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
			if (.@rm[.@k])
				@cl_rm$[getarraysize(@cl_rm$)] = @cl_rsn$[.@k];
		}
	}
	return;

// Starter kits: each of your companions drafted with the app's "Companions
// start unequipped" (the engine's row says so), here and standing, that
// hasn't had its chance yet (mod store, char scope, kit.c<char id>).
L_KitCheck:
	.@n = query_sql("SELECT name FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0) + " AND start_unequipped = 1", .@nm$);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nm$[.@k]);
		if (.@ix < 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_hp[.@ix] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (modstore_get("char", "kit.c" + @cl_cid[.@ix], 0))
			continue;
		callsub L_Kit, .@ix;
	}
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) (index) gets the kit for its class and level, once:
// only if it wears nothing yet (you may have dressed it first). Each piece
// goes into its bag and on, as a trade does; a companion that started
// unequipped counts all it wears as yours (the engine), so the Gear tab's
// Take back and our Bag tab both hand it to you, and nothing grows back.
L_Kit:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	modstore_set "char", "kit.c" + .@c, 1;	// its one chance, whatever happens next
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES, EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@ei); .@s++) {
		if (getequipid(.@ei[.@s], .@c) > 0)
			return;
	}
	.@lv = readparam(BaseLevel, .@c);
	.@t = 0;
	if (.@lv >= 70)
		.@t = 3;
	else if (.@lv >= 40)
		.@t = 2;
	else if (.@lv >= 20)
		.@t = 1;
	// The base class: transcendent and baby folded in, a Peco Knight is a Knight.
	.@job = roclass(eaclass(readparam(Class, .@c)) & ~(EAJL_UPPER | EAJL_BABY), readparam(Sex, .@c));
	.@v$ = ".kit_" + .@job + "_" + .@t;
	.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
	if (!.@n)
		return;
	.@got$ = "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@id = getd(.@v$ + "[" + .@k + "]");
		if (getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_EQUIPLEVELMIN) > .@lv)
			continue;	// not yet (a low companion skips the odd piece)
		getitem .@id, 1, .@aid;
		equip .@id, .@c;
		.@got$ += (.@got$ == "" ? "" : ", ") + getitemname(.@id);
	}
	.@am = getd(".kitammo_" + .@job + "_" + .@t);
	if (.@am > 0) {
		getitem .@am, 500, .@aid;
		equip .@am, .@c;
		.@got$ += ", 500 " + getitemname(.@am);
	}
	// Our record of what it wears from you, so the Bag tab shows it and takes
	// it back (as gear dropped on it there).
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	getinventorylist .@c;
	.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
