		.@eq = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c);
		if (!.@eq || .@eq == EQP_AMMO)
			continue;
		.@rec$ += (.@rec$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@eq + "," + getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c) + ",0";
	}
	if (.@rec$ != "")
		modstore_set "char", "dg.c" + .@c, .@rec$;
	if (.@got$ != "")
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " got a starter kit: " + .@got$ + ".", 0xAADDFF;
	return;

// A gift from companion getarg(0) (index) to you (attached), getarg(1) the moment:
// "" a quiet one, "mvp", "lvl" or "ann". At most one gift every 4 minutes, and
// from the same companion every 30. 1 when it gave something.
L_Gift:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@k$ = getarg(1);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	if (!.gifts || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !unitexists(.@aid))
		return 0;
	if (.@sec < @cl_gall || .@sec < getd("@cl_gcd_" + .@aid))
		return 0;
	.@ar = @cl_ar[.@i];
	// Rare and very rare, in tenths of a percent; better at the big moments.
	.@rr = 120;
	.@vr = 5;
	if (.@k$ == "mvp") {
		.@rr = 300;
		.@vr = 20;
	} else if (.@k$ != "") {
		.@rr = 200;
		.@vr = 10;
	}
	// Bonds (3.7.0): from Trusted on, half again the chance of a rare gift, and twice
	// the very rare.
	if (.mem) {
		if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@i])) >= 3) {
			.@rr = .@rr * 3 / 2;
			.@vr = .@vr * 2;
		}
	}
	.@roll = rand(1000);
	if (.@roll < .@vr) {
		.@e$ = "616:1:1";	// Old Card Album
		.@cat$ = "giftv";
	} else {
		.@tb$ = getd(".gc_" + .@ar + "$");
		.@cat$ = "giftc";
		if (.@roll < .@vr + .@rr) {
			.@tb$ = getd(".gr_" + .@ar + "$");
			.@cat$ = "giftr";
		}
		if (.@tb$ == "")
			return 0;
		explode(.@ent$, .@tb$, ",");
		.@e$ = .@ent$[rand(getarraysize(.@ent$))];
	}
	explode(.@p$, .@e$, ":");
	.@id = atoi(.@p$[0]);
	.@lo = atoi(.@p$[1]);
	.@hi = atoi(.@p$[2]);
	.@n = .@lo;
	if (.@hi > .@lo)
		.@n = rand(.@lo, .@hi);
	if (.@id == 0) {
		if (Zeny + .@n > MAX_ZENY)
			return 0;
		Zeny += .@n;
		.@what$ = .@n + " zeny";
	} else {
		if (!checkweight(.@id, .@n))
			return 0;	// you can't carry it: no gift this time
		getitem .@id, .@n;
		.@what$ = getitemname(.@id);
		if (.@n > 1)
			.@what$ = .@n + " " + .@what$;
	}
	setd "@cl_gcd_" + .@aid, .@sec + 1800;
	@cl_gall = .@sec + 240;
	callsub L_GiftWalk, .@i;
	// What it says: about the moment half the time, else about the gift.
	.@lc$ = .@cat$;
	if (.@k$ != "" && .@cat$ != "giftv" && rand(2))
		.@lc$ = "gift" + .@k$;
	.@t$ = replacestr(callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, .@lc$, .@ar), .@i, ""), "{i}", .@what$);
	.@em = ET_DELIGHT;
	if (.@cat$ == "giftv")
		.@em = ET_THROB;
	else if (.@id == 0)
		.@em = ET_MONEY;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = .@aid;
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = .@em;
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " gave you " + .@what$ + ".", 0xFFCC66;
	if (.mem)
		modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".gifts", 1);
	return 1;

// Companion getarg(0) walks over to stand next to you (on your side facing it),
// when it's on your map and within 12 cells.
L_GiftWalk:
	.@aid = @cl_aid[getarg(0)];
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@om$)
		return;
	.@dx = .@cx - .@ox;
	.@dy = .@cy - .@oy;
	if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) > 12 || max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) <= 1)
		return;
	.@gx = .@ox + (.@dx > 0) - (.@dx < 0);
	.@gy = .@oy + (.@dy > 0) - (.@dy < 0);
	if (!checkcell(.@om$, .@gx, .@gy, cell_chkpass))
		return;
	callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, .@om$, .@cx, .@cy, .@gx, .@gy, .@ox, .@oy, 4000;
	return;

// Whether companion getarg(0) (an index of the tick's @cl_ arrays) roams.
L_Roams:
	return (inarray(@cl_rm$, @cl_nm$[getarg(0)]) >= 0);

// 2.5.6: roaming companions don't sit down to rest. The engine rests a companion
// before its strategy rules get a turn (population_engine.cpp: pop_shell_rest), so a
// rule can't keep it up; the only switch is "@companion rest <below> <until>", which
// is for the whole party (0 = never). While any of yours roams, the thresholds are
// saved (char var cl_rest_saved$, the most common pair among your companions) and set
// to never; once none roams, they're put back. Survives a server restart.
L_RoamRest:
	if (!.roamhelp)
		return;
	.@cid = getcharid(0);
	query_sql "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@cid + " AND active = 1 AND roam = 1", .@r;
	if (.@r[0] > 0) {
		if (cl_rest_saved$ != "")
			return;
		query_sql "SELECT rest_below, rest_until, COUNT(*) AS c FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@cid
			+ " AND active = 1 GROUP BY rest_below, rest_until ORDER BY c DESC LIMIT 1", .@b, .@u, .@cnt;
		if (.@b[0] <= 0)
			return;	// already never resting: nothing to give back later
		cl_rest_saved$ = .@b[0] + "," + .@u[0];
		atcommand "@companion rest 0 " + .@u[0];
		return;
	}
	if (cl_rest_saved$ == "")
		return;
	explode(.@p$, cl_rest_saved$, ",");
	cl_rest_saved$ = "";
	atcommand "@companion rest " + .@p$[0] + " " + .@p$[1];
	return;

// The highest outfit colour offered for a class. Built on the engine's own
// table (population_engine.cpp get_job_max_cloth_color, counted from kRO and
// iRO data, which re-rolls any colour above it at every summon), one lower:
// in play (2026-10-12) the top colour of every 0-4 class came out black and
// green. Knights and Crusaders always ride (the engine mounts them), and a
// dye tints the Peco too, which looked wrong: they keep the default.
L_ClothMax:
	.@j = getarg(0);
	setarray .@ride, Job_Knight, Job_Knight2, Job_Crusader, Job_Crusader2, Job_Lord_Knight, Job_Lord_Knight2,
		Job_Paladin, Job_Paladin2, Job_Baby_Knight, Job_Baby_Knight2, Job_Baby_Crusader, Job_Baby_Crusader2;
	if (inarray(.@ride, .@j) >= 0)
		return getbattleflag("min_cloth_color");
	setarray .@p3, Job_Assassin, Job_Crusader, Job_Crusader2, Job_Monk, Job_Sage, Job_Rogue, Job_Dancer,
		Job_Lord_Knight, Job_Lord_Knight2, Job_High_Priest, Job_High_Wizard, Job_Whitesmith, Job_Sniper,
		Job_Assassin_Cross, Job_Paladin, Job_Paladin2, Job_Champion, Job_Professor, Job_Stalker, Job_Creator,
		Job_Clown, Job_Gypsy, Job_Baby_Assassin, Job_Baby_Crusader, Job_Baby_Crusader2, Job_Baby_Monk,
		Job_Baby_Sage, Job_Baby_Rogue;
	setarray .@p4, Job_Swordman, Job_Mage, Job_Archer, Job_Acolyte, Job_Merchant, Job_Thief,
		Job_Knight, Job_Knight2, Job_Priest, Job_Wizard, Job_Blacksmith, Job_Hunter, Job_Alchemist, Job_Bard,
		Job_Swordman_High, Job_Mage_High, Job_Archer_High, Job_Acolyte_High, Job_Merchant_High, Job_Thief_High,
		Job_Baby_Swordman, Job_Baby_Mage, Job_Baby_Archer, Job_Baby_Acolyte, Job_Baby_Merchant, Job_Baby_Thief,
		Job_Baby_Knight, Job_Baby_Knight2, Job_Baby_Priest, Job_Baby_Wizard, Job_Baby_Blacksmith,
		Job_Baby_Hunter, Job_Baby_Alchemist, Job_Baby_Bard, Job_Baby_Dancer;
	.@max = getbattleflag("max_cloth_color");
	if (inarray(.@p3, .@j) >= 0)
		return min(3, .@max);
	if (inarray(.@p4, .@j) >= 0)
		return min(3, .@max);
	return .@max;

// Colours you picked (Bag tab), put back on a companion the engine summoned
// with its old ones (at login, after a map change, after a revive).
L_Dyes:
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
		.@p$ = "dye.c" + .@c;
		if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".cl")) {
			.@v = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".cl", 0);
			// A colour no longer offered (1.4.4), or a job change: the top one left.
			.@mx = callsub(L_ClothMax, readparam(Class, .@c));
			if (.@v > .@mx) {
				.@v = .@mx;
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".cl", .@v;
			}
			if (getlook(LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@c) != .@v)
				setlook LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
		} else if (getlook(LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@c) > callsub(L_ClothMax, readparam(Class, .@c))) {
			// Never dyed, but the engine rolled one of the colours that look broken
			// (or tint a Peco): the default instead, kept like a dye.
			.@v = getbattleflag("min_cloth_color");
			setlook LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
			modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".cl", .@v;
		}
		if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".hc")) {
			.@v = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".hc", 0);
			if (getlook(LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@c) != .@v)
				setlook LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
		}
	}
	return;

// A stack in companion getarg(0)'s bag, at getarg(1) in its @inventorylist_*
// (read just before), getarg(2) of it to you: refine, cards, random options
// and bound type come along.
L_ToYou:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@j = getarg(1);
	.@amt = getarg(2);
	.@id = getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c);
	.@ident = getvar(@inventorylist_identify[.@j], .@c);
	.@ref = getvar(@inventorylist_refine[.@j], .@c);
	.@attr = getvar(@inventorylist_attribute[.@j], .@c);
	.@c1 = getvar(@inventorylist_card1[.@j], .@c);
	.@c2 = getvar(@inventorylist_card2[.@j], .@c);
	.@c3 = getvar(@inventorylist_card3[.@j], .@c);
	.@c4 = getvar(@inventorylist_card4[.@j], .@c);
	.@bound = getvar(@inventorylist_bound[.@j], .@c);
	setarray .@oid, getvar(@inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], .@c),
		getvar(@inventorylist_option_id3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id5[.@j], .@c);
	setarray .@oval, getvar(@inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], .@c),
		getvar(@inventorylist_option_value3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value5[.@j], .@c);
	setarray .@opar, getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@j], .@c),
		getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@j], .@c);
	delitemidx getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@c), .@amt, .@c;
	if (.@bound)
		getitembound3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@bound, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
	else
		getitem3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
	return;

// Our record of gear dropped on companion getarg(0) (test build 2.3.0, mod
// store dg.c<char id>: "<slots>,<item id>,<its own item id>" split by ";").
// Entries for gear it no longer wears are dropped here (the engine changed
// it, or it was taken back some other way).
// L_DgMask: the slots in it. L_DgOwn: ",id,id," of its own pieces in its bag.
L_DgMask:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	if (.@rec$ == "")
		return 0;
	setarray .@eb, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_SHOES,
		EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES,
		EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQI_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	explode(.@e$, .@rec$, ";");
	.@mask = 0;
	.@keep$ = "";
	for (.@r = 0; .@r < getarraysize(.@e$); .@r++) {
		if (.@e$[.@r] == "")
			continue;
		explode(.@f$, .@e$[.@r], ",");
		.@m = atoi(.@f$[0]);
		.@ok = 0;
		for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++) {
			if (.@m & .@eb[.@s]) {
				.@ok = getequipid(.@ei[.@s], .@c) == atoi(.@f$[1]);
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!.@ok)
			continue;
		.@mask = .@mask | .@m;
		.@keep$ += (.@keep$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@e$[.@r];
	}
	if (.@keep$ != .@rec$)
		callsub L_DgSave, .@c, .@keep$;
	return .@mask;

// Gear you dropped on companion getarg(0) (name getarg(1)) in slots getarg(2),
// from our record: off, back to you, and its own piece back on. Returns
// "OK|text", "ERR|text", or "" when no such gear is in the record.
L_DgBack:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@nm$ = getarg(1);
	.@eq = getarg(2);
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	if (.@rec$ == "")
		return "";
	explode(.@e$, .@rec$, ";");
	.@n = getarraysize(.@e$);
	for (.@r = 0; .@r < .@n; .@r++) {
		explode(.@f$, .@e$[.@r], ",");
		if (atoi(.@f$[0]) & .@eq)
			break;
	}
	if (.@r >= .@n)
		return "";
	.@m = atoi(.@f$[0]);
	.@id = atoi(.@f$[1]);
	.@own = atoi(.@f$[2]);
	// The record without this entry, kept whatever happens next.
	.@keep$ = "";
	for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@n; .@q++) {
		if (.@q != .@r && .@e$[.@q] != "")
			.@keep$ += (.@keep$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@e$[.@q];
	}
	getinventorylist .@c;
	.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c) == .@id && (getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c) & .@m))
			break;
	}
	if (.@j >= .@cnt) {
		callsub L_DgSave, .@c, .@keep$;
		return "ERR|" + .@nm$ + " isn't wearing that any more.";
	}
	if (!checkweight(.@id, 1))
		return "ERR|You can't carry the " + getitemname(.@id) + " it wears there.";
	setarray .@eb, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_SHOES,
		EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES,
		EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQI_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++) {
		if (.@m & .@eb[.@s]) {
			unequip .@ei[.@s], .@c;
			break;
		}
	}
	// Off now, still at the same index in its bag.
	callsub L_ToYou, .@c, .@j, 1;
	callsub L_DgSave, .@c, .@keep$;
	.@msg$ = "Took back the " + getitemname(.@id) + " from " + .@nm$ + ".";
	// (Two ifs: && reads both sides, and countitem(0) is an error.)
	if (.@own > 0) {
		if (countitem(.@own, getcharid(3, .@nm$))) {
			equip .@own, .@c;
			.@msg$ += " It put its " + getitemname(.@own) + " back on.";
		}
	}
	return "OK|" + .@msg$;

// Save the record getarg(1) for companion getarg(0); an empty one is removed.
L_DgSave:
	if (getarg(1) == "")
		modstore_delete "char", "dg.c" + getarg(0);
	else
		modstore_set "char", "dg.c" + getarg(0), getarg(1);
	return;

L_DgOwn:
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + getarg(0), "");
	.@out$ = ",";
	if (.@rec$ == "")
		return .@out$;
	explode(.@e$, .@rec$, ";");
	for (.@r = 0; .@r < getarraysize(.@e$); .@r++) {
		explode(.@f$, .@e$[.@r], ",");
		if (atoi(.@f$[2]) > 0)
			.@out$ += .@f$[2] + ",";
	}
	return .@out$;

// 3.8.1: puts gear you gave on companion getarg(0) (name getarg(1)): item
// getarg(2), into slot getarg(3) (0: an accessory with a side free), and keeps
// the record of it (dg.c<char id>) for Take back. getarg(4) its account id,
// getarg(5) the extra words, getarg(6) the reply so far. Returns the reply.
// (Was inline in the give; the give runs it at once, L_WearLater once the
// engine has taken back what was traded for that slot.)
L_WearNow:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@nm$ = getarg(1);
	.@id = getarg(2);
	.@t = getarg(3);
	.@aid = getarg(4);
	.@extra$ = getarg(5);
	.@msg$ = getarg(6);
	.@loc = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_LOCATIONS);
	setarray .@eb, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_SHOES,
		EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES,
		EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQI_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++)
		.@old[.@s] = (.@t & .@eb[.@s]) ? getequipid(.@ei[.@s], .@c) : 0;
	// rAthena puts an accessory on the right unless only the left is free,
	// so the chosen side is emptied first (its piece is in .@old).
	if (.@t == EQP_ACC_R && getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) > 0)
		unequip EQI_ACC_R, .@c;
	else if (.@t == EQP_ACC_L && getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) > 0)
		unequip EQI_ACC_L, .@c;
	// (This server has no equip2: by item id, so the first of it in its bag.)
	// 3.7.1: two of the same accessory (a player's report: 2 Skull Rings).
	// When the first copy in the bag is the one already worn, "equip" takes
	// that one and the new copy never goes on. So that worn copy leaves the bag
	// for a moment: the new copy goes on alone, then the old one comes back
	// (into the bag slot it left, before the new copy) and goes on the other
	// side. The two may swap sides; same item, so the record below keeps the
	// side you chose. 3.8.1: traded gear too (the engine takes traded gear back
	// by its slot, whatever piece is in it).
	.@swap = 0;
	.@free = 0;
	if (!.@t && .@loc == EQP_ACC_RL)
		.@free = getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) <= 0 ? EQP_ACC_R : EQP_ACC_L;
	.@first = -1;
	.@u = -1;
	if (.@loc == EQP_ACC_RL) {
		getinventorylist .@c;
		.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
			if (getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@i], .@c) != .@id)
				continue;
			if (.@first < 0)
				.@first = .@i;
			if (!getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@i], .@c) && .@u < 0)
				.@u = .@i;
		}
	}
	// 3.8.5: rAthena's && reads both sides, so @inventorylist_equip[-1] (any
	// non-accessory: .@first is -1) ended the whole give with no answer.
	.@bworn = 0;
	if (.@first >= 0)
		.@bworn = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@first], .@c);
	if (.@first >= 0 && .@u >= 0 && .@first != .@u && (.@bworn & EQP_ACC_RL)) {
		.@b = .@first;
		.@bidx = getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@b], .@c);
		.@bi = getvar(@inventorylist_identify[.@b], .@c);
		.@br = getvar(@inventorylist_refine[.@b], .@c);
		.@ba = getvar(@inventorylist_attribute[.@b], .@c);
		.@b1 = getvar(@inventorylist_card1[.@b], .@c);
		.@b2 = getvar(@inventorylist_card2[.@b], .@c);
		.@b3 = getvar(@inventorylist_card3[.@b], .@c);
		.@b4 = getvar(@inventorylist_card4[.@b], .@c);
		.@bb = getvar(@inventorylist_bound[.@b], .@c);
		setarray .@boid, getvar(@inventorylist_option_id1[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id2[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id3[.@b], .@c),
			getvar(@inventorylist_option_id4[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id5[.@b], .@c);
		setarray .@boval, getvar(@inventorylist_option_value1[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value2[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value3[.@b], .@c),
			getvar(@inventorylist_option_value4[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value5[.@b], .@c);
		setarray .@bopar, getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@b], .@c),
			getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@b], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@b], .@c);
		delitemidx .@bidx, 1, .@c;
		equip .@id, .@c;
		if (.@bb)
			getitembound3 .@id, 1, .@bi, .@br, .@ba, .@b1, .@b2, .@b3, .@b4, .@bb, .@boid, .@boval, .@bopar, .@aid;
		else
			getitem3 .@id, 1, .@bi, .@br, .@ba, .@b1, .@b2, .@b3, .@b4, .@boid, .@boval, .@bopar, .@aid;
		equip .@id, .@c;
		.@swap = 1;
	} else if (.@t == EQP_HAND_L)
		equip .@id, .@c, EQP_HAND_L;	// 3.9.0: dual wield (an unworn copy, so a second of the same dagger works)
	else
		equip .@id, .@c;
	// Where it went (an accessory picks a side, a two-hander takes both hands).
	.@m = 0;
	getinventorylist .@c;
	.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@i], .@c) == .@id && (getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@i], .@c) & .@loc)) {
			.@m = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@i], .@c);
			break;
		}
	}
	// 3.7.1: both sides now wear this item: the record is for the side you
	// chose (or the one that was free).
	if (.@swap && getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) == .@id && getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) == .@id)
		.@m = .@t ? .@t : .@free;
	// 3.9.0: the left hand. With the same dagger in both hands, the loop above
	// found the right-hand copy first.
	if (.@t == EQP_HAND_L) {
		.@m = 0;
		if (getequipid(EQI_HAND_L, .@c) == .@id)
			.@m = EQP_HAND_L;
	}
	if (!.@m)
		return .@msg$ + " " + .@nm$ + " can't wear it, so it's in the bag." + .@extra$;
	.@own = 0;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++) {
		if ((.@m & .@eb[.@s]) && .@old[.@s] > 0) {
			.@own = .@old[.@s];
			break;
		}
	}
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	modstore_set "char", "dg.c" + .@c, .@rec$ + (.@rec$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@m + "," + .@id + "," + .@own;
	return .@nm$ + " put on the " + getitemname(.@id) + "." + .@extra$;

// 3.8.1: gear waiting for the engine (the give's .@later): "@companion gear"
// takes traded gear back a moment later, by its slot. Once the engine's record
// (given_mask) no longer has that slot, the new piece goes on (L_WearNow);
// after 10 s without that, it stays in the companion's bag. @cl_pwl: the
// companions waiting; @cl_pw_/pwt_/pwx_<char id>, @cl_pwn_<char id>$: item, slot, deadline, name.
L_WearLater:
	.@now = gettimetick(0);
	.@n = getarraysize(@cl_pwl);
	.@left = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@c = @cl_pwl[.@k];
		.@id = getd("@cl_pw_" + .@c);
		.@t = getd("@cl_pwt_" + .@c);
		.@nm$ = getd("@cl_pwn_" + .@c + "$");
		if (.@id <= 0)
			continue;
		.@gm = 0;
		if (query_sql("SELECT given_mask FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0)
				+ " AND name = '" + escape_sql(.@nm$) + "' LIMIT 1", .@gmv) > 0)
			.@gm = .@gmv;
		.@aid = getcharid(3, .@nm$);
		.@done = 0;
		if (!(.@gm & .@t) && .@aid > 0) {
			.@done = 1;
			if (countitem(.@id, .@aid) > 0)
				dispbottom "[Companions] " + callsub(L_WearNow, .@c, .@nm$, .@id, .@t, .@aid, "", ""), 0xFFCC66;
		} else if (.@now >= getd("@cl_pwx_" + .@c)) {
			.@done = 1;
			dispbottom "[Companions] " + .@nm$ + " couldn't put on the " + getitemname(.@id) + ": it's in the bag. Try again in a moment.", 0xFFCC66;
		}
		if (.@done) {
			setd "@cl_pw_" + .@c, 0;
			continue;
		}
		.@keep[.@left] = .@c;
		.@left++;
	}
	deletearray @cl_pwl;
	if (.@left)
		copyarray @cl_pwl[0], .@keep[0], .@left;
	return;

// "@@reply <request> <part>/<parts> <text>", 180 characters a line.
L_BagReply:
	.@rt$ = getarg(1);
	.@rl = getstrlen(.@rt$);
	.@rn = max(1, (.@rl + 179) / 180);
	for (.@rk = 0; .@rk < .@rn; .@rk++) {
		.@part$ = "";
		if (.@rl > 0)
			.@part$ = substr(.@rt$, .@rk * 180, min(.@rl, (.@rk + 1) * 180) - 1);
		dispbottom "@@reply " + getarg(0) + " " + (.@rk + 1) + "/" + .@rn + " " + .@part$;
	}
	return;

// The window's separators out of a text.
L_Clean:
	return replacestr(replacestr(getarg(0), "|", "/"), "~", "-");

// Memories: a companion seen for the first time is "met" here and now; one
// already known may have an anniversary today.
L_MemSeen:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
	if (!modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".met")) {
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".met", gettimetick(2);
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".metmap", strcharinfo(3);
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".metlv", BaseLevel;
		return;
	}
	.@days = (gettimetick(2) - modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", gettimetick(2))) / 86400;
	.@ann = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".ann", 0);
	setarray .@ms[0], 730, 365, 182, 100, 30, 7;
	// 3.0.1: said in words ("A month together already"), not a count of days.
	setarray .@mw$[0], "Two years", "A whole year", "Half a year", "A hundred days", "A month", "A week";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
		if (.@days >= .@ms[.@k]) {
			if (.@ms[.@k] > .@ann && @cl_hp[.@i] > 0) {
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".ann", .@ms[.@k];
				callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "anniv", "", ET_CONGRATULATION, .@days, .@mw$[.@k];
				if (rand(100) < 50)
					callsub L_Gift, .@i, "ann";
			}
			break;
		}
	}
	return;

// Memories: a place visited together for the first time. Instance maps
// (1@tower and the like) don't fit a store key and aren't counted.
L_MemMap:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	if (compare(.@m$, "@") || compare(.@m$, "-") || compare(.@m$, "."))
		return;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".maps";
		if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + "." + .@m$))
			continue;
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + "." + .@m$, 1;	// counted for the Memories tab only (3.0.1)
	}
	return;

// A quiet thought out loud (3.0.1, replaces reciting stats): a "did you know" about
// where you are (or anywhere), a small shared moment, or now and then an easter egg.
// The last few of each aren't repeated (L_PickConv's list, @cl_rec$). 1 if one was said.
L_Thought:
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	if (.@a < 0)
		return 0;
	.@ar = @cl_ar[.@a];
	.@r = rand(100);
	.@lore = 0;
	// 3.9.1: a tale about the world a quarter of the time (was half), told as a story
	// someone heard; the small moments of their own life the rest.
	if (.@r < 5) {
		.@t$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "segg");
	} else if (.@r < 30) {
		.@t$ = callsub(L_PickLore, callsub(L_LoreRegion, strcharinfo(3)));
		if (.@t$ != "") {
			.@t$ = callsub(L_Line, "dyk", .@ar) + .@t$;
			.@lore = 1;
		}
	} else {
		// {m}: the town where you met, by name; lines that need it are passed over
		// when you met in a field or dungeon.
		.@m$ = callsub(L_TownName, modstore_get("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@a] + ".metmap", ""));
		.@t$ = "";
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
			.@t$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "mom_" + .@ar);
			if (.@m$ != "" || !compare(.@t$, "{m}"))
				break;
		}
		if (.@m$ == "" && compare(.@t$, "{m}"))
			return 0;
		.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{m}", .@m$);
	}
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return 0;
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, .@t$, .@a, "");
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@a];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@a];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = 0;
	// 3.9.1: a tale gets an answer from a friend, more often than not.
	if (.@lore && rand(100) < 65) {
		.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
		if (.@b >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, "lorere", @cl_nm$[.@a], 0;
	}
	return 1;

// A "did you know" fact for region getarg(0): one about that region 60% of the time
// (when there is one), else one that fits anywhere. The last 20 aren't repeated.
L_PickLore:
	.@rg$ = getarg(0);
	.@n = getarraysize(.lore$);
	.@want$ = "any";
	if (.@rg$ != "" && rand(100) < 60)
		.@want$ = .@rg$;
	.@k = 0;
	for (.@pass = 0; .@pass < 3 && !.@k; .@pass++) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@n; .@i++) {
			if (!compare(.lore$[.@i], .@want$ + "|") || inarray(@cl_lore, .@i) >= 0)
				continue;
			.@c[.@k] = .@i;
			.@k++;
		}
		if (!.@k) {
			if (.@want$ != "any")
				.@want$ = "any";	// nothing left for here: anywhere
			else
				deletearray @cl_lore;	// heard them all: start over
		}
	}
	if (!.@k)
		return "";
	.@i = .@c[0];
	if (.@k > 1)
		.@i = .@c[rand(.@k)];
	if (getarraysize(@cl_lore) >= 20)
		deletearray @cl_lore[0], 1;
	@cl_lore[getarraysize(@cl_lore)] = .@i;
	explode(.@part$, .lore$[.@i], "|");
	return .@part$[1];

// The lore region of map getarg(0): a code .lore$ uses, or "".
L_LoreRegion:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	setarray .@pf$, "prt_maze", "prt_sewb", "prontera", "prt_", "izlude", "iz_", "geffen", "gef_", "orcsdun", "payon", "pay_", "morocc", "moc_", "in_sphinx", "anthell",
		"alberta", "alb", "treasure", "tur_dun", "aldebaran", "alde_", "c_tower", "mjolnir_", "mjo_", "yuno", "juperos", "jupe_", "comodo", "cmd_", "beach_dun", "umbala", "um_",
		"xmas", "niflheim", "nif_", "glast_01", "gl_", "einbroch", "einbech", "ein_", "lighthalzen", "lhz_", "hugel", "hu_", "odin_tem", "rachel", "ra_", "amatsu", "ama_";
	setarray .@rg$, "prt", "prt", "prt", "prt", "izl", "izl", "gef", "gef", "gef", "pay", "pay", "moc", "moc", "moc", "moc",
		"alb", "alb", "alb", "alb", "alde", "alde", "alde", "mjo", "mjo", "yuno", "yuno", "yuno", "cmd", "cmd", "cmd", "um", "um",
		"xmas", "nif", "nif", "gl", "gl", "ein", "ein", "ein", "lhz", "lhz", "hu", "hu", "hu", "ra", "ra", "ama", "ama";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@pf$); .@i++) {
		if (compare(.@m$, .@pf$[.@i]))
			return .@rg$[.@i];
	}
	return "";

// A town's name for a map name, or "" if it isn't one of the towns.
L_TownName:
	setarray .@tm$, "prontera", "izlude", "geffen", "payon", "morocc", "alberta", "aldebaran", "yuno", "comodo", "umbala", "amatsu", "gonryun", "louyang", "ayothaya", "xmas", "niflheim", "einbroch", "einbech", "lighthalzen", "hugel", "rachel", "veins", "moscovia", "pay_arche";
	setarray .@tn$, "Prontera", "Izlude", "Geffen", "Payon", "Morroc", "Alberta", "Al De Baran", "Juno", "Comodo", "Umbala", "Amatsu", "Kunlun", "Luoyang", "Ayothaya", "Lutie", "Niflheim", "Einbroch", "Einbech", "Lighthalzen", "Hugel", "Rachel", "Veins", "Moscovia", "Archer Village";
	.@i = inarray(.@tm$, getarg(0));
	if (.@i < 0)
		return "";
	return .@tn$[.@i];

// Like L_QueueIdx, with {n} = getarg(4) and {m} = getarg(5) filled in first.
L_QueueFmt:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Line, getarg(1), @cl_ar[.@i]);
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	// 3.9.0: a pet with a name of its own is "Mochi", not "a Mochi" or "the Mochi".
	// (Nested: && reads both sides, and substr on a short key errors.)
	if (getstrlen(getarg(1)) > 3 && getarg(5) != "") {
		if (substr(getarg(1), 0, 2) == "pet" && inarray(.pet_nm$, getarg(5)) < 0) {
			setarray .@art$, "A {m}", "a {m}", "The {m}", "the {m}", "This {m}", "this {m}", "That {m}", "that {m}";
			for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8; .@k++)
				.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, .@art$[.@k], "{m}");
		}
	}
	.@t$ = replacestr(replacestr(.@t$, "{n}", "" + getarg(4)), "{m}", getarg(5));
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, .@t$, .@i, getarg(2));
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@i];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = getarg(3);
	return;

// ---- Pets (3.2.0) -------------------------------------------------------------
// Per companion (account id): @cl_pg_ the pet's id, @cl_pp_ which pet (.pet_*),
// @cl_qpl_ the level its strength was set for, @cl_qpw_ no new try before (s),
// @cl_pe_ its next worried emote, @cl_pdd_ its companion fell. @cl_po_<char id>:
// allowed, read once from the store (pet.c<char id>), stored +1.

// Each companion here: a pet that's missing (gone with its companion on a warp, a
// teleport or a fall) comes back beside it; one sent home goes; a level-up makes it
// stronger. getarg(0) = now (s).
L_PetTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + .@aid);
		// (Nested: && reads both sides, and getunittype(0) warns.)
		if (.@g) {
			if (!unitexists(.@g))
				.@g = 0;
			else if (getunittype(.@g) != BL_MOB)
				.@g = 0;
		}
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0) {
			// 3.9.0: it falls with a pet out: the pet stays by it, crying (L_PetMourn).
			if (!getd("@cl_pdd_" + .@aid) && getd("@cl_pg_" + .@aid) && .emotes)
				callsub L_PetMournStart, .@i;
			setd "@cl_pdd_" + .@aid, 1;
		}
		// Out: kept and switched on, or come along to be asked about (@cl_pv_, L_PetAsk).
		.@out = callsub(L_PetOn, @cl_cid[.@i]);
		if (getd("@cl_pv_" + @cl_cid[.@i]))
			.@out = 1;
		if (!.@out || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0) {
			if (.@g)
				callsub L_PetHide, .@g;
			setd "@cl_pg_" + .@aid, 0;
			continue;
		}
		if (!.@g) {
			setd "@cl_pg_" + .@aid, 0;
			if (.@sec >= getd("@cl_qpw_" + .@aid))
				callsub L_PetSpawn, .@i;
			continue;
		}
		if (getd("@cl_qpl_" + .@aid) != @cl_lv[.@i]) {
			setd "@cl_qpl_" + .@aid, @cl_lv[.@i];
			callsub L_PetStats, .@g, @cl_lv[.@i];
		}
		// 3.9.0: left behind (the user's test: run ahead or teleport and the pet stood
		// where it was). rAthena only runs a monster's AI with a player near it, so a
		// pet out of sight stops following. Over 12 cells away, or on another map: it
		// catches up, next to its companion. (getmapxy can't find a monster, "Invalid
		// type 2": so the monsters within 12 cells of the companion, on its map.)
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) == 0) {
			deletearray .@near;
			.@nn = getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@cm$, .@cx - 12, .@cy - 12, .@cx + 12, .@cy + 12, .@near);
			if (inarray(.@near, .@g) < 0) {
				unitstopwalk .@g, USW_FORCE_STOP;	// a stuck play walk (L_PetLife) too
				unitwarp .@g, .@cm$, .@cx, .@cy;
			}
		}
		// 3.10.2: the play walk's forced-walk flag, cleared once it had its time.
		if (getd("@cl_ppf_" + .@aid) && .@sec >= getd("@cl_ppf_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_ppf_" + .@aid, 0;
			unitstopwalk .@g, USW_FORCE_STOP;
		}
		// Its companion in trouble.
		if (.emotes && @cl_incombat && @cl_mhp[.@i] > 0 && @cl_hp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_mhp[.@i] < 35 && .@sec >= getd("@cl_pe_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_pe_" + .@aid, .@sec + 30;
			emotion ET_SWEAT, .@g;
		}
	}
	// 3.9.0: the pets' ids to the window's client, which marks them friendly (app
	// 1.7.3, api.monsters.friendly): the plain cursor over them, and attack-nearest
	// leaves them alone. Sent when the list changes ("@@event clpets id|id|").
	.@all$ = "";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@i]);
		if (.@g)
			.@all$ += .@g + "|";
	}
	if (.@all$ != @cl_petids$) {
		@cl_petids$ = .@all$;
		dispbottom "@@event clpets " + .@all$;
	}
	return;

// Is companion getarg(0) (char id)'s pet out: kept (pk.c, 3.9.0: you said yes when it
// asked) and switched On in the Bag tab (pet.c)?
L_PetOn:
	.@v = getd("@cl_po_" + getarg(0));
	if (!.@v) {
		.@v = 1 + (callsub(L_PetKept, getarg(0)) && modstore_get("char", "pet.c" + getarg(0), 0) > 0);
		setd "@cl_po_" + getarg(0), .@v;
	}
	return .@v - 1;

// 3.9.0: did you let companion getarg(0) (char id) keep its pet?
L_PetKept:
	return (modstore_get("char", "pk.c" + getarg(0), 0) > 0);

// 3.9.0: what companion getarg(0) (char id) calls its pet: the name you gave it, or what
// it is ("Lunatic").
L_PetName:
	.@n$ = modstore_get("char", "pn.c" + getarg(0), "");
	if (.@n$ != "")
		return .@n$;
	return .pet_nm$[callsub(L_PetOf, readparam(Class, getarg(0)))];

// 3.9.0: a pet sent home or no longer needed leaves without falling over on screen: out
// of sight to a closed map first (the prison, nobody there), then gone.
L_PetHide:
	.@g = getarg(0);
	if (!.@g || !unitexists(.@g))
		return;
	unitwarp .@g, "sec_pri", -1, -1;
	unitkill .@g;
	return;

// Bag tab state of companion getarg(0) (char id)'s pet: 3 kept, 2 yours to allow
// ("Let them keep it": asked twice, or no bonds with Memories off), 1 it will ask
// soon (Companion bond), 0 not yet.
L_PetState:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	if (callsub(L_PetKept, .@c))
		return 3;
	if (!.mem || modstore_get("char", "pa.c" + .@c, 0) >= 2)
		return 2;
	if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, .@c)) >= 2)
		return 1;
	return 0;

// ---- Shopping trips (3.10.1, setting shopping 2 = Ask) ---------------------------
// Steps (@cl_shs): 1 back in town from the field or a dungeon (set on arrival), waiting
// up to 60 s for a calm moment (no fight, nobody talking, no other box up, you standing
// still 2 s); 2 one companion's portrait asks ("@@event clshop", client/index.js), the
// answer comes back as "@clshop yes|no". No calm moment or no answer in 2 minutes:
// they restock quietly, as Silent does. The trip itself (char vars, so a relog or a
// restart ends it cleanly): cl_shop$ the names out shopping ("A|B|"), cl_shopmap$ the
// town, cl_shopend when time's up.
L_ShopTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	if (cl_shop$ != "") {
		if (strcharinfo(3) != cl_shopmap$)
			callsub L_ShopEnd, 2;
		else if (.@sec >= cl_shopend && !@cl_incombat)
			callsub L_ShopEnd, 0;
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_shs == 1) {
		if (.@sec >= @cl_sht || strcharinfo(3) != @cl_shmap$) {
			@cl_shs = 0;
			callsub L_Restock, 0;
			return;
		}
		if (@cl_incombat || @cl_mopc || @cl_akc || @cl_stc || !callsub(L_Calm) || .@sec - @cl_still < 2)
			return;
		.@k = 0;
		getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@om$)
				continue;
			.@can[.@k] = .@i;
			.@k++;
		}
		if (!.@k) {
			@cl_shs = 0;
			callsub L_Restock, 0;
			return;
		}
		.@i = .@can[0];
		if (.@k > 1)
			.@i = .@can[rand(.@k)];
		.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, "shopask", @cl_ar[.@i]), .@i, "");
		dispbottom "@@event clshop " + @cl_cid[.@i] + "|" + @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@i] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_cid[.@i])
			+ "|" + replacestr(.@t$, "|", "/") + "|Yes, have fun|Not now";
		@cl_shc = @cl_cid[.@i];
		@cl_shs = 2;
		@cl_sht = .@sec + 120;
		@cl_shnext = .@sec + 300;
		return;
	}
	// 2: the box is up.
	if (.@sec >= @cl_sht || @cl_incombat || strcharinfo(3) != @cl_shmap$) {
		dispbottom "@@event clshop -";
		@cl_shs = 0;
		callsub L_Restock, 0;
	}
	return;

// Whether companion getarg(0) (an index of the tick's @cl_ arrays) is out shopping.
L_Shopping:
	return (cl_shop$ != "" && compare("|" + cl_shop$, "|" + @cl_nm$[getarg(0)] + "|"));

// Off they go: getarg(0) the companion who asked (an index; -1: anyone). They
// roam the town (the restock is done when they're back, L_ShopEnd) on the
// engine's own roam ("@companion roam <name> on"), walking off. Each roams alone (L_Shopping: no group status).
L_ShopStart:
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	@cl_shs = 0;
	@cl_shnext = .@sec + 300;
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") <= 0) {
		callsub L_Restock, 0;
		dispbottom "[Companions] Companion roaming is off in the app's Settings (Population), so they only restocked.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	.@n = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@om$)
			continue;
		.@go[.@n] = .@i;
		.@n++;
	}
	if (!.@n) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] Nobody's free to go shopping right now.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	.@ask = getarg(0);
	if (.@ask < 0 || inarray(.@go, .@ask) < 0) {
		.@ask = .@go[0];
		if (.@n > 1)
			.@ask = .@go[rand(.@n)];
	}
	callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ask, "shopgo", "", ET_DELIGHT;
	.@names$ = "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@i = .@go[.@k];
		// 3.10.1: they all walk off. A Fly Wing away (unitwarp) showed them landing
		// back where they stood before walking off (user's test), so it went; the
		// Fly Wing back (L_ShopEnd) stays.
		atcommand "@companion roam " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " on";
		.@names$ += @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|";
	}
	cl_shop$ = .@names$;
	cl_shopmap$ = .@map$;
	cl_shopend = .@sec + 180;	// 3.10.1: 3 minutes (was 5)
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	dispbottom "[Companions] Off shopping round town. They're back in 3 minutes, when you leave town, or when you say \"come back\".", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

// Back from shopping: getarg(0) 0 time's up, 1 you called them ("come back"), 2 you
// left town (the engine already brought them along, still roaming). Roam goes off for
// those we sent; the ones still in town Fly Wing back beside you, the restock summary
// shows, and one says they're back.
L_ShopEnd:
	.@how = getarg(0);
	explode(.@nm$, cl_shop$, "|");
	cl_shop$ = "";
	cl_shopmap$ = "";
	cl_shopend = 0;
	@cl_shnext = gettimetick(2) + 300;
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	.@b = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < getarraysize(.@nm$); .@k++) {
		if (.@nm$[.@k] == "")
			continue;
		if (inarray(@cl_rm$, .@nm$[.@k]) >= 0)
			atcommand "@companion roam " + .@nm$[.@k] + " off";
		.@i = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nm$[.@k]);
		if (.@i < 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@back[.@b] = .@i;
		.@b++;
		if (.@how == 2)
			continue;
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@om$)
			continue;
		.@x = .@ox + rand(-2, 2);
		.@y = .@oy + rand(-2, 2);
		if (!checkcell(.@map$, .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass)) {
			.@x = .@ox;
			.@y = .@oy;
		}
		specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
		unitwarp @cl_aid[.@i], .@map$, .@x, .@y;
		specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	// What they bought while out: the summary comes back with them (quietly: the
	// "back" line below is their word).
	callsub L_Restock, 0, 1;
	if (.@b) {
		.@i = .@back[0];
		if (.@b > 1)
			.@i = .@back[rand(.@b)];
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "shopback", "", ET_SMILE;
	}
	return;

// "@clshop <request> yes|no|go": the portrait's answer, or "go shopping" in party chat.
OnCmdShop:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@a$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	if (.@a$ == "yes" || .@a$ == "no") {
		if (@cl_shs != 2) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|no ask";
			end;
		}
		.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_shc);
		if (.@a$ == "yes")
			callsub L_ShopStart, .@i;
		else {
			@cl_shs = 0;	// no shopping this visit
			if (.@i >= 0) {
				if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "shopno", "", ET_OK;
			}
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK";
		end;
	}
	if (.@a$ == "go") {
		if (cl_shop$ != "") {
			dispbottom "[Companions] They're already out shopping. Say \"come back\" to call them.", 0xFFCC66;
		} else if (callsub(L_MapKind, strcharinfo(3)) != "town") {
			dispbottom "[Companions] Shopping is for towns.", 0xFFCC66;
		} else {
			if (@cl_shs == 2)
				dispbottom "@@event clshop -";
			callsub L_ShopStart, -1;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|yes, no or go";
	end;

// ---- MVP hunts and Fly Wing hops (3.11.0) ------------------------------------------
// With .hops off (OnInit) they walk, and an MVP hunter fights what it meets on the way,
// because holding it (ct_hunt_look) would stop it.
// While they roam a field or dungeon, every 3 s each roamer looks round (14 cells, the
// screen). An MVP in sight: it calls you (L_HuntFound), and they all wait out of its
// reach until you come (L_HuntWait). Otherwise they fly on with a Fly Wing from their
// own bag (the engine's own teleport: the ct_hunt_fly rule, db/population_strategy.yml):
//   Normal (CL_HUNT 0): once nothing has been in sight for 25 s. Alone, each by itself;
//     as a group the leader flies and the rest Fly Wing to its side (L_HuntGroup).
//   MVP (CL_HUNT 1): each on its own every 8-14 s, starting no fight in between
//     (ct_hunt_look), only defending itself.
// Not while one of yours lies dead (the rally), nor where wings don't work, nor in the
// first 6 s on a map. Out of wings: it walks, and says so (every 10 minutes at most).
// Marks (harmless statuses the rules read): Reuse_Limit_A fly now, B look but start no
// fight, C keep out of the MVP's reach. They can't be ended, so a 1 ms restart clears them.
L_HuntTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@m$ = strcharinfo(3);
	if (.@m$ != @cl_hu_map$) {
		if (@cl_hu_g)
			callsub L_HuntEnd;
		@cl_hu_map$ = .@m$;
		@cl_hu_arr = .@sec;
		@cl_hu_since = 0;
		@cl_hu_lead = 0;
	}
	if (@cl_hu_g) {
		callsub L_HuntWait, .@sec;
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_hu_lead)
		callsub L_HuntGroup, .@sec;
	if (.@sec < @cl_hu_scan)
		return;
	@cl_hu_scan = .@sec + 3;
	.@kind$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@m$);
	if (.@kind$ != "field" && .@kind$ != "dungeon")
		return;
	.@n = 0;
	.@dead = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			.@dead = 1;
		else if (callsub(L_Roams, .@i)) {
			.@r[.@n] = .@i;
			.@n++;
		}
	}
	if (!.@n) {
		@cl_hu_since = 0;
		return;
	}
	if (!@cl_hu_since)
		@cl_hu_since = .@sec;
	.@fly = (.hops && !.@dead && !getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOTELEPORT) && .@sec >= @cl_hu_arr + 6);
	.@idle = 99999;	// as a group: the shortest time any of them has had nothing in sight
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@i = .@r[.@k];
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@m$)
			continue;
		.@u = getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@m$, .@cx - 14, .@cy - 14, .@cx + 14, .@cy + 14, .@g);
		.@near = 0;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@u; .@j++) {
			getunitdata .@g[.@j], .@d;
			if (.@d[UMOB_HP] <= 0 || .@d[UMOB_MASTERAID] > 0)
				continue;	// gone, or someone's pet or slave (an MVP's own too)
			.@mvp = .@d[UMOB_MODE] & MD_MVP;
			if (!.@mvp)
				.@mvp = (getmonsterinfo(.@d[UMOB_CLASS], MOB_MVPEXP) > 0);
			if (!.@mvp && CL_HUNT)
				.@mvp = (inarray(.hunt_mini, .@d[UMOB_CLASS]) >= 0);	// 3.12.7: MVP hunts only
			if (.@mvp) {
				callsub L_HuntFound, .@i, .@g[.@j], .@d[UMOB_CLASS], .@d[UMOB_X], .@d[UMOB_Y];
				return;
			}
			.@dx = .@d[UMOB_X] - .@cx;
			.@dy = .@d[UMOB_Y] - .@cy;
			if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) <= 9)
				.@near = 1;
		}
		if (.@near)
			setd "@cl_hu_s_" + .@aid, .@sec;
		.@seen = max(getd("@cl_hu_s_" + .@aid), @cl_hu_arr);
		.@idle = min(.@idle, .@sec - .@seen);
		if (!.@fly)
			continue;
		.@tp = callsub(L_HopTp, .@i);
		if (!.@tp && countitem(601, .@aid) < 1) {
			if (.@sec >= getd("@cl_hu_w_" + .@aid)) {
				setd "@cl_hu_w_" + .@aid, .@sec + 600;
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "mvpnowing", "", ET_SWEAT;
				dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " is out of Fly Wings: add some to their restock list (Bag tab).", 0xFFCC66;
			}
			continue;	// it walks (and in an MVP hunt, isn't held still either)
		}
		if (CL_HUNT) {
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_B, 5000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
			if (.@sec >= getd("@cl_hu_n_" + .@aid)) {
				setd "@cl_hu_n_" + .@aid, .@sec + 8 + rand(7);
				callsub L_Hop, .@i, .@tp, .@m$;
			}
		} else if (CL_ROAMALONE && .@sec - .@seen >= 25) {
			setd "@cl_hu_s_" + .@aid, .@sec;
			callsub L_Hop, .@i, .@tp, .@m$;
		}
	}
	// As a group: the leader (a Swordman-line one first) flies once none has seen a thing.
	if (.@fly && !CL_HUNT && !CL_ROAMALONE && .@idle >= 25 && .@idle < 99999 && !@cl_hu_lead) {
		.@lead = -1;
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
			.@i = .@r[.@k];
			if (countitem(601, @cl_aid[.@i]) < 1 && !callsub(L_HopTp, .@i))
				continue;
			if (.@lead < 0 || @cl_ar[.@i] == 1)
				.@lead = .@i;
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 1)
				break;
		}
		if (.@lead >= 0) {
			@cl_hu_lead = @cl_aid[.@lead];
			getmapxy(.@lm$, @cl_hu_lx, @cl_hu_ly, BL_PC, @cl_hu_lead);
			@cl_hu_lt = .@sec + 6;
			callsub L_Hop, .@lead, callsub(L_HopTp, .@lead), .@m$;
			for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++)
				setd "@cl_hu_s_" + @cl_aid[.@r[.@k]], .@sec;
		}
	}
	// Five minutes of an MVP hunt and no sign of it: one says so (again 5 minutes on).
	if (CL_HUNT && .@sec - @cl_hu_since >= 300 && callsub(L_Calm)) {
		@cl_hu_since = .@sec;
		.@i = .@r[0];
		if (.@n > 1)
			.@i = .@r[rand(.@n)];
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "mvpnone", "", ET_QUESTION;
	}
	return;

// 3.12.6: the Acolyte family (Priest, Monk and up) knows Teleport, so it hops for Teleport
// Lv 1's 10 SP and keeps its wings. Not a real cast: a shell can't answer the skill's Random
// menu, and no companion's skill list holds Teleport, so the script does what it does.
// Companion getarg(0) can teleport now? The SP left keeps Resurrection and a heal in reach;
// short of that it uses a Fly Wing if it has one, else it walks.
L_HopTp:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_ACOLYTE)
		return 0;
	return (@cl_sp[.@i] >= 90);

// Companion getarg(0) hops on map getarg(2): by Teleport (getarg(1) 1) or by Fly Wing (the
// ct_hunt_fly rule). unitwarp -1, -1 is a random free cell; app 1.7.8 keeps a roamer there.
L_Hop:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	if (!getarg(1)) {
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_A, 2500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
		return;
	}
	heal 0, -10, @cl_cid[.@i];
	specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
	unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;	// a walk under way would carry on from the old spot
	unitwarp .@aid, getarg(2), -1, -1;
	specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
	return;

// The group's flight (Normal, as a group): once the leader has landed somewhere else,
// the others Fly Wing to its side (one from each one's bag; one with none walks over on
// the group rules). Given up after 6 s (the wing didn't take).
L_HuntGroup:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@m$ = strcharinfo(3);
	if (getmapxy(.@lm$, .@lx, .@ly, BL_PC, @cl_hu_lead) != 0 || .@lm$ != .@m$ || .@sec > @cl_hu_lt) {
		@cl_hu_lead = 0;
		return;
	}
	.@dx = .@lx - @cl_hu_lx;
	.@dy = .@ly - @cl_hu_ly;
	if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) < 10)
		return;	// not yet
	.@lead = @cl_hu_lead;
	@cl_hu_lead = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		.@tp = callsub(L_HopTp, .@i);
		if (.@aid == .@lead || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !callsub(L_Roams, .@i) || (countitem(601, .@aid) < 1 && !.@tp))
			continue;
		.@x = .@lx + rand(-2, 2);
		.@y = .@ly + rand(-2, 2);
		if (!checkcell(.@m$, .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass)) {
			.@x = .@lx;
			.@y = .@ly;
		}
		if (.@tp)
			heal 0, -10, @cl_cid[.@i];
		else
			delitem 601, 1, .@aid;
		specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
		unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;	// a walk under way would carry on from the old spot
		unitwarp .@aid, .@m$, .@x, .@y;
		specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
		setd "@cl_hu_s_" + .@aid, .@sec;
	}
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) (index) sees an MVP (getarg(1) its unit, getarg(2) its class, at
// getarg(3),getarg(4)): it calls out, a red mark goes on your minimap, and its portrait
// asks ("@@event clhunt", client/index.js; 3.13.0: Engage together / Stand by, I'll solo /
// Retreat, OnCmdHunt). Then L_HuntWait.
L_HuntFound:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	@cl_hu_g = getarg(1);
	@cl_hu_f = @cl_aid[.@i];
	@cl_hu_m$ = getmonsterinfo(getarg(2), MOB_NAME);
	@cl_hu_t = gettimetick(2);
	@cl_hu_lost = 0;
	@cl_hu_lead = 0;
	@cl_hu_k = 0;	// the one mark: this find takes it over from one you put off
	viewpoint 1, getarg(3), getarg(4), 77, 0xFF3030;
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, "mvpfound", @cl_ar[.@i]), .@i, @cl_hu_m$);
	callsub L_SayAid, @cl_hu_f, @cl_nm$[.@i], .@t$;
	callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_SURPRISE;
	@cl_talk = gettimetick(2) + 6;
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " found " + @cl_hu_m$ + "! It's marked red on your minimap.", 0xFFCC66;
	dispbottom "@@event clhunt " + @cl_cid[.@i] + "|" + @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@i] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_cid[.@i])
		+ "|" + replacestr(.@t$, "|", "/") + "|Engage together|Stand by, I'll solo|Retreat";
	if (CL_DEBUG) {
		callsub L_Dbg, "find: " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " found " + @cl_hu_m$ + " at " + getarg(3) + "," + getarg(4) + " on " + strcharinfo(3) + (CL_HUNT ? " (MVP hunt)" : " (Normal hunt)");
		callsub L_DbgPos, "find", @cl_hu_g;
		callsub L_DbgHits, "wait", @cl_hu_g, 1;
	}
	return;

// Waiting for you at the MVP: every roamer keeps out of its reach (ct_hunt_wait_away) and
// starts no fight (ct_hunt_look); the mark follows it. You get there (9 cells from it, or
// 3 from the one who found it): L_HuntGather. It dies, the finder falls or is called
// back: it ends quietly. It gets away (16 cells from the finder for 15 s), or 3 minutes
// pass: the finder says so, and the hunt goes on.
L_HuntWait:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@fi = inarray(@cl_aid[0], @cl_hu_f);
	.@ok = (.@fi >= 0 && unitexists(@cl_hu_g));
	if (.@ok) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@fi] <= 0 || !callsub(L_Roams, .@fi))
			.@ok = 0;
	}
	if (.@ok) {
		getunitdata @cl_hu_g, .@d;
		.@ok = (.@d[UMOB_HP] > 0);
	}
	if (!.@ok) {
		if (CL_DEBUG) {
			.@why$ = @cl_hu_m$ + " is gone or dead";
			if (.@fi < 0)
				.@why$ = "the finder left the party";
			else if (@cl_hp[.@fi] <= 0)
				.@why$ = "the finder fell";
			else if (!callsub(L_Roams, .@fi))
				.@why$ = "the finder stopped roaming";
			callsub L_Dbg, "wait over: " + .@why$;
		}
		callsub L_HuntEnd;
		return;
	}
	if (CL_DEBUG)
		callsub L_DbgHits, "wait", @cl_hu_g, 0;
	.@bx = .@d[UMOB_X];
	.@by = .@d[UMOB_Y];
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	getmapxy(.@fm$, .@fx, .@fy, BL_PC, @cl_hu_f);
	.@dx = .@ox - .@bx;
	.@dy = .@oy - .@by;
	.@ex = .@ox - .@fx;
	.@ey = .@oy - .@fy;
	if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) <= 9 || max(.@ex, -.@ex, .@ey, -.@ey) <= 3) {
		if (CL_DEBUG) {
			.@how$ = ": gather, no answer yet";
			if (@cl_hu_mode == 1)
				.@how$ = ": engage";
			else if (@cl_hu_mode == 2)
				.@how$ = ": solo";
			callsub L_Dbg, "you reached the find on foot (" + max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) + " cells from " + @cl_hu_m$ + ")" + .@how$;
		}
		callsub L_HuntGather, .@fi;
		return;
	}
	.@gx = .@fx - .@bx;
	.@gy = .@fy - .@by;
	.@away = (max(.@gx, -.@gx, .@gy, -.@gy) > 16);
	if (!.@away) {
		@cl_hu_lost = 0;
		viewpoint 1, .@bx, .@by, 77, 0xFF3030;
	} else if (!@cl_hu_lost)
		@cl_hu_lost = .@sec;
	if ((.@away && .@sec - @cl_hu_lost >= 15) || .@sec - @cl_hu_t >= 180) {
		if (CL_DEBUG)
			callsub L_Dbg, "wait over: " + (.@away ? @cl_hu_m$ + " got 16+ cells from the finder for 15 s" : "3 minutes passed");
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@fi, "mvplost", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_HuntEnd;
		return;
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_B, 4500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_C, 4500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	}
	return;

// You came (getarg(0) the finder's index): its word, and everyone comes to you (L_HuntRecall).
// 3.13.0: on foot after Engage together or Stand by, I'll solo (OnCmdHunt): that answer.
L_HuntGather:
	if (@cl_hu_mode) {
		getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
		callsub L_HuntGo, .@x, .@y;
		return;
	}
	callsub L_SpeakNow, getarg(0), "mvpgather", "", ET_GO;
	callsub L_HuntEnd;
	callsub L_HuntRecall;
	return;

// 3.13.0: you're at the find (getarg(0),getarg(1)), with an answer in @cl_hu_mode: everyone
// flies to you and follows (L_HuntRecall), and the mark stays on the monster (L_HuntMark).
// 1 Engage together: they all go for it now (L_HuntCharge). 2 Stand by, I'll solo: they keep
// clear and only defend themselves while it lives (L_SoloTick), until you say "help me".
L_HuntGo:
	.@g = @cl_hu_g;
	.@mode = @cl_hu_mode;
	.@fi = inarray(@cl_aid[0], @cl_hu_f);
	callsub L_HuntEnd;
	callsub L_HuntRecall, getarg(0), getarg(1);
	@cl_hu_k = .@g;
	@cl_hu_km$ = strcharinfo(3);
	@cl_hu_kx = 0;
	@cl_hu_ky = 0;
	callsub L_HuntMark;
	if (.@fi < 0 || @cl_hp[.@fi] <= 0)
		.@fi = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	if (CL_DEBUG) {
		callsub L_Dbg, "go: " + (.@mode == 1 ? "engage" : "solo") + ", everyone called to you at " + getarg(0) + "," + getarg(1);
		callsub L_DbgPos, "go", .@g;
	}
	if (.@mode == 1) {
		callsub L_HuntCharge;
		if (.@fi >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@fi, "mvpengage", @cl_hu_m$, ET_GO;
		return;
	}
	@cl_hu_solo = .@g;
	@cl_hu_so_m$ = strcharinfo(3);
	@cl_hu_so_next = 0;
	if (CL_DEBUG)
		callsub L_DbgHits, "solo", .@g, 1;
	callsub L_SoloTick, gettimetick(2);
	if (.@fi >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@fi, "mvpsolo", @cl_hu_m$, ET_OK;
	dispbottom "[Companions] They keep clear of " + @cl_hu_m$ + " and only defend themselves. Say \"help me\" in party chat to call them in.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

// 3.13.0: everyone on this map goes for the boss: Reuse_Limit_E (ct_hunt_engage) makes it
// their target for 10 s, whatever their stance; then their usual rules carry on the fight.
L_HuntCharge:
	if (CL_DEBUG)
		callsub L_Dbg, "charge: everyone targets the boss for 10 s";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_E, 10000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	}
	return;

// 3.13.0: Stand by, I'll solo, each tick: Reuse_Limit_D (ct_solo_away, ct_solo_still) renewed
// every 3 s for 6 s. Over when it dies (they cheer), you leave the map, they're sent roaming,
// or "help me" (OnCmdHunt) or your fall (OnPCDieEvent) ends it early.
L_SoloTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@ok = (strcharinfo(3) == @cl_hu_so_m$ && unitexists(@cl_hu_solo));
	if (.@ok) {
		getunitdata @cl_hu_solo, .@d;
		.@ok = (.@d[UMOB_HP] > 0);
	}
	if (!.@ok) {
		.@won = (strcharinfo(3) == @cl_hu_so_m$ && Hp > 0);
		if (CL_DEBUG) {
			.@why$ = @cl_hu_m$ + " is gone";
			if (strcharinfo(3) != @cl_hu_so_m$)
				.@why$ = "you left the map";
			else if (.@won)
				.@why$ = @cl_hu_m$ + " is dead, you won";
			callsub L_Dbg, "solo over: " + .@why$;
		}
		callsub L_SoloEnd;
		if (.@won) {
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "mvpsolowin", @cl_hu_m$, ET_BEST;
		}
		return;
	}
	if (getarraysize(@cl_rm$) || CL_STANDBY) {
		if (CL_DEBUG)
			callsub L_Dbg, "solo over: " + (CL_STANDBY ? "Stand By was ordered" : "someone was sent roaming");
		callsub L_SoloEnd;
		return;
	}
	if (CL_DEBUG)
		callsub L_DbgHits, "solo", @cl_hu_solo, 0;
	if (.@sec < @cl_hu_so_next)
		return;
	@cl_hu_so_next = .@sec + 3;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_D, 6000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	}
	return;

// 3.13.0: the solo is over: the marks can't be ended, so a 1 ms restart clears them.
L_SoloEnd:
	@cl_hu_solo = 0;
	@cl_hu_so_m$ = "";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_D, 1, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	return;

// 3.13.2: the Debug recorder's line (getarg(0)); call it only behind `if (CL_DEBUG)`.
L_Dbg:
	.@w$ = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = gettimestr("%H:%M:%S", 9) + " " + .@w$;
	.@n = getarraysize(@cl_dbg$);
	if (.@n && @cl_dbg_last$ == .@w$) {
		@cl_dbg_x++;
		@cl_dbg$[.@n - 1] = .@t$ + " (x" + (@cl_dbg_x + 1) + ")";
		return;
	}
	@cl_dbg_last$ = .@w$;
	@cl_dbg_x = 0;
	if (.@n >= 50)
		deletearray @cl_dbg$[0], .@n - 49;
	@cl_dbg$[getarraysize(@cl_dbg$)] = .@t$;
	debugmes "[CPO debug] " + strcharinfo(0) + " " + .@t$;
	if (CL_DEBUG == 2)
		dispbottom "[CPO debug] " + .@t$, 0x99CCFF;
	return;

// 3.13.2, recorder: who lost HP since the last look (getarg(0) the phase, getarg(1) the boss's
// unit id, getarg(2) 1 = only note their HP now), with how far each is from the boss. This is
// what lets a "Hold unless attacked" go.
L_DbgHits:
	.@g = getarg(1);
	.@bx = -1;
	if (unitexists(.@g)) {
		getunitdata .@g, .@d;
		.@bx = .@d[UMOB_X];
		.@by = .@d[UMOB_Y];
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@cid = @cl_cid[.@i];
		.@hp = readparam(Hp, .@cid);
		.@k$ = "@cl_dbhp_" + .@cid;
		.@was = getd(.@k$);
		setd .@k$, .@hp;
		if (getarg(2) || .@was <= 0 || .@hp >= .@was)
			continue;
		.@far$ = "";
		if (.@bx >= 0 && getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) == 0) {
			.@dx = .@x - .@bx;
			.@dy = .@y - .@by;
			.@far$ = ", " + max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) + " cells from it";
		}
		.@mhp = readparam(MaxHp, .@cid);
		.@pc = 0;
		if (.@mhp > 0)
			.@pc = .@hp * 100 / .@mhp;
		callsub L_Dbg, getarg(0) + ": " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " lost " + (.@was - .@hp) + " HP (" + .@pc + "% left)" + .@far$ + (.@hp <= 0 ? ", FELL" : "");
	}
	return;

// 3.13.2, recorder: where everyone stands, from the boss (getarg(0) the phase, getarg(1) its id).
L_DbgPos:
	.@g = getarg(1);
	if (!unitexists(.@g))
		return;
	getunitdata .@g, .@d;
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	.@dx = .@ox - .@d[UMOB_X];
	.@dy = .@oy - .@d[UMOB_Y];
	.@s$ = "you " + max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0)
			continue;
		.@dx = .@x - .@d[UMOB_X];
		.@dy = .@y - .@d[UMOB_Y];
		.@s$ += ", " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " " + max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) + (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 ? " (down)" : "");
	}
	callsub L_Dbg, getarg(0) + ": cells from " + @cl_hu_m$ + ": " + .@s$;
	return;

// A find you put off ("Not now", OnCmdHunt skip; @cl_hu_k the monster): the mark follows it
// while it lives on this map, and goes when it dies or you leave.
L_HuntMark:
	.@ok = (strcharinfo(3) == @cl_hu_km$ && unitexists(@cl_hu_k));
	if (.@ok) {
		getunitdata @cl_hu_k, .@d;
		.@ok = (.@d[UMOB_HP] > 0);
	}
	if (!.@ok) {
		viewpoint 2, 0, 0, 77, 0;
		@cl_hu_k = 0;
		return;
	}
	if (.@d[UMOB_X] != @cl_hu_kx || .@d[UMOB_Y] != @cl_hu_ky) {
		@cl_hu_kx = .@d[UMOB_X];
		@cl_hu_ky = .@d[UMOB_Y];
		viewpoint 1, @cl_hu_kx, @cl_hu_ky, 77, 0xFF3030;
	}
	return;

// Everyone stops roaming and follows you, and those further off Fly Wing to you (one from
// their bag; without one they walk over).
// 3.13.0: getarg(0),getarg(1) where you are, when you've only just flown there; and the
// Acolyte family Teleports for 10 SP first (L_HopTp), as on the hunt.
L_HuntRecall:
	.@m$ = strcharinfo(3);
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (getargcount() >= 2) {
		.@ox = getarg(0);
		.@oy = getarg(1);
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		atcommand "@companion roam " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " off";
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@m$)
			continue;
		.@dx = .@cx - .@ox;
		.@dy = .@cy - .@oy;
		.@tp = callsub(L_HopTp, .@i);
		if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) <= 9 || (countitem(601, .@aid) < 1 && !.@tp))
			continue;
		.@x = .@ox + rand(-2, 2);
		.@y = .@oy + rand(-2, 2);
		if (!checkcell(.@m$, .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass)) {
			.@x = .@ox;
			.@y = .@oy;
		}
		if (.@tp)
			heal 0, -10, @cl_cid[.@i];
		else
			delitem 601, 1, .@aid;
		specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
		unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;	// the flag: no warning for one already standing
		unitwarp .@aid, .@m$, .@x, .@y;
		specialeffect2 EF_TELEPORTATION, AREA, @cl_nm$[.@i];
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	return;

// The wait is over: the mark and the portrait's box go, and so do the marks on them.
L_HuntEnd:
	if (@cl_hu_g) {
		viewpoint 2, 0, 0, 77, 0;
		dispbottom "@@event clhunt -";
	}
	@cl_hu_g = 0;
	@cl_hu_f = 0;
	@cl_hu_lost = 0;
	@cl_hu_mode = 0;
	@cl_hu_since = gettimetick(2);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_B, 1, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_C, 1, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
	}
	return;

// ---- "Can we keep it?" (3.9.0) ---------------------------------------------------
// One companion at a time, in a calm moment (no fight, not camping, you standing still
// a few seconds, nobody talking, not in the first 2 minutes): the first time it has
// reached the Companion bond, or (second ask) an hour of play together after your first
// "Not now". Steps (@cl_akc its char id, @cl_aks the step, @cl_akt when the next one is
// due, s): 1 its pet comes along (@cl_pv_<char id>, L_PetTick); 2 it walks up to you and
// says so; 3 the portrait asks, Yes / Not now ("@@event clask", client/portraits.js),
// and the answer comes back as "@clbag keep". No answer in 2 minutes (a warp, the window
// closed): it lets it go and tries again in 10 minutes. Store per companion: pa.c asks
// so far, pat.c the minutes together (m.c<id>.mins) when the second may come.
L_PetAsk:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	if (@cl_akc) {
		.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_akc);
		.@drop = (.@i < 0 || @cl_incombat);
		if (!.@drop) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
				.@drop = 1;
		}
		if (.@drop) {
			callsub L_PetAskEnd, .@sec + 600;
			return;
		}
		if (.@sec < @cl_akt)
			return;
		if (@cl_aks == 1) {
			// It walks over with its pet at its heels and says so.
			callsub L_GiftWalk, .@i;
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "pcome", "", ET_QUESTION, 0, "";
			.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@i]);
			if (.@g && .emotes)
				emotion ET_LUV, .@g;
			@cl_aks = 2;
			@cl_akt = .@sec + 5;
			return;
		}
		if (@cl_aks == 2) {
			.@sp$ = .pet_nm$[callsub(L_PetOf, @cl_cls[.@i])];
			.@t$ = callsub(L_Line, "pask" + @cl_akr, @cl_ar[.@i]);
			.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, replacestr(.@t$, "{m}", .@sp$), .@i, "");
			dispbottom "@@event clask " + @cl_akc + "|" + @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@i] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_akc)
				+ "|" + @cl_akr + "|" + .@sp$ + "|" + replacestr(.@t$, "|", "/");
			@cl_aks = 3;
			@cl_akt = .@sec + 120;
			return;
		}
		callsub L_PetAskEnd, .@sec + 600;	// no answer
		return;
	}
	if (.@sec < @cl_aknext || @cl_incombat || @cl_camp || @cl_mopc || @cl_stc || @cl_shs || !callsub(L_Calm) || .@sec - @cl_peace < 15
			|| .@sec - @cl_still < 4 || .@sec < @cl_login + 120)
		return;
	@cl_aknext = .@sec + 20;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || callsub(L_Roams, .@i) || callsub(L_PetKept, .@c))
			continue;
		.@n = modstore_get("char", "pa.c" + .@c, 0);
		if (.@n >= 2)
			continue;
		if (.@n == 1) {
			if (modstore_get("char", "m.c" + .@c + ".mins", 0) < modstore_get("char", "pat.c" + .@c, 0))
				continue;
		}
		if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, .@c)) < 2)
			continue;
		@cl_akc = .@c;
		@cl_aks = 1;
		@cl_akt = .@sec + 3;	// time for its pet to appear beside it
		@cl_akr = .@n + 1;
		setd "@cl_pv_" + .@c, 1;
		return;
	}
	return;

// The ask is over: its pet goes back unless you kept it, the window's box closes, and
// the next ask (of anyone) waits until getarg(0) (s).
L_PetAskEnd:
	if (@cl_akc)
		setd "@cl_pv_" + @cl_akc, 0;
	if (@cl_aks >= 3)
		dispbottom "@@event clask -";
	@cl_akc = 0;
	@cl_aks = 0;
	@cl_aknext = getarg(0);
	return;

// ---- "Hey boss" moments (3.9.1, setting moments) ---------------------------------
// In a calm moment (as for the pet ask, at most one every 20-30 minutes, not in the
// first 5 minutes) a companion with a moment due walks over and asks (.mo_*: see
// L_MoAdd). Due: its personality (or any), its bond tier with you (a story choice:
// its life-story chapter), not answered before (a "check": every two hours, when
// you've played two hours or fell lately). Asking to use your name comes first.
// Steps (@cl_mopc its char id, @cl_mopid the moment, @cl_mos the step, @cl_mot when):
// 1 it walks over (a vote: two others give their opinion), 2 the portrait asks
// ("@@event clmoment", client/portraits.js askChoice), 3 the answer comes back as
// "@clmoment" (OnCmdMoment). No answer in 2 minutes: let go, again in 10.
// Before any new moment, a story answer's "later" line when it's due (store
// m.c<id>.mofu = minutes together then, .mofut = the line).
L_MomentTry:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	if (@cl_mopc) {
		.@i = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_mopc);
		.@drop = (.@i < 0 || @cl_incombat);
		if (!.@drop) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
				.@drop = 1;
		}
		if (.@drop) {
			callsub L_MomentEnd, .@sec + 600;
			return;
		}
		.@k = @cl_mopid;
		// "..." (step 10): it waits for you to come over. Every few seconds the
		// thinking emote; you within 2 cells: it asks; 5 minutes: it lets it go.
		if (@cl_mos == 10) {
			if (.@sec >= @cl_mot) {
				callsub L_MomentEnd, .@sec + 600;
				return;
			}
			callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_THINK;	// (at most every 12 s, L_EmoteAid)
			getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
			if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) == 0 && .@cm$ == .@om$) {
				if (max(.@cx - .@ox, .@ox - .@cx, .@cy - .@oy, .@oy - .@cy) <= 2) {
					@cl_mos = 2;	// you came over: it asks now
					@cl_mot = .@sec;
					if (.mo_kind$[.@k] == "vote")
						callsub L_MoVoteLines, .@i, .@k;
					else if (.@k == .qz_k && @cl_qzb)
						callsub L_QuizArgue, .@i;
				}
			}
			return;
		}
		if (.@sec < @cl_mot)
			return;
		if (@cl_mos == 1) {
			// 3.9.1: who comes to you and who waits. Checking on you, a party vote, and
			// right after an MVP, a level-up or with you overloaded: it walks over. Its
			// own things (your name, advice, its story, places, the quieter encounters)
			// can wait: "..." over its head until you come over (step 10). At the fire
			// it's always "...": you go to the one who's thinking.
			.@kd$ = .mo_kind$[.@k];
			.@come = (.@kd$ == "check" || .@kd$ == "vote");
			if (.@kd$ == "enc") {
				.@x$ = .mo_x1$[.@k];
				if (.@x$ == "mvp" || .@x$ == "lvl" || .@x$ == "heavy")
					.@come = 1;
			}
			if (.@k == .qz_k)
				.@come = @cl_qzcome;	// a monster here, an argument, a bet: it comes over
			if (@cl_camp)
				.@come = 0;
			if (!.@come) {
				@cl_mos = 10;
				@cl_mot = .@sec + 300;
				dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " has something on their mind. Walk up to them.", 0xFFCC66;
				callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_THINK;
				return;
			}
			callsub L_GiftWalk, .@i;
			callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_QUESTION;
			@cl_mot = .@sec + 4;
			if (.mo_kind$[.@k] == "vote")
				callsub L_MoVoteLines, .@i, .@k;
			else if (.@k == .qz_k && @cl_qzb)
				callsub L_QuizArgue, .@i;
			@cl_mos = 2;
			return;
		}
		if (@cl_mos == 2) {
			if (getarraysize(@cl_qa))
				return;	// a vote's opinions first
			if (.@k == .qz_k) {
				// 3.10.2: the pop quiz (L_QuizStart); the last field picks the bubble's look.
				dispbottom "@@event clmoment " + @cl_mopc + "|" + .@k + "|" + @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@i] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_mopc)
					+ "|" + callsub(L_Clean, @cl_qzt$) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, @cl_qzo$[0]) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, @cl_qzo$[1])
					+ "|" + (@cl_qzno > 2 ? callsub(L_Clean, @cl_qzo$[2]) : "") + "|" + @cl_qztag$;
				@cl_mos = 3;
				@cl_mot = .@sec + 120;
				return;
			}
			.@o1$ = callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_Text, .mo_o1$[.@k], .@i, ""));
			.@o2$ = callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_Text, .mo_o2$[.@k], .@i, ""));
			.@o3$ = callsub(L_Clean, callsub(L_Text, .mo_o3$[.@k], .@i, ""));
			.@q$ = callsub(L_Text, .mo_q$[.@k], .@i, "");
			dispbottom "@@event clmoment " + @cl_mopc + "|" + .@k + "|" + @cl_nm$[.@i] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@i] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_mopc)
				+ "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@q$) + "|" + .@o1$ + "|" + .@o2$ + "|" + .@o3$;
			@cl_mos = 3;
			@cl_mot = .@sec + 120;
			return;
		}
		callsub L_MomentEnd, .@sec + 600;	// no answer
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_incombat || @cl_akc || @cl_stc || @cl_shs || !callsub(L_Calm) || .@sec < @cl_login + 300 || .@sec - @cl_still < 6 || .@sec - @cl_peace < 20)
		return;
	// A story answer's "later" line, when it's due.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
		.@due = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mofu", 0);
		if (!.@due || modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) < .@due)
			continue;
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mofut", ""), ET_SMILE;
		modstore_delete "char", .@p$ + ".mofu";
		modstore_delete "char", .@p$ + ".mofut";
		return;
	}
	// 3.10.2: the pop quiz keeps its own time (the first one half a gap after you're calm).
	if (.quiz && @cl_n) {
		if (!@cl_qznext)
			@cl_qznext = .@sec + .quiz * 30;
		else if (.@sec >= @cl_qznext) {
			if (callsub(L_QuizStart, .@sec))
				return;
			@cl_qznext = .@sec + 60;	// nothing to ask right now
		}
	}
	if (!.moments || .@sec < @cl_monext)
		return;
	@cl_monext = .@sec + 60;	// nothing due: look again in a minute
	// What fits right now, for the encounters (kind enc, extra 1).
	.@trig$ = ",";
	if (@cl_camp)
		.@trig$ += "camp,";
	if (@cl_lastmvp && .@sec - @cl_lastmvp < 900)
		.@trig$ += "mvp,";
	.@lh = gettime(DT_HOUR);
	if ($@dn_on)
		.@lh = $@dn_min / 60;	// the game's hour, with the day-night mod
	if (.@lh >= 21 || .@lh < 5)
		.@trig$ += "night,";
	.@mk$ = callsub(L_MapKind, strcharinfo(3));
	if (.@mk$ == "town" || .@mk$ == "dungeon")
		.@trig$ += .@mk$ + ",";
	if (@cl_plvl && .@sec - @cl_plvl < 600)
		.@trig$ += "lvl,";
	if (Zeny >= 1000000)
		.@trig$ += "rich,";
	else if (Zeny < 5000)
		.@trig$ += "broke,";
	if (MaxWeight > 0 && Weight * 100 / MaxWeight >= 70)
		.@trig$ += "heavy,";
	// A hundred moments for each companion, once a minute: more steps than the loop
	// guard allows in one run with everything else the tick does (2.3.3's "infinity
	// loop"), so it's off for the scan.
	freeloop(1);
	.@nc = 0;
	.@sum = 0;
	.@nm = -1;	// a name moment, if one is due: it comes first
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;
		.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
		.@p$ = "m.c" + .@c;
		.@bt = getd("@cl_bt_" + .@c);
		if (!.@bt) {
			.@bt = callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, .@c));
			setd "@cl_bt_" + .@c, .@bt;
		}
		.@arc = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arc", 0);
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < .mo_n; .@k++) {
			.@kd$ = .mo_kind$[.@k];
			if (.@kd$ == "gone" || .@kd$ == "quiz")
				continue;	// a retired place to visit; the quiz has its own timer
			.@own = 0;
			if (compare(.@kd$, "story")) {
				// a choice in its own story (1-3 or a class's 11-14, L_StoryVar), once it
				// told chapter 3; a class's story stays theirs whatever their personality now
				.@own = callsub(L_MoStory, .@kd$);
				if (.@own != modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".story", 1))
					continue;
				.@own = (.@own >= 11);
				.@kd$ = "story";
			}
			if (!.@own && .mo_p[.@k] >= 0 && .mo_p[.@k] != @cl_ar[.@i])
				continue;
			if (.@kd$ == "story") {
				if (.@arc < .mo_tier[.@k])
					continue;
			} else if (.@bt < .mo_tier[.@k])
				continue;
			if (.@kd$ == "vote" && @cl_n < 3)
				continue;
			if (.@kd$ == "enc") {
				if (!compare(.@trig$, "," + .mo_x1$[.@k] + ","))
					continue;
			}
			if (.@kd$ == "name") {
				if (modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".callname", 0))
					continue;
			}
			// Places, votes and encounters anyone may ask are asked once for the whole
			// party (your store, mop<moment>); their own questions once each.
			.@party = 0;
			if (.mo_p[.@k] < 0 && .@kd$ != "check")
				.@party = 1;
			if (.mo_rep[.@k]) {
				if (.@sec < @cl_mochk || .@sec < modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".moat" + .@k, 0) + .mo_rep[.@k])
					continue;
				if (.mo_x1$[.@k] == "died" && .@sec - @cl_pdead > 600)
					continue;
				if (.mo_x1$[.@k] == "long" && .@sec - @cl_login < 7200)
					continue;
			} else if (.@party) {
				if (modstore_get("char", "mop" + .@k, 0))
					continue;
			} else if (modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mo" + .@k, 0))
				continue;
			if (.@kd$ == "name" && .@nm < 0) {
				.@nm = .@nc;
			}
			// Their own story and how you are weigh most; an encounter that fits now
			// next; a party-wide one is listed under every companion, so it weighs less.
			.@w = 10;
			if (.@kd$ == "story" || .@kd$ == "check")
				.@w = 30;
			else if (.@kd$ == "enc")
				.@w = 25;
			if (.@party)
				.@w = max(3, .@w / @cl_n);
			.@ci[.@nc] = .@i;
			.@ck[.@nc] = .@k;
			.@cw[.@nc] = .@w;
			.@sum += .@w;
			.@nc++;
		}
	}
	if (!.@nc)
		return;
	.@s = .@nm;
	if (.@s < 0) {
		.@r = rand(.@sum);
		for (.@s = 0; .@s < .@nc - 1; .@s++) {
			.@r -= .@cw[.@s];
			if (.@r < 0)
				break;
		}
	}
	@cl_mopc = @cl_cid[.@ci[.@s]];
	@cl_mopid = .@ck[.@s];
	@cl_mos = 1;
	@cl_mot = .@sec + 1;
	@cl_monext = .@sec + 1200 + rand(601);
	return;

// A vote (moment getarg(1), asked by companion getarg(0)): two others say their
// opinion first (extra 1 and 2).
L_MoVoteLines:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@k = getarg(1);
	.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
	.@c = -1;
	for (.@t = 0; .@t < 6 && .@c < 0 && .@b >= 0; .@t++) {
		.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
		if (.@x != .@b)
			.@c = .@x;
	}
	if (.@b >= 0)
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@b, .mo_x1$[.@k], ET_GO;
	if (.@c >= 0)
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@c, .mo_x2$[.@k], ET_SWEAT;
	return;

// The moment is over: the box closes (when it was up), the next one not before
// getarg(0) (s).
L_MomentEnd:
	if (@cl_mos >= 3)
		dispbottom "@@event clmoment -";
	// 3.10.2: a quiz that never got its answer (a fight, no answer) comes again in 5
	// minutes; after any other moment, the quiz waits at least 3.
	.@now = gettimetick(2);
	if (@cl_mopid == .qz_k && @cl_qzmode) {
		@cl_qzmode = 0;
		@cl_qzb = 0;
		@cl_qznext = .@now + 300;
	} else if (@cl_qznext && @cl_qznext < .@now + 180)
		@cl_qznext = .@now + 180;
	@cl_mopc = 0;
	@cl_mos = 0;
	if (getarg(0) > @cl_monext)
		@cl_monext = getarg(0);
	return;

// 3.9.1: companion getarg(0) (an index) says line getarg(1) as written ({t}, {p},
// {a} filled in) at its turn, with emote getarg(2).
L_QueueRaw:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, getarg(1), .@i, "");
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@i];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = getarg(2);
	return;

// ---- A pet by its fallen companion (3.9.0) ---------------------------------------
// The pet itself can't stay: rAthena's slave AI (mob.cpp mob_ai_sub_hard_slavemob) ends
// a slave the moment its master falls. So a lookalike takes its place beside the body:
// a copy of one of the CpoPetMourn0-5 NPCs (only you see it; one copy per source NPC
// per player, so six), with the pet's look and name. It cries every few seconds, and
// goes once its companion is up again (its pet comes back with a happy emote,
// L_PetSpawn), when the companion isn't with you any more, or when you leave the map.
// Slots: @cl_mo<n>$ the copy's name, @cl_moa<n> its companion, @cl_mot<n> next cry (s).
L_PetMournStart:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	if (getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@m$ != strcharinfo(3))
		return;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < 6; .@s++) {
		if (getd("@cl_mo" + .@s + "$") == "")
			break;
		if (getd("@cl_moa" + .@s) == .@aid)
			return;	// already there
	}
	if (.@s >= 6)
		return;
	// A free cell beside the body.
	setarray .@ox, 1, -1, 0, 0, 1, -1, 1, -1;
	setarray .@oy, 0, 0, 1, -1, 1, 1, -1, -1;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8; .@k++) {
		if (checkcell(.@m$, .@x + .@ox[.@k], .@y + .@oy[.@k], cell_chkpass))
			break;
	}
	if (.@k >= 8)
		return;
	.@n$ = duplicate_dynamic("CpoPetMourn" + .@s);
	if (.@n$ == "")
		return;
	setnpcdisplay .@n$, callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@i]), .pet_mob[callsub(L_PetOf, @cl_cls[.@i])];
	movenpc .@n$, .@x + .@ox[.@k], .@y + .@oy[.@k];
	setd "@cl_mo" + .@s + "$", .@n$;
	setd "@cl_moa" + .@s, .@aid;
	setd "@cl_mot" + .@s, gettimetick(2) + 1;
	return;

L_PetMourn:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < 6; .@s++) {
		.@n$ = getd("@cl_mo" + .@s + "$");
		if (.@n$ == "")
			continue;
		.@keep = 0;
		.@i = inarray(@cl_aid, getd("@cl_moa" + .@s));
		if (.@i >= 0) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 && getmapxy(.@nm$, .@nx, .@ny, BL_NPC, .@n$) == 0) {
				if (.@nm$ == strcharinfo(3))
					.@keep = 1;
			}
		}
		if (!.@keep) {
			unloadnpc .@n$;
			setd "@cl_mo" + .@s + "$", "";
			setd "@cl_moa" + .@s, 0;
		} else if (.@sec >= getd("@cl_mot" + .@s)) {
			setd "@cl_mot" + .@s, .@sec + 5 + rand(4);
			emotion ET_CRY, getnpcid(0, .@n$);
		}
	}
	return;

// Which pet (.pet_* index) class getarg(0) brings.
L_PetOf:
	.@j = roclass(eaclass(getarg(0)) & ~(EAJL_UPPER|EAJL_BABY));
	if (.@j >= 0 && .@j < getarraysize(.pet_job))
		return .pet_job[.@j];
	if (.@j == JOB_SOUL_LINKER)
		return 3;	// Chonchon, the TaeKwon line's scholar
	return 10;	// Smokie: TaeKwon, Star Gladiator

// Companion getarg(0) (index) gets its pet beside it.
L_PetSpawn:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	setd "@cl_qpw_" + .@aid, gettimetick(2) + 3;	// a try every 3 s at most
	if (getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0)
		return;
	.@p = callsub(L_PetOf, @cl_cls[.@i]);
	.@nm$ = callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@i]);	// 3.9.0: the name you gave it
	if (.@nm$ == .pet_nm$[.@p]) {
		.@nm$ = @cl_nm$[.@i] + "'s " + .pet_nm$[.@p];
		if (getstrlen(.@nm$) > 23)
			.@nm$ = .pet_nm$[.@p];	// a name stops at 23 letters
	}
	monster .@m$, .@x, .@y, .@nm$, .pet_mob[.@p], 1;
	.@g = $@mobid[0];
	if (.@g <= 0 || !unitexists(.@g))
		return;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_MASTERAID, .@aid;
	// Monster spells off (it has none, but to be sure). The server's slaves_inherit_mode
	// (4) has every slave move and attack, so a pet always joins in.
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_MODE, MD_CANMOVE | MD_CANATTACK | MD_NOCAST | MD_KNOCKBACKIMMUNE;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_DMGIMMUNE, 1;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_SPEED, 150;	// slaves_inherit_speed then gives it its companion's
	callsub L_PetStats, .@g, @cl_lv[.@i];
	setd "@cl_pg_" + .@aid, .@g;
	setd "@cl_qpl_" + .@aid, @cl_lv[.@i];
	setd "@cl_pp_" + .@aid, .@p;
	if (getd("@cl_pdd_" + .@aid)) {
		setd "@cl_pdd_" + .@aid, 0;
		if (.emotes)
			emotion ET_THROB, .@g;	// its companion is up again
	}
	return;

// The same for every pet, by its companion's level getarg(1): attack about 2 x the
// level (x pet_strength %), one hit per 1.5 s, accuracy enough to land on monsters
// of that level, no criticals.
L_PetStats:
	.@g = getarg(0);
	.@lv = max(1, getarg(1));
	.@atk = max(1, .@lv * 2 * .pet_pow / 100);
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_LEVEL, .@lv;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_ATKMIN, max(1, .@atk * 4 / 5);
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_ATKMAX, .@atk * 6 / 5;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_HIT, .@lv * 2 + 30;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_CRIT, 0;
	setunitdata .@g, UMOB_ADELAY, 1500;
	return;

// A companion here whose pet is out (not index getarg(0)); -1 none.
L_PetPick:
	.@k = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (.@i == getarg(0) || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@i]);
		if (.@g && unitexists(.@g)) {
			.@c[.@k] = .@i;
			.@k++;
		}
	}
	if (!.@k)
		return -1;
	if (.@k == 1)
		return .@c[0];
	return .@c[rand(.@k)];

// Every pet out shows emote getarg(0), each at getarg(1) % chance.
L_PetEmotes:
	if (!.emotes)
		return;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@g = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@i]);
		if (.@g && unitexists(.@g) && rand(100) < getarg(1))
			emotion getarg(0), .@g;
	}
	return;

// Between fights: a pet's emote every 40-90 s (sleepy once the party has sat a
