// while or by the campfire), and every 5-9 minutes a word: a companion about its
// own pet, or about another's, who answers. getarg(0) = now (s).
L_PetLife:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	// 3.9.0: by the campfire every pet sleeps (zzz every 25-40 s), and a minute in,
	// one companion says so, once a camp.
	if (!@cl_camp)
		@cl_pcl = 0;
	else {
		if (!@cl_pcl)
			@cl_pcl = .@sec + 60;
		if (.emotes && .@sec >= @cl_penext) {
			@cl_penext = .@sec + 25 + rand(16);
			callsub L_PetEmotes, ET_SLEEPY, 100;
		}
		if (@cl_pcl > 1 && .@sec >= @cl_pcl && .chatter && callsub(L_Calm)) {
			@cl_pcl = 1;
			.@a = callsub(L_PetPick, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "petcamp", "", 0, 0, callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@a]);
		}
		return;
	}
	// 3.9.0: two pets play (every 6-10 minutes, you standing still 20 s+): one runs
	// over to the other's companion and its own companion's pull brings it back (a
	// slave stays within 2 cells of its master), both delighted, and someone remarks.
	if (.@sec >= @cl_pplay && .@sec - @cl_still >= 20 && callsub(L_Calm) && .@sec >= @cl_soft) {
		if (!@cl_pplay)
			@cl_pplay = .@sec + 240 + rand(240);
		else {
			@cl_pplay = .@sec + 360 + rand(241);
			.@a = callsub(L_PetPick, -1);
			.@b = callsub(L_PetPick, .@a);	// -1 with no pets at all, as .@a
			if (.@a >= 0 && .@b >= 0) {
				.@ga = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@a]);
				.@gb = getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@b]);
				if (getmapxy(.@bm$, .@bx, .@by, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@b]) == 0) {
					// 3.10.2: a unitwalk sets rAthena's forced-walk flag, and a monster with it
					// set runs NO AI (mob_ai_sub_hard): no following, no fighting. It clears
					// only on arrival, so a walk cut short (a fight, Engage mode) froze the pet
					// for good. Clear it first, and again 8 s on (L_PetTick, @cl_ppf_).
					unitstopwalk .@ga, USW_FORCE_STOP;
					unitwalk .@ga, .@bx + 1, .@by;
					setd "@cl_ppf_" + @cl_aid[.@a], .@sec + 8;
				}
				if (.emotes) {
					emotion ET_KIK, .@ga;
					emotion ET_DELIGHT, .@gb;
				}
				if (.chatter) {
					.@w = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
					if (.@w >= 0)
						callsub L_QueueFmt, .@w, "petplay", callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@b]), ET_SMILE, 0, callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@a]);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if (.emotes && .@sec >= @cl_penext) {
		@cl_penext = .@sec + 40 + rand(51);
		.@a = callsub(L_PetPick, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0) {
			setarray .@es, ET_THROB, ET_DELIGHT, ET_SMILE, ET_LUV, ET_KIK, ET_QUESTION;
			.@e = .@es[rand(6)];
			if (@cl_camp || .@sec - @cl_still >= 120)
				.@e = ET_SLEEPY;
			emotion .@e, getd("@cl_pg_" + @cl_aid[.@a]);
		}
	}
	if (!.chatter || !callsub(L_Calm) || .@sec < @cl_talk || .@sec < @cl_soft)
		return;
	if (!@cl_ptnext) {
		@cl_ptnext = .@sec + 120 + rand(121);	// not right as you log in
		return;
	}
	if (.@sec < @cl_ptnext)
		return;
	@cl_ptnext = .@sec + 300 + rand(241);
	.@a = callsub(L_PetPick, -1);
	if (.@a < 0)
		return;
	.@pa$ = callsub(L_PetName, @cl_cid[.@a]);
	.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
	if (.@b >= 0 && rand(100) < 45) {
		callsub L_QueueFmt, .@b, "petcx", @cl_nm$[.@a], 0, 0, .@pa$;
		if (rand(100) < 70)
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "petre", @cl_nm$[.@b], ET_DELIGHT, 0, .@pa$;
	} else {
		callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "petidle", "", 0, 0, .@pa$;
	}
	return;

// Companions on your map, into @cl_* arrays (index 0..@cl_n-1).
L_Gather:
	@cl_n = 0;
	deletearray @cl_aid;
	deletearray @cl_cid;
	deletearray @cl_nm$;
	deletearray @cl_hp;
	deletearray @cl_mhp;
	deletearray @cl_sp;
	deletearray @cl_msp;
	deletearray @cl_ar;
	deletearray @cl_lv;
	deletearray @cl_cls;
	.@pid = getcharid(1);
	if (!.@pid)
		return;
	getpartymember .@pid, 1;
	.@cnt = $@partymembercount;
	if (.@cnt <= 0)
		return;
	copyarray .@cid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@cnt;
	getpartymember .@pid, 2;
	copyarray .@aid[0], $@partymemberaid[0], .@cnt;
	getpartymember .@pid, 0;
	copyarray .@nm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@cnt;
	// 3.0.1: the client colours companions' lines in the chat box (setting chat_colour)
	// and learns who they are from this, sent when the names change or after a map change.
	.@all$ = "";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
		if (.@cid[.@i] >= 95000000)
			.@all$ += .@nm$[.@i] + "|";
	}
	if (.@all$ != @cl_names$) {
		@cl_names$ = .@all$;
		dispbottom "@@event clnames " + .@all$;
	}
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
		if (.@cid[.@i] < 95000000)
			continue;	// a real player, not a companion
		.@hp = readparam(Hp, .@cid[.@i]);
		if (.@hp < 0)
			continue;	// not loaded
		if (strcharinfo(3, .@cid[.@i]) != .@map$)
			continue;	// left behind on another map
		@cl_aid[@cl_n] = .@aid[.@i];
		@cl_cid[@cl_n] = .@cid[.@i];
		@cl_nm$[@cl_n] = .@nm$[.@i];
		@cl_hp[@cl_n] = .@hp;
		@cl_mhp[@cl_n] = readparam(MaxHp, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_sp[@cl_n] = readparam(Sp, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_msp[@cl_n] = readparam(MaxSp, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_cls[@cl_n] = readparam(Class, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_lv[@cl_n] = readparam(BaseLevel, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_ar[@cl_n] = callsub(L_Arch, @cl_cls[@cl_n]);
		@cl_n++;
	}
	// 3.2.0: the client's portraits (client/portraits.js) need each
	// one's class and sex, "Name:class:sex|" (sex 0 female, 1 male).
	if (.portraits) {
		.@cast$ = "";
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
			.@cast$ += @cl_nm$[.@i] + ":" + @cl_cls[.@i] + ":" + readparam(Sex, @cl_cid[.@i]) + "|";
		if (.@cast$ != @cl_cast$) {
			@cl_cast$ = .@cast$;
			dispbottom "@@event clcast " + .@cast$;
		}
	}
	return;

// Personality from the class family.
L_Arch:
	.@ea = eaclass(getarg(0));
	.@b = .@ea & EAJ_BASEMASK;
	if (.@b == EAJ_SWORDMAN)
		return 1;
	if (.@b == EAJ_MAGE)
		return 2;
	if (.@b == EAJ_ARCHER || .@b == EAJ_GUNSLINGER)
		return 3;
	if (.@b == EAJ_ACOLYTE) {
		if (.@ea & EAJL_2_2)
			return 5;	// Monk line
		return 4;
	}
	if (.@b == EAJ_TAEKWON) {
		if (.@ea & EAJL_2_2)
			return 2;	// Soul Linker
		return 5;
	}
	if (.@b == EAJ_MERCHANT)
		return 7;
	if (.@b == EAJ_THIEF || .@b == EAJ_NINJA)
		return 6;
	return 0;

// 3.12.4: the weather here changed (getarg(0) the time, getarg(1) the map): one companion
// remarks on it once things are calm, at most every 10 minutes. Plain cloud isn't worth a
// word, and walking indoors isn't the sky clearing.
L_Weather:
	.@wx = callfunc("F_DNWeather", getarg(1));
	if (.@wx == @cl_wx) {
		@cl_wx_map$ = getarg(1);
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_incombat)
		return;
	if (!callsub(L_Calm))
		return;
	.@old = @cl_wx;
	.@same = (getarg(1) == @cl_wx_map$);
	@cl_wx = .@wx;
	@cl_wx_map$ = getarg(1);
	.@cat$ = "";
	if (.@wx > 1)
		.@cat$ = "wx" + .@wx;
	else if (!.@wx && .@same && (.@old == 2 || .@old == 3 || .@old == 5 || .@old == 6))
		.@cat$ = "wx0";
	if (.@cat$ == "" || getarg(0) < @cl_wx_cd)
		return;
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	if (.@a >= 0) {
		@cl_wx_cd = getarg(0) + 600;
		callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, .@cat$, "", 0;
	}
	return;

// A living companion: getarg(0) index to skip (-1 none), getarg(1)
// personality to prefer (-1 none). Returns an index or -1.
L_Pick:
	.@ex = getarg(0);
	.@pref = getarg(1);
	.@k = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (.@i == .@ex || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (.@pref >= 0 && @cl_ar[.@i] == .@pref)
			return .@i;
		.@c[.@k] = .@i;
		.@k++;
	}
	if (!.@k)
		return -1;
	if (.@k == 1)
		return .@c[0];	// rand(1) logs an error on this server
	return .@c[rand(.@k)];

// A random line for category getarg(0), personality getarg(1).
L_Line:
	.@v$ = "." + getarg(0) + "_" + getarg(1) + "$";
	.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
	if (!.@n) {
		.@v$ = "." + getarg(0) + "_0$";
		.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
		if (!.@n)
			return "";
	}
	if (.@n == 1)
		return getd(.@v$ + "[0]");
	return getd(.@v$ + "[" + rand(.@n) + "]");

// Fill {t} (companion getarg(1)'s title for you) and {a} (getarg(2)).
// 3.9.1: and {p}, your name. {t} is your name instead of your title when the
// companion asked to use it and you said yes (L_Title); then "the {t}" is just
// your name.
L_Text:
	.@w$ = callsub(L_Title, getarg(1));
	.@t$ = getarg(0);
	if (.@w$ == strcharinfo(0)) {
		.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "the {t}", "{t}");
		.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "The {t}", "{t}");
	}
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{t}", .@w$);
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{p}", strcharinfo(0));
	return replacestr(.@t$, "{a}", getarg(2));

// 3.9.1: text getarg(0) for the Journal: {t} as getarg(1) (its title, or your name
// when it uses it: then "the {t}" is just the name), {p} your name, {a} getarg(2).
L_JText:
	.@t$ = getarg(0);
	if (getarg(1) == strcharinfo(0)) {
		.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "the {t}", "{t}");
		.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "The {t}", "{t}");
	}
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{t}", getarg(1));
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{p}", strcharinfo(0));
	return replacestr(.@t$, "{a}", getarg(2));

// 3.9.1: what companion getarg(0) (an index) calls you: its title for you, or your
// name once it asked to use it and you said yes (m.c<id>.callname 1; 2 = you kept
// the title). By the bond (@cl_bt_<char id>, the minute's bond check): half the time
// at Companion, mostly from Trusted on, less in a fight.
L_Title:
	.@c = @cl_cid[getarg(0)];
	.@tt$ = .ttl$[.@c % .nt];
	if (modstore_get("char", "m.c" + .@c + ".callname", 0) != 1)
		return .@tt$;
	.@bt = getd("@cl_bt_" + .@c);
	.@pct = 50;
	if (.@bt >= 3)
		.@pct = 85;
	if (@cl_incombat)
		.@pct = 30;
	if (rand(100) < .@pct)
		return strcharinfo(0);
	return .@tt$;

// 3.9.1: which life story a story-choice moment's kind belongs to: "story" 1,
// "story2" 2, "story3" 3, and a class's own "story11" ... "story14".
L_MoStory:
	if (getarg(0) == "story")
		return 1;
	return atoi(substr(getarg(0), 5, getstrlen(getarg(0)) - 1));

// 3.9.1: one "hey boss" moment into the .mo_* tables (OnInit). See .mo_n's note.
L_MoAdd:
	.@k = .mo_n;
	.mo_kind$[.@k] = getarg(0);
	.mo_p[.@k] = getarg(1);
	.mo_tier[.@k] = getarg(2);
	.mo_rep[.@k] = getarg(3);
	.mo_q$[.@k] = getarg(4);
	.mo_o1$[.@k] = getarg(5);
	.mo_r1$[.@k] = getarg(6);
	.mo_o2$[.@k] = getarg(7);
	.mo_r2$[.@k] = getarg(8);
	.mo_o3$[.@k] = getarg(9);
	.mo_r3$[.@k] = getarg(10);
	.mo_x1$[.@k] = getarg(11);
	.mo_x2$[.@k] = getarg(12);
	.mo_n++;
	return;

// ---- Pop quiz (3.10.2, setting quiz) ------------------------------------------------
// Every few minutes of calm (the setting, ±25%) a companion asks you something about the
// game. The questions come from the server itself: monsters and places from
// npc/companion_quiz_data.txt (tools/quiz/make_quiz.ps1), element/race/size/level/drops
// from getmonsterinfo/getmobdrops, so the answer always matches this server. It rides on
// the moment steps (L_MomentTry) as moment .qz_k:
//   @cl_qzt$ the bubble, @cl_qzo$[] the answers (@cl_qzno: 2 or 3), @cl_qzc the right one,
//   @cl_qzr$ its text, @cl_qztag$ the bubble's look (quiz/gold/don), @cl_qzmode 1 a
//   question / 2 the "double or nothing" offer, @cl_qzx the reward times (golden 3,
//   double 2), @cl_qzdbl a double, @cl_qzb/@cl_qzg1/@cl_qzg2 an argument's other companion
//   (char id) and the two guesses (answer numbers), @cl_qzfr the frame.
// Right: 30 Cash Points times @cl_qzx, +1 m.c<id>.quiz (20 bond points, L_BondPts), and
// the streak (char store qz.streak): 5 = Old Card Album, 10 = Mystical Card Album, then it
// starts again. Wrong: the streak starts over (not in a double), no bond lost. Asked
// questions are kept (qz.seen, about the last 400) and not asked again.

// Starts a quiz at getarg(0) (s): 1 started, 0 nothing to ask.
L_QuizStart:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@n = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0 && !callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			.@can[.@n++] = .@i;
	}
	if (!.@n)
		return 0;
	.@a = .@can[rand(.@n)];
	.@b = -1;
	if (.@n >= 2) {
		.@b = .@can[rand(.@n - 1)];
		if (.@b == .@a)
			.@b = .@can[.@n - 1];	// (the last one stood in for the asker)
	}
	@cl_qzx = 1;
	@cl_qzgold = 0;
	@cl_qzdbl = 0;
	@cl_qzb = 0;
	.@mob = 0;
	if (!rand(40)) {
		// the golden question: about one in 40, worth three times
		@cl_qzx = 3;
		@cl_qzgold = 1;
		.@fr = 5;
	} else {
		// frames: 0 plain 20, 1 a monster near you 35, 2 an argument 30, 3 a bet 12
		// (24 from a Rogue or a Merchant), 4 a letter 13
		.@mob = callsub(L_QuizNear);
		.@bet = 12;
		if (@cl_ar[.@a] == 6 || @cl_ar[.@a] == 7)
			.@bet = 24;
		setarray .@w, 20, (.@mob ? 35 : 0), (.@b >= 0 ? 30 : 0), (.@b >= 0 ? .@bet : 0), 13;
		.@r = rand(.@w[0] + .@w[1] + .@w[2] + .@w[3] + .@w[4]);
		for (.@fr = 0; .@fr < 4; .@fr++) {
			.@r -= .@w[.@fr];
			if (.@r < 0)
				break;
		}
	}
	if (.@fr != 1)
		.@mob = 0;
	if (!callsub(L_QuizBuild, .@a, .@mob))
		return 0;
	if (.@fr == 2 || .@fr == 3) {
		// the two guesses: two different answers at random (the right one, or not)
		@cl_qzb = @cl_cid[.@b];
		@cl_qzg1 = rand(@cl_qzno);
		@cl_qzg2 = (@cl_qzg1 + 1 + rand(@cl_qzno - 1)) % @cl_qzno;
	}
	@cl_qzfr = .@fr;
	@cl_qzt$ = callsub(L_QuizFill, callsub(L_Line, "qzf" + .@fr, @cl_ar[.@a]), "");
	.@bn$ = "";	// (not ?: — both sides are read, and @cl_nm$[-1] ends the script)
	if (.@b >= 0)
		.@bn$ = @cl_nm$[.@b];
	@cl_qzt$ = callsub(L_Text, @cl_qzt$, .@a, .@bn$);
	@cl_qztag$ = (@cl_qzgold ? "gold" : "quiz");
	@cl_qzcome = (.@fr >= 1 && .@fr <= 3);
	@cl_qzmode = 1;
	@cl_mopc = @cl_cid[.@a];
	@cl_mopid = .qz_k;
	@cl_mos = 1;
	@cl_mot = .@sec + 1;
	return 1;

// {q} {Q} {m} {g} (getarg(1)) and {r} into quiz line getarg(0).
L_QuizFill:
	.@t$ = replacestr(getarg(0), "{q}", @cl_qzq$);
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{Q}", strtoupper(charat(@cl_qzq$, 0)) + delchar(@cl_qzq$, 0));
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{m}", @cl_qzm$);
	.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{g}", getarg(1));
	return replacestr(.@t$, "{r}", @cl_qzr$);

// An argument or a bet before the question: the other one guesses, then the asker
// (companion getarg(0), an index).
L_QuizArgue:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@b = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_qzb);
	if (.@b < 0) {
		@cl_qzb = 0;
		return;
	}
	.@k$ = (@cl_qzfr == 3 ? "qzb" : "qzc");
	callsub L_QueueRaw, .@b, callsub(L_QuizFill, callsub(L_Line, .@k$ + "1", @cl_ar[.@b]), @cl_qzo$[@cl_qzg1]), ET_GO;
	.@t$ = callsub(L_QuizFill, callsub(L_Line, .@k$ + "2", @cl_ar[.@i]), @cl_qzo$[@cl_qzg2]);
	callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, replacestr(.@t$, "{a}", @cl_nm$[.@b]), ET_SWEAT;
	return;

// A monster from the quiz list within 8 cells of you (its id), or 0.
L_QuizNear:
	if (!callsub(L_QuizLoad))
		return 0;
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	.@n = getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@m$, .@x - 8, .@y - 8, .@x + 8, .@y + 8, .@g);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n && .@j < 30; .@j++) {
		getunitdata .@g[.@j], .@d;
		if (getd(".qzi_" + .@d[UMOB_CLASS]))
			return .@d[UMOB_CLASS];
	}
	return 0;

// The quiz data (npc/companion_quiz_data.txt, another NPC) copied in once: .qz_m monsters
// (.qzi_<id> = index + 1), .qz_r$ their places, .qz_rg$ place names, .qz_a element table,
// .qz_f*$ facts.
L_QuizLoad:
	if (.qz_nqm)
		return 1;
	.@d$ = "cpo_quiz_data";
	.@n = getvariableofnpc(.nqm, .@d$);
	if (!.@n)
		return 0;
	freeloop(1);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.qz_m[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.qm[.@k], .@d$);
		.qz_r$[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.qr$[.@k], .@d$);
		setd ".qzi_" + .qz_m[.@k], .@k + 1;
	}
	.qz_nrg = getvariableofnpc(.nrg, .@d$);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .qz_nrg; .@k++)
		.qz_rg$[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.rg$[.@k], .@d$);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 400; .@k++)
		.qz_a[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.qa[.@k], .@d$);
	.qz_nf = getvariableofnpc(.nf, .@d$);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .qz_nf; .@k++) {
		.qz_fq$[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.fq$[.@k], .@d$);
		.qz_fa$[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.fa$[.@k], .@d$);
		.qz_fw1$[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.fw1$[.@k], .@d$);
		.qz_fw2$[.@k] = getvariableofnpc(.fw2$[.@k], .@d$);
	}
	freeloop(0);
	.qz_nqm = .@n;
	return 1;

// Makes a question for companion getarg(0) (an index), about monster getarg(1) when it's
// not 0 (one near you: "it"). Kinds: 0 element, 1 race, 2 size, 3 what hits it hardest,
// 4 where it lives, 5 what it drops, 6 its level, 7 who lives in a place, 8 who drops an
// item, 9 the element chart, 10 jobs/skills/cards. The asker's class leans on some kinds.
// Monsters from your level -30 to +10 (MVPs to +20, from level 40). 1 made, 0 not.
L_QuizBuild:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@mob = getarg(1);
	if (!callsub(L_QuizLoad))
		return 0;
	setarray .@w, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 6, 8, 8, 10, 14;
	switch (eaclass(@cl_cls[.@a]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) {
	case EAJ_SWORDMAN:
		.@w[2] += 25; .@w[1] += 15; .@w[6] += 10;
		break;
	case EAJ_MAGE:
		.@w[0] += 25; .@w[3] += 20; .@w[9] += 15;
		break;
	case EAJ_ARCHER:
		.@w[4] += 25; .@w[7] += 15; .@w[2] += 10;
		break;
	case EAJ_ACOLYTE:
		.@w[1] += 20; .@w[3] += 20; .@w[9] += 10;
		break;
	case EAJ_MERCHANT:
		.@w[5] += 25; .@w[8] += 20; .@w[6] += 5;
		break;
	case EAJ_THIEF:
		.@w[5] += 20; .@w[4] += 15; .@w[8] += 10;
		break;
	default:
		.@w[10] += 20;
	}
	if (.@mob) {
		.@w[7] = 0; .@w[8] = 0; .@w[9] = 0; .@w[10] = 0;
	}
	.@sum = 0;
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < 11; .@j++)
		.@sum += .@w[.@j];
	.@seen$ = modstore_get("char", "qz.seen", ",");
	freeloop(1);
	for (.@try = 0; .@try < 15; .@try++) {
		.@r = rand(.@sum);
		for (.@kd = 0; .@kd < 10; .@kd++) {
			.@r -= .@w[.@kd];
			if (.@r < 0)
				break;
		}
		.@id = 0;
		.@nm$ = "";
		if (.@kd <= 8) {
			.@id = .@mob;
			for (.@p = 0; .@p < 15 && !.@id; .@p++) {
				.@c = .qz_m[rand(.qz_nqm)];
				.@lv = getmonsterinfo(.@c, MOB_LV);
				.@top = BaseLevel + 10;
				if (getmonsterinfo(.@c, MOB_MVPEXP) > 0)
					.@top = (BaseLevel >= 40 ? BaseLevel + 20 : 0);
				if (.@lv >= BaseLevel - 30 && .@lv <= .@top)
					.@id = .@c;
			}
			if (!.@id)
				continue;
			.@mi = getd(".qzi_" + .@id) - 1;
			.@nm$ = getmonsterinfo(.@id, MOB_NAME);
			.@key = .@kd * 100000 + .@id;
		}
		deletearray .@o$;
		if (.@kd == 0 || .@kd == 1) {
			// element / race: two other names from the same list
			.@v = getmonsterinfo(.@id, (.@kd ? MOB_RACE : MOB_ELEMENT));
			if (.@v < 0 || .@v > 9)
				continue;
			.@x = rand(9);
			if (.@x >= .@v)
				.@x++;
			.@y = rand(8);
			if (.@y >= min(.@v, .@x))
				.@y++;
			if (.@y >= max(.@v, .@x))
				.@y++;
			.@nl$ = (.@kd ? ".qz_rn$" : ".qz_en$");
			setarray .@o$, getd(.@nl$ + "[" + .@v + "]"), getd(.@nl$ + "[" + .@x + "]"), getd(.@nl$ + "[" + .@y + "]");
			.@core$ = (.@kd ? "what race is {m}?" : "what element is {m}?");
		} else if (.@kd == 2) {
			.@v = getmonsterinfo(.@id, MOB_SIZE);
			if (.@v < 0 || .@v > 2)
				continue;
			setarray .@o$, .qz_sn$[.@v], .qz_sn$[(.@v + 1) % 3], .qz_sn$[(.@v + 2) % 3];
			.@core$ = "is {m} small, medium or large?";
		} else if (.@kd == 3) {
			// the element that hits hardest at its element level; the others hit normally or worse
			.@e = getmonsterinfo(.@id, MOB_ELEMENT);
			.@el = max(1, getmonsterinfo(.@id, MOB_ELEMENTLV));
			if (.@e < 0 || .@e > 9 || .@el > 4)
				continue;
			.@best = 0;
			for (.@t = 0; .@t < 10; .@t++) {
				.@v = .qz_a[(.@el - 1) * 100 + .@t * 10 + .@e];	// (a scalar: max() on an array element reads the rest of the array)
				.@best = max(.@best, .@v);
			}
			if (.@best <= 100)
				continue;
			.@nb = 0;
			.@nl = 0;
			for (.@t = 0; .@t < 10; .@t++) {
				.@v = .qz_a[(.@el - 1) * 100 + .@t * 10 + .@e];
				if (.@v == .@best)
					.@hi[.@nb++] = .@t;
				else if (.@v <= 100)
					.@lo[.@nl++] = .@t;
			}
			if (.@nl < 2)
				continue;
			.@x = rand(.@nl);
			.@y = (.@x + 1 + rand(.@nl - 1)) % .@nl;
			setarray .@o$, .qz_en$[.@hi[rand(.@nb)]], .qz_en$[.@lo[.@x]], .qz_en$[.@lo[.@y]];
			.@core$ = "what element hits {m} hardest?";
		} else if (.@kd == 4 || .@kd == 7) {
			// where it lives (its busiest place) / which of three lives there
			explode(.@rp$, .qz_r$[.@mi], ",");
			.@home = atoi(.@rp$[1]);
			if (.@kd == 4) {
				.@nw = 0;
				for (.@p = 0; .@p < 20 && .@nw < 2; .@p++) {
					.@g = rand(.qz_nrg);
					if (compare(.qz_r$[.@mi], "," + .@g + ",") || (.@nw && .@g == .@wv[0]))
						continue;
					.@wv[.@nw++] = .@g;
				}
				if (.@nw < 2)
					continue;
				setarray .@o$, .qz_rg$[.@home], .qz_rg$[.@wv[0]], .qz_rg$[.@wv[1]];
				.@core$ = "where does {m} live?";
			} else {
				.@nw = 0;
				for (.@p = 0; .@p < 20 && .@nw < 2; .@p++) {
					.@g = rand(.qz_nqm);
					.@gn$ = getmonsterinfo(.qz_m[.@g], MOB_NAME);
					if (compare(.qz_r$[.@g], "," + .@home + ",") || .@gn$ == .@nm$ || (.@nw && .@gn$ == .@wn$[0]))
						continue;
					.@wn$[.@nw++] = .@gn$;
				}
				if (.@nw < 2)
					continue;
				setarray .@o$, .@nm$, .@wn$[0], .@wn$[1];
				.@core$ = "which of these lives in " + .qz_rg$[.@home] + "?";
			}
		} else if (.@kd == 5 || .@kd == 8) {
			// its most common drop that isn't a card / who drops that
			if (!getmobdrops(.@id))
				continue;
			deletearray .@di;
			.@dn = $@MobDrop_count;
			.@it = 0;
			.@br = -1;
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@dn; .@j++) {
				.@di[.@j] = $@MobDrop_item[.@j];
				if (getiteminfo(.@di[.@j], ITEMINFO_TYPE) != IT_CARD && $@MobDrop_rate[.@j] > .@br) {
					.@br = $@MobDrop_rate[.@j];
					.@it = .@di[.@j];
				}
			}
			if (!.@it)
				continue;
			.@nw = 0;
			for (.@p = 0; .@p < 20 && .@nw < 2; .@p++) {
				.@g = .qz_m[rand(.qz_nqm)];
				if (.@g == .@id || !getmobdrops(.@g) || !$@MobDrop_count)
					continue;
				if (.@kd == 5) {
					// an item of another monster's that this one doesn't drop
					.@x = $@MobDrop_item[rand($@MobDrop_count)];
					if (getiteminfo(.@x, ITEMINFO_TYPE) == IT_CARD || inarray(.@di, .@x) >= 0 || (.@nw && .@x == .@wv[0]))
						continue;
					.@wv[.@nw++] = .@x;
				} else {
					// another monster that doesn't drop it
					.@has = 0;
					for (.@j = 0; .@j < $@MobDrop_count; .@j++) {
						if ($@MobDrop_item[.@j] == .@it)
							.@has = 1;
					}
					.@gn$ = getmonsterinfo(.@g, MOB_NAME);
					if (.@has || .@gn$ == .@nm$ || (.@nw && .@gn$ == .@wn$[0]))
						continue;
					.@wn$[.@nw++] = .@gn$;
				}
			}
			if (.@nw < 2)
				continue;
			if (.@kd == 5) {
				setarray .@o$, getitemname(.@it), getitemname(.@wv[0]), getitemname(.@wv[1]);
				.@core$ = "what does {m} drop?";
			} else {
				setarray .@o$, .@nm$, .@wn$[0], .@wn$[1];
				.@core$ = "which monster drops " + getitemname(.@it) + "?";
			}
		} else if (.@kd == 6) {
			.@lv = getmonsterinfo(.@id, MOB_LV);
			.@x = .@lv + rand(6, 15);
			.@y = .@lv - rand(6, 15);
			if (.@y < 1)
				.@y = .@x + rand(6, 15);
			setarray .@o$, "Lv " + .@lv, "Lv " + .@x, "Lv " + .@y;
			.@core$ = "about what level is {m}?";
		} else if (.@kd == 9) {
			// the element chart (Lv 1): strong (over 100%), normal, weak (under, or none)
			.@t = rand(10);
			.@e = rand(10);
			.@v = .qz_a[.@t * 10 + .@e];
			if (.@v == 100 && rand(2))
				continue;	// the plain ones less often
			.@x = (.@v > 100 ? 0 : (.@v == 100 ? 1 : 2));
			setarray .@o$, .qz_cn$[.@x], .qz_cn$[(.@x + 1) % 3], .qz_cn$[(.@x + 2) % 3];
			.@core$ = .qz_en$[.@t] + " attacks against " + .qz_en$[.@e] + " monsters: strong, normal or weak?";
			.@key = 900000 + .@t * 10 + .@e;
		} else {
			.@f = rand(.qz_nf);
			setarray .@o$, .qz_fa$[.@f], .qz_fw1$[.@f], .qz_fw2$[.@f];
			.@core$ = .qz_fq$[.@f];
			.@key = 1000000 + .@f;
		}
		if (compare(.@seen$, "," + .@key + ","))
			continue;	// asked before
		// the right answer goes to a random place
		.@no = (.@o$[2] == "" ? 2 : 3);
		.@c = rand(.@no);
		.@r$ = .@o$[0];
		.@o$[0] = .@o$[.@c];
		.@o$[.@c] = .@r$;
		copyarray @cl_qzo$[0], .@o$[0], 3;
		@cl_qzno = .@no;
		@cl_qzc = .@c;
		@cl_qzr$ = .@r$;
		@cl_qzm$ = .@nm$;
		@cl_qzq$ = replacestr(.@core$, "{m}", (.@mob ? "it" : .@nm$));
		// kept: the last 400 or so
		.@seen$ += .@key + ",";
		if (getstrlen(.@seen$) > 3200)
			.@seen$ = substr(.@seen$, strpos(.@seen$, ",", 800), getstrlen(.@seen$) - 1);
		modstore_set "char", "qz.seen", .@seen$;
		freeloop(0);
		return 1;
	}
	freeloop(0);
	return 0;

// Your answer getarg(1) (1-3) to the quiz of companion getarg(0) (an index), at getarg(2) (s).
L_QuizAnswer:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@ch = getarg(1) - 1;
	.@sec = getarg(2);
	.@ar = @cl_ar[.@i];
	.@nm$ = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	if (@cl_qzmode == 2) {
		// double or nothing: yes = one more right away, worth double
		if (!.@ch && callsub(L_QuizBuild, .@i, 0)) {
			@cl_qzx = 2;
			@cl_qzdbl = 1;
			@cl_qzmode = 1;
			@cl_qzb = 0;
			@cl_qzt$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_QuizFill, callsub(L_Line, "qzdq", .@ar), ""), .@i, "");
			@cl_qztag$ = "quiz";
			@cl_mos = 2;
			@cl_mot = .@sec + 2;
			return;
		}
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, callsub(L_Line, "qzpass", .@ar), ET_SMILE;
		callsub L_QuizEnd, .@sec;
		return;
	}
	.@ok = (.@ch == @cl_qzc);
	.@b = -1;
	if (@cl_qzb)
		.@b = inarray(@cl_cid, @cl_qzb);
	// what they say
	if (!.@ok)
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, callsub(L_QuizFill, callsub(L_Line, (@cl_qzgold ? "qzgno" : "qzno"), .@ar), ""), ET_SWEAT;
	else if (.@b >= 0 && (.@ch == @cl_qzg1 || .@ch == @cl_qzg2)) {
		// the one who guessed right gloats, the other sulks
		.@w = (.@ch == @cl_qzg1 ? .@b : .@i);
		.@l = (.@w == .@b ? .@i : .@b);
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@w, replacestr(callsub(L_Line, "qzwin", @cl_ar[.@w]), "{a}", @cl_nm$[.@l]), ET_DELIGHT;
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@l, replacestr(callsub(L_Line, "qzsulk", @cl_ar[.@l]), "{a}", @cl_nm$[.@w]), ET_SWEAT;
	} else if (.@b >= 0)
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, replacestr(callsub(L_QuizFill, callsub(L_Line, "qzboth", .@ar), ""), "{a}", @cl_nm$[.@b]), ET_SWEAT;
	else
		callsub L_QueueRaw, .@i, callsub(L_Line, (@cl_qzgold ? "qzgok" : "qzok"), .@ar), (@cl_qzgold ? ET_CONGRATULATION : ET_DELIGHT);
	.@what$ = (@cl_qzgold ? "[Golden question]" : (@cl_qzdbl ? "[Double or nothing]" : "[Pop quiz]"));
	if (!.@ok) {
		if (@cl_qzdbl)
			dispbottom .@what$ + " Not quite: it's " + @cl_qzr$ + ". Your streak is safe.", 0xFFCC66;
		else {
			modstore_set "char", "qz.streak", 0;
			dispbottom .@what$ + " Not quite: it's " + @cl_qzr$ + ". Streak back to 0.", 0xFFCC66;
		}
		callsub L_QuizEnd, .@sec;
		return;
	}
	.@pts = 30 * @cl_qzx;
	#CASHPOINTS += .@pts;
	modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".quiz", 1);
	.@st = modstore_inc("char", "qz.streak", 1);
	dispbottom .@what$ + " Right! +" + .@pts + " Cash Points (" + callfunc("F_InsertComma", #CASHPOINTS) + "), +20 bond with " + .@nm$
		+ ". Streak " + .@st + (.@st < 5 ? " (5 in a row: Old Card Album)." : (.@st < 10 ? " (10 in a row: Mystical Card Album)." : ".")), 0xFFCC66;
	if (.@st == 5 || .@st >= 10) {
		.@it = (.@st >= 10 ? 12246 : 616);
		getitem .@it, 1;
		dispbottom "[Pop quiz] " + .@st + " in a row! " + .@nm$ + " gives you a " + getitemname(.@it) + "." + (.@st >= 10 ? " The streak starts again." : ""), 0xFFCC66;
		if (.@st >= 10)
			modstore_set "char", "qz.streak", 0;
	}
	if (!@cl_qzgold && !@cl_qzdbl && !rand(10)) {
		// about one in ten: double or nothing
		@cl_qzmode = 2;
		@cl_qzb = 0;
		@cl_qzt$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, "qzdon", .@ar), .@i, "");
		setarray @cl_qzo$, "You're on", "I'll pass", "";
		@cl_qzno = 2;
		@cl_qztag$ = "don";
		@cl_mos = 2;
		@cl_mot = .@sec + 4;
		return;
	}
	callsub L_QuizEnd, .@sec;
	return;

// The quiz is over: the next in about the setting's minutes (±25%), other moments not
// for 3.
L_QuizEnd:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	@cl_qzmode = 0;
	@cl_qzb = 0;
	.@gap = .quiz * 60;
	@cl_qznext = .@sec + .@gap * 3 / 4 + rand(.@gap / 2 + 1);
	if (@cl_monext < .@sec + 180)
		@cl_monext = .@sec + 180;
	@cl_mos = 0;	// the box closed itself
	callsub L_MomentEnd, 0;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) says a line of category getarg(1) now.
L_SpeakNow:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, getarg(1), @cl_ar[.@i]), .@i, getarg(2));
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	callsub L_SayAid, @cl_aid[.@i], @cl_nm$[.@i], .@t$;
	if (getarg(3))
		callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, getarg(3);
	@cl_talk = gettimetick(2) + 6;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) says a line of category getarg(1) at its turn.
L_QueueIdx:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, getarg(1), @cl_ar[.@i]), .@i, getarg(2));
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@i];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = getarg(3);
	return;

// 3.9.1: may small talk start now? Not while a conversation is being said, nor in the
// quiet after one (@cl_hush, L_ConvEnd). Fights, falls and calls for help don't ask.
L_Calm:
	if (getarraysize(@cl_qa))
		return 0;
	return (gettimetick(2) >= @cl_hush);

// 3.9.1: a conversation's last line has been said. 30-45 s of quiet follow, so the
// next line doesn't step on it, then what its mood (@cl_cvmood, L_QueueConv) brings:
//   heartfelt: a minute of quiet, the second speaker's heart, the listener smiles or
//     answers the one who shared (.react_*); the talk stays soft 4 minutes (@cl_soft);
//     the pair's friendship +2 more (3 for the talk).
//   flirty: the second speaker blushes; often the listener teases them (.ftease_*);
//     they sit by each other for 10 minutes (L_PairBond).
//   argument: the second speaker fumes; sometimes the listener calms it (.peace_*,
//     a Priest most of all); they sulk 20-30 minutes (no talk, no sitting together:
//     L_PickFriend, L_PairBond), then make up first thing (L_Conversation); the
//     talk doesn't count toward their friendship.
//   funny: the listener laughs (an emote, or .laugh_*); 3 minutes stay light (@cl_light).
//   plain: now and then the listener teases the pair (.tease_*).
// The closing beat: the tick, @cl_cbat (2 s, @cl_cbb's emote @cl_cbe), then
// @cl_cbat2 (3 s later, the listener @cl_cbc does @cl_cbk).
L_ConvEnd:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	.@mood = @cl_cvmood;
	@cl_cvon = 0;
	@cl_cvsoft = 0;
	@cl_cvmood = 0;
	@cl_hush = .@sec + 30 + rand(16);
	@cl_cbk = 0;
	@cl_cbe = 0;
	// A listener: someone here, standing, who wasn't in the talk.
	@cl_cbc = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@x = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || .@x == @cl_cva || .@x == @cl_cvb || .@x == @cl_cvc)
			continue;
		@cl_cbc = .@x;
		.@lp = @cl_ar[.@i];
		if (rand(100) < 50)
			break;
	}
	.@rk$ = callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cvca, @cl_cvcb);
	.@sk$ = callsub(L_SulkKey, @cl_cvca, @cl_cvcb);
	if (.@mood == 1) {
		modstore_inc("char", .@rk$, 2);
		@cl_hush += 30;
		@cl_soft = .@sec + 240;
		@cl_cbe = ET_THROB;
		if (@cl_cbc) {
			if (.chatter && rand(100) < 45)
				@cl_cbk = 2;
			else if (rand(100) < 60)
				@cl_cbk = 1;
		}
	} else if (.@mood == 2) {
		@cl_cbe = ET_SHY;
		setd "@cl_fl" + .@sk$, .@sec + 600;
		if (@cl_cbc && .chatter && rand(100) < 50)
			@cl_cbk = 4;
	} else if (.@mood == 3) {
		modstore_inc("char", .@rk$, -1);
		@cl_hush += 30;
		@cl_cbe = ET_ANGER;
		setd "@cl_sk" + .@sk$, .@sec + 1200 + rand(601);
		setd "@cl_mk" + .@sk$, 1;
		if (@cl_cbc && .chatter) {
			if (rand(100) < 40 || (.@lp == 4 && rand(100) < 60))
				@cl_cbk = 5;
		}
	} else if (.@mood == 4) {
		@cl_light = .@sec + 180;
		if (@cl_cbc) {
			@cl_cbk = 6;
			if (.chatter && rand(100) < 35)
				@cl_cbk = 7;
		}
	} else if (@cl_cbc && .chatter && rand(100) < 12) {
		@cl_cbk = 3;
	}
	if (@cl_cbe) {
		@cl_cbat = .@sec + 2;
		@cl_cbb = @cl_cvb;
	} else if (@cl_cbk)
		@cl_cbat2 = .@sec + 2;
	return;

// 3.9.1: the session key for two companions (char ids) sulking (@cl_sk<key>: until
// when), owing a make-up (@cl_mk<key>) or flirting (@cl_fl<key>: until when).
L_SulkKey:
	.@x = getarg(0);
	.@y = getarg(1);
	return "_" + min(.@x, .@y) + "_" + max(.@x, .@y);

// 3.9.1: someone for companion getarg(0) to talk with, leaning toward the ones they've
// talked with least, so every pair gets its turn and the party grows close evenly
// (one companion with everyone's ear made them all its friend first). Not one they're
// sulking with. -1 for none.
L_PickFriend:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@n = 0;
	.@top = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (.@i == .@a || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (gettimetick(2) < getd("@cl_sk" + callsub(L_SulkKey, @cl_cid[.@a], @cl_cid[.@i])))
			continue;
		.@ci[.@n] = .@i;
		.@cf[.@n] = modstore_get("char", callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@a], @cl_cid[.@i]), 0);
		if (.@cf[.@n] > .@top)
			.@top = .@cf[.@n];
		.@n++;
	}
	if (!.@n)
		return -1;
	// Weight: how far behind the best-known pair, plus 20 so a close pair still talks.
	.@sum = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@w[.@k] = .@top - .@cf[.@k] + 20;
		.@sum += .@w[.@k];
	}
	.@r = rand(.@sum);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@r -= .@w[.@k];
		if (.@r < 0)
			return .@ci[.@k];
	}
	return .@ci[.@n - 1];

// 3.9.1: a chapter of someone's life story (.arc_<personality>$), if one is due. Their
// next chapter (store m.c<id>.arc = told so far) needs that bond tier with you
// (chapter 2 at Companion ... 5 at Kindred), 45 minutes together since the last one
// (m.c<id>.arcat, in minutes together), and for chapters 4 and 5 the campfire
// (getarg(0) = 1). Told to the friend they've talked with most. 1 if one was queued.
L_ArcTry:
	.@camp = getarg(0);
	if (gettimetick(2) < @cl_light)
		return 0;	// not right after a laugh
	// 3.9.1: never told to an empty room: only when you did something in the last
	// minute, and not while a story invite, a moment or a pet ask is waiting (an invite
	// is a question box too, and a new box closes the one that's up).
	if (checkidle() > 60 || @cl_stc || @cl_mopc || @cl_akc || @cl_shs)
		return 0;
	.@k = 0;
	// 3.9.1: only those near you (6 cells) tell or listen: a story from across the
	// street (a scout out on its lookout round) doesn't feel like one.
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@near[.@i] = 0;
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) == 0 && .@cm$ == .@om$) {
			if (max(.@cx - .@ox, .@ox - .@cx, .@cy - .@oy, .@oy - .@cy) <= 6)
				.@near[.@i] = 1;
		}
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !.@near[.@i] || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
		.@ch = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arc", 0) + 1;
		if (.@ch > 5)
			continue;
		if (.@ch >= 4 && !.@camp)
			continue;
		if (.@ch == 1 && callsub(L_NoStory, @cl_cls[.@i]))
			continue;	// a Novice starts no story (one already begun goes on)
		if (modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) < modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arcat", 0))
			continue;
		if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@i])) < .@ch)
			continue;
		.@ci[.@k] = .@i;
		.@cc[.@k] = .@ch;
		.@k++;
	}
	if (!.@k)
		return 0;
	.@s = 0;
	if (.@k > 1)
		.@s = rand(.@k);	// (rand(1) logs an error on this server)
	.@a = .@ci[.@s];
	.@ch = .@cc[.@s];
	// The listener: whoever they've talked with most.
	.@b = -1;
	.@best = -1;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (.@i == .@a || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !.@near[.@i])
			continue;
		.@f = modstore_get("char", callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@a], @cl_cid[.@i]), 0);
		if (.@f > .@best) {
			.@best = .@f;
			.@b = .@i;
		}
	}
	if (.@b < 0)
		return 0;
	.@line$ = callsub(L_ArcText, @cl_ar[.@a], callsub(L_StoryVar, .@a), .@ch, 0);
	if (.@line$ == "")
		return 0;
	// 3.9.1: chapter 1 is a passing talk, told now. From chapter 2 on, an invite:
	// both portraits, "X and Y are talking quietly... Listen in?" (client
	// portraits.js, "@@event clstory"). Told only on Listen (@clstory, OnCmdStory);
	// Not now, or no click in 2 minutes: it waits (L_StoryEnd). Pending: @cl_stc
	// teller's char id, @cl_stl listener's, @cl_stch the chapter, @cl_stt time out.
	if (.@ch >= 2) {
		@cl_stc = @cl_cid[.@a];
		@cl_stl = @cl_cid[.@b];
		@cl_stch = .@ch;
		@cl_stt = gettimetick(2) + 120;
		.@where$ = (@cl_camp ? " by the fire" : "");
		.@q$ = @cl_nm$[.@a] + " and " + @cl_nm$[.@b] + " are talking quietly" + .@where$ + ". Listen in?";
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@a] + " and " + @cl_nm$[.@b] + " are talking quietly" + .@where$ + ".", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@a, ET_THINK;
		dispbottom "@@event clstory " + @cl_nm$[.@a] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@a] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_cid[.@a])
			+ "|" + @cl_nm$[.@b] + "|" + @cl_cls[.@b] + "|" + readparam(Sex, @cl_cid[.@b]) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@q$);
		return 1;
	}
	callsub L_StoryTell, .@a, .@b, .@ch;
	return 1;

// 3.9.1: which life story companion getarg(0) (an index) tells: 1 (.arc_), 2 (.arcb_)
// or 3 (.arcc_). Chosen once, at its first chapter, and kept (m.c<id>.story): the one
// written for its sex (.arcsex: story 1's sex, story 2 the other), unless another of
// the same personality here already has it, then the other one, then 3, the backup.
// Personalities with one story (.arcsex -1), or one that told chapters before there
// were more stories: 1.
L_StoryVar:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
	.@p$ = "m.c" + .@c;
	.@v = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".story", 0);
	if (.@v)
		return .@v;
	.@ar = @cl_ar[.@i];
	.@v = 1;
	// The expanded classes tell their own story first (A 11-14, then B 21-24), unless
	// others here have both.
	.@cs = callsub(L_ClassStory, @cl_cls[.@i]);
	if (.@cs && modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arc", 0) == 0) {
		for (.@s = .@cs; .@s <= .@cs + 10; .@s += 10) {
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < @cl_n; .@j++) {
				if (.@j != .@i && modstore_get("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@j] + ".story", 0) == .@s)
					break;
			}
			if (.@j == @cl_n) {
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".story", .@s;
				return .@s;
			}
		}
	}
	if (.arcsex[.@ar] != -1 && modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".arc", 0) == 0) {
		.@tk$ = ",";
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < @cl_n; .@j++) {
			if (.@j == .@i || @cl_ar[.@j] != .@ar)
				continue;
			.@tv = modstore_get("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@j] + ".story", 0);
			if (.@tv)
				.@tk$ += .@tv + ",";
		}
		.@pref = 2;
		if (readparam(Sex, .@c) == .arcsex[.@ar] || .arcsex[.@ar] == -2)
			.@pref = 1;
		.@oth = 3 - .@pref;
		if (!compare(.@tk$, "," + .@pref + ","))
			.@v = .@pref;
		else if (!compare(.@tk$, "," + .@oth + ","))
			.@v = .@oth;
		else if (!compare(.@tk$, ",3,"))
			.@v = 3;
		else
			.@v = .@pref;
	}
	modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".story", .@v;
	return .@v;

// 3.9.1: an expanded class's own life story (11-14, .arcx<n>$; its second one is
// n + 10) for class getarg(0), or 0 when it has none: Gunslinger 11, Ninja 12,
// Taekwon and Star Gladiator 13, Soul Linker 14.
L_ClassStory:
	.@ea = eaclass(getarg(0));
	.@b = .@ea & EAJ_BASEMASK;
	if (.@b == EAJ_GUNSLINGER)
		return 11;
	if (.@b == EAJ_NINJA)
		return 12;
	if (.@b == EAJ_TAEKWON) {
		if (.@ea & EAJL_2_2)
			return 14;
		return 13;
	}
	return 0;

// 3.9.1: 1 for a plain Novice (not a Super Novice): no life story of their own. They
// choose a class long before the bond reaches chapter 2, and tell that class's story.
L_NoStory:
	.@ea = eaclass(getarg(0));
	return (.@ea & EAJ_BASEMASK) == EAJ_NOVICE && (.@ea & EAJ_UPPERMASK) != EAJ_SUPER_NOVICE;

// 3.9.1: the text of chapter getarg(2) of story getarg(1) (1-3, or a class's 11-14)
// for personality getarg(0); getarg(3) 1 for its title instead.
L_ArcText:
	.@v$ = "arc";
	if (getarg(3))
		.@v$ = "arct";
	if (getarg(1) >= 11)
		return getd("." + .@v$ + "x" + getarg(1) + "$[" + (getarg(2) - 1) + "]");
	if (getarg(1) == 2)
		.@v$ += "b";
	else if (getarg(1) == 3)
		.@v$ += "c";
	return getd("." + .@v$ + "_" + getarg(0) + "$[" + (getarg(2) - 1) + "]");

// 3.9.1: chapter getarg(2) of companion getarg(0)'s story, told to getarg(1)
// (indexes): marked told (m.c<id>.arc), the next one 45 minutes together later
// (.arcat), who heard it kept for the Journal (.arcl<chapter>).
L_StoryTell:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@b = getarg(1);
	.@ch = getarg(2);
	.@line$ = callsub(L_ArcText, @cl_ar[.@a], callsub(L_StoryVar, .@a), .@ch, 0);
	.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@a];
	modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".arc", .@ch;
	modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".arcat", modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) + 45;
	modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".arcl" + .@ch, @cl_nm$[.@b];
	callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@b;
	callsub L_QueueConv, .@a, .@b, -1, .@line$, "", "", -1;
	return;

// 3.9.1: the story invite is over: Not now, no answer in time, or someone left. The
// chapter waits (15 minutes together before it's offered again); the box closes
// when getarg(0) is 1.
L_StoryEnd:
	if (@cl_stc) {
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_stc;
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".arcat", modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) + 15;
	}
	if (getarg(0))
		dispbottom "@@event clstory -";
	@cl_stc = 0;
	return;

// Two companions (getarg(0), getarg(1)) talk; a third joins sometimes.
// getarg(2) the map, getarg(3) "night" / "morning" / "". What about:
//   the first time they become friends or close friends: that moment;
//   else, by chance: all three; their two personalities; the place, the
//   hour or a boss just beaten; a memory; how well they know each other;
//   anything (.conv$). The last 12 conversations aren't repeated.
// How well they know each other is the number of talks they've had,
// kept in the store (r.c<low char id>_<high char id>).
L_Conversation:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@b = getarg(1);
	.@c = -1;
	.@d = -1;
	if (@cl_n >= 3) {
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 5 && .@c < 0; .@k++) {
			.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
			if (.@x >= 0 && .@x != .@b)
				.@c = .@x;
		}
	}
	if (@cl_n >= 4 && .@c >= 0) {
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8 && .@d < 0; .@k++) {
			.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
			if (.@x >= 0 && .@x != .@b && .@x != .@c)
				.@d = .@x;
		}
	}
	// How the topic is chosen (by a roll under 100): a small party mostly
	// talks in pairs; with three or more companions the whole group joins in.
	// 3.0.0: what's left after friends (anything, .conv$) got bigger with the new lines.
	setarray .@th, 0, 15, 35, 52, 60, 72;	// four, three, pair, place, memory, friends
	if (@cl_n >= 3)
		setarray .@th, 20, 42, 55, 66, 72, 80;
	if (.@d < 0)
		.@th[0] = 0;
	.@rk$ = callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@a], @cl_cid[.@b]);
	.@fam = modstore_inc("char", .@rk$, 1);
	if (.@fam <= 0)
		.@fam = 10;	// the store refused: treat them as friends
	.@n$ = "";
	.@m$ = "";
	.@cat$ = "";
	// 3.9.1: two who argued and have cooled off make up first (L_ConvEnd, mood 3).
	.@sk$ = callsub(L_SulkKey, @cl_cid[.@a], @cl_cid[.@b]);
	// 3.9.1: the friendship moments once, by a store flag (<pair>m): a heartfelt talk
	// now counts 3, so the count can step over 6 or 20. Only near those counts, so
	// pairs long past them aren't told "Friends?" again.
	.@ms = modstore_get("char", .@rk$ + "m", 0);
	if (getd("@cl_mk" + .@sk$) && gettimetick(2) >= getd("@cl_sk" + .@sk$)) {
		setd "@cl_mk" + .@sk$, 0;
		.@cat$ = "makeup";
	} else if (.@fam >= 6 && .@fam <= 9 && .@ms < 1) {
		modstore_set "char", .@rk$ + "m", 1;
		.@cat$ = "tu1";
	} else if (.@fam >= 20 && .@fam <= 23 && .@ms < 2) {
		modstore_set "char", .@rk$ + "m", 2;
		.@cat$ = "tu2";
	} else if (gettimetick(2) < @cl_soft) {
		// 3.9.1: a few minutes after a heartfelt talk, the mood holds: no jokes or
		// easter eggs, only how well these two know each other.
		.@cat$ = "t0";
		if (.@fam >= 20) .@cat$ = "t2";
		else if (.@fam >= 6) .@cat$ = "t1";
	} else {
		.@r = rand(100);
		if (.@d >= 0 && .@r < .@th[0])
			.@cat$ = "quad";
		// 3.9.1: now and then friends clash (6%, not right after a laugh), and close
		// friends flirt a little (8%). Light only: no romance.
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@fam >= 6 && gettimetick(2) >= @cl_light && rand(100) < 6)
			.@cat$ = "argue";
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@fam >= 20 && rand(100) < 8)
			.@cat$ = "flirt";
		// 3.0.0: about one in five is an easter egg: an isekai nod, a bit of
		// Rune-Midgard lore, or a what-if about the world and its NPCs.
		if (.@cat$ == "" && rand(100) < 20) {
			setarray .@eg$, "egg_isekai", "egg_lore", "egg_npc";
			.@cat$ = .@eg$[rand(3)];
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@c >= 0 && .@r < .@th[1])
			.@cat$ = "trio";
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[2]) {
			// their pair, lower personality speaks first
			.@pa = @cl_ar[.@a];
			.@pb = @cl_ar[.@b];
			if (.@pa > .@pb) {
				.@x = .@a; .@a = .@b; .@b = .@x;
				.@x = .@pa; .@pa = .@pb; .@pb = .@x;
			}
			if (getarraysize(getd(".pc_" + .@pa + "_" + .@pb + "$")))
				.@cat$ = "pc_" + .@pa + "_" + .@pb;
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[3]) {
			.@nc = 0;
			if (@cl_lastmvp && gettimetick(2) - @cl_lastmvp < 900) {
				.@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_boss"; .@nc++;
				.@m$ = @cl_lastmvp$;
			}
			if (getarg(3) != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_" + getarg(3); .@nc++; }
			.@rg$ = callsub(L_Region, getarg(2));
			if (.@rg$ != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_" + .@rg$; .@nc++; }
			.@mk$ = callsub(L_MapKind, getarg(2));
			if (.@mk$ != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_" + .@mk$; .@nc++; }
			if (.@nc) {
				.@cat$ = .@cx$[0];
				if (.@nc > 1)
					.@cat$ = .@cx$[rand(.@nc)];
			}
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[4] && .mem) {
			.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@a];
			.@nc = 0;
			if (modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", "") != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cm_mvp"; .@nc++; }
			// 3.0.1: no counts (kills, days); where they met only when it was a town.
			.@town$ = callsub(L_TownName, modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".metmap", ""));
			if (.@town$ != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cm_met"; .@nc++; }
			if (.@nc) {
				.@cat$ = .@cx$[0];
				if (.@nc > 1)
					.@cat$ = .@cx$[rand(.@nc)];
				if (.@cat$ == "cm_mvp") .@m$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", "");
				else .@m$ = .@town$;
			}
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[5]) {
			.@cat$ = "t0";
			if (.@fam >= 20) .@cat$ = "t2";
			else if (.@fam >= 6) .@cat$ = "t1";
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "")
			.@cat$ = "conv";
	}
	.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, .@cat$);
	if (.@line$ == "")
		.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "conv");
	// 3.9.1: the mood holds. Soon after a heartfelt talk, no joke or quarrel; soon
	// after a laugh, nothing heavy. (Mood marks: L_QueueConv.)
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
		.@tg$ = charat(.@line$, 0);
		.@bad = 0;
		if (gettimetick(2) < @cl_soft) {
			if (.@tg$ == "+" || .@tg$ == "!")
				.@bad = 1;
		}
		if (gettimetick(2) < @cl_light && .@tg$ == "#")
			.@bad = 1;
		if (!.@bad)
			break;
		.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, .@cat$);
		if (.@line$ == "")
			.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "t1");
	}
	if (.@cat$ != "quad")
		.@d = -1;
	if (.@cat$ != "trio" && .@cat$ != "quad")
		.@c = -1;
	// A group talk brings everyone in it closer, not only the first two.
	if (.@c >= 0) {
		callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@c;
		callsub L_Bump, .@b, .@c;
	}
	if (.@d >= 0) {
		callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@d;
		callsub L_Bump, .@b, .@d;
		callsub L_Bump, .@c, .@d;
	}
	// 3.9.1: the heartfelt kinds: becoming friends, close friends, where they met.
	if (.@cat$ == "tu1" || .@cat$ == "tu2" || .@cat$ == "t2" || .@cat$ == "cm_met")
		@cl_cvsoft = 1;
	callsub L_QueueConv, .@a, .@b, .@c, .@line$, .@n$, .@m$, .@d;
	return;

// One more talk between companions getarg(0) and getarg(1) (the store key
// L_Conversation keeps for each pair).
L_Bump:
	.@x = @cl_cid[getarg(0)];
	.@y = @cl_cid[getarg(1)];
	modstore_inc("char", "r.c" + min(.@x, .@y) + "_" + max(.@x, .@y), 1);
	return;

// A conversation of category getarg(0) (an array .<cat>$) that isn't one
// of the last 12 used, or "" when the category has none.
L_PickConv:
	.@v$ = "." + getarg(0) + "$";
	.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
	if (!.@n)
		return "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
		.@i = 0;
		if (.@n > 1)
			.@i = rand(.@n);
		if (inarray(@cl_rec$, getarg(0) + "#" + .@i) < 0)
			break;
	}
	if (getarraysize(@cl_rec$) >= 12)
		deletearray @cl_rec$[0], 1;
	@cl_rec$[getarraysize(@cl_rec$)] = getarg(0) + "#" + .@i;
	return getd(.@v$ + "[" + .@i + "]");

// snow / desert / sea / forest, or "".
L_Region:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	if (compare(.@m$, "xmas") || compare(.@m$, "ice_dun"))
		return "snow";
	if (compare(.@m$, "moc_") || compare(.@m$, "morocc") || compare(.@m$, "sphinx"))
		return "desert";
	if (compare(.@m$, "alberta") || compare(.@m$, "alb_") || compare(.@m$, "izlude") || compare(.@m$, "iz_") || compare(.@m$, "beach") || compare(.@m$, "comodo") || compare(.@m$, "cmd_"))
		return "sea";
	if (compare(.@m$, "pay_") || compare(.@m$, "payon") || compare(.@m$, "um_") || compare(.@m$, "umbala"))
		return "forest";
	return "";

// Queue conversation getarg(3) between companions getarg(0) (A), getarg(1)
// (B), getarg(2) (C, -1 for none) and getarg(6) (D, -1 or left out for none),
// with {n} = getarg(4), {m} = getarg(5).
L_QueueConv:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@b = getarg(1);
	.@c = getarg(2);
	.@d = getarg(6, -1);
	// 3.9.1: a conversation is running (L_ConvEnd closes it once its last line is
	// said). Its mood (@cl_cvmood) from a leading mark: "#" heartfelt (1), "%" flirty
	// (2), "!" an argument (3), "+" funny (4), none plain (0). The callers flag the
	// heartfelt kinds too (@cl_cvsoft).
	.@line$ = getarg(3);
	.@tg$ = charat(.@line$, 0);
	@cl_cvmood = 0;
	if (.@tg$ == "#") @cl_cvmood = 1;
	else if (.@tg$ == "%") @cl_cvmood = 2;
	else if (.@tg$ == "!") @cl_cvmood = 3;
	else if (.@tg$ == "+") @cl_cvmood = 4;
	if (@cl_cvmood)
		.@line$ = delchar(.@line$, 0);
	else if (@cl_cvsoft)
		@cl_cvmood = 1;	// the pool's default; a line's own mark comes first
	@cl_cvon = 1;
	@cl_cva = @cl_aid[.@a];
	@cl_cvb = @cl_aid[.@b];
	@cl_cvca = @cl_cid[.@a];
	@cl_cvcb = @cl_cid[.@b];
	@cl_cvna$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
	@cl_cvnb$ = @cl_nm$[.@b];
	@cl_cvc = 0;	// (not ?: : it reads both sides, and @cl_aid[-1] ends the script)
	if (.@c >= 0)
		@cl_cvc = @cl_aid[.@c];
	.@line$ = replacestr(replacestr(.@line$, "{n}", getarg(4)), "{m}", getarg(5));
	.@line$ = replacestr(replacestr(.@line$, "{A}", @cl_nm$[.@a]), "{B}", @cl_nm$[.@b]);
	if (.@c >= 0)
		.@line$ = replacestr(.@line$, "{C}", @cl_nm$[.@c]);
	if (.@d >= 0)
		.@line$ = replacestr(.@line$, "{D}", @cl_nm$[.@d]);
	explode(.@turn$, .@line$, "^");
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@turn$); .@i++) {
		.@s$ = charat(.@turn$[.@i], 0);
		.@who = .@a;
		.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@b];
		if (.@s$ == "B") {
			.@who = .@b;
			.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
		} else if (.@s$ == "C" && .@c >= 0) {
			.@who = .@c;
			.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
		} else if (.@s$ == "D" && .@d >= 0) {
			.@who = .@d;
			.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
		}
		@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@who];
		@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@who];
		@cl_qt$[.@q] = callsub(L_Text, delchar(.@turn$[.@i], 0), .@who, .@other$);
		@cl_qe[.@q] = 0;
		.@q++;
	}
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) strolls 1-3 cells to a free spot 2-4 cells from you
// (you at getarg(2), getarg(3) on map getarg(1)). The engine's follow and
// the spacing_* rules' Hold would stop the walk, so the
// companion gets Entry_Queue_Apply_Delay for 2.5 s: a battleground queue
// timer that does nothing by itself, which the spacing Hold waits out.
L_Wander:
	.@aid = @cl_aid[getarg(0)];
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(1))
		return;
	.@ox = getarg(2);
	.@oy = getarg(3);
	// As far as the party spacing lets them stand (.near); a scout (Wanderer)
	// checks one cell further out.
	.@far = .near;
	if (@cl_ar[getarg(0)] == 3)
		.@far = .near + 1;
	.@dmin = 2;
	if (.near <= 2)
		.@dmin = 1;
	// Where the others stand, so nobody walks onto someone.
	.@nb = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
		if (@cl_aid[.@k] != .@aid && getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
			.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
			.@nb++;
		}
	}
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 10; .@k++) {
		.@x = .@ox + rand(-.@far, .@far);
		.@y = .@oy + rand(-.@far, .@far);
		.@do = max(.@x - .@ox, .@ox - .@x, .@y - .@oy, .@oy - .@y);
		.@dc = max(.@x - .@cx, .@cx - .@x, .@y - .@cy, .@cy - .@y);
		if (.@do < .@dmin || .@dc < 1 || .@dc > max(1, .@far - 1))
			continue;
		if (!checkcell(getarg(1), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
			continue;
		for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
			if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
				break;
		}
		if (.@q < .@nb)
			continue;	// taken
		sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, 2500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
		// A stroll the engine cut short (a fight, catching up with you) leaves
		// rAthena's forced-walk flag set until the unit next arrives somewhere,
		// and unitwalk refuses it till then: clear it first (quietly).
		unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitwalk .@aid, .@x, .@y;
		if (@cl_ar[getarg(0)] == 3) {
			// A scout looks about, and now and then reports.
			if (rand(100) < 15 && gettimetick(2) >= @cl_talk && callsub(L_Calm))
				callsub L_SpeakNow, getarg(0), "patrol", "", ET_STARE_ABOUT;
			else if (rand(100) < 25)
				callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		} else if (rand(100) < 20)
			callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_QUESTION;
		return;
	}
	return;

// 3.9.1: scout getarg(0)'s lookout round you (getarg(2), getarg(3) on map
// getarg(1)); the tick calls it every second. Four lookout spots about 6 cells out
// (the straight or the diagonal ones, a cell closer where the ground doesn't allow),
// walked in turn either way round. At each it stands watch about 10 s (marker
// Reuse_Limit_F: the idle_sit scout Hold keeps it there), looking about, now and then
// a word. One or two rounds, then it's done until you stop somewhere else (or
// L_Restless sends it round once more), and the idle_sit rules sit it down.
// The walk is asked for again every second until it gets there (the party's follow
// would otherwise pull it back), and given up after 8 s.
// Per scout (account id): @cl_ps_ the stop (@cl_still) this round belongs to,
// @cl_pdn_ done for that stop, @cl_pw_ the spot (0-3), @cl_pd_ the way round,
// @cl_pph_ 0 walking / 1 on watch, @cl_ptx_/@cl_pty_ where, @cl_pdl_ walk deadline,
// @cl_pn_ watch over (s), @cl_pl_ spots seen, @cl_prt_ rounds to do.
L_Patrol:
	.@aid = @cl_aid[getarg(0)];
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(1))
		return;
	.@m$ = getarg(1);
	.@ox = getarg(2);
	.@oy = getarg(3);
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	// 3.9.1: the scout keeps the watch while you stay: a round, a rest (it sits, the
	// idle_sit rule), then the next round (@cl_prx_, set when a round ends).
	if (getd("@cl_ps_" + .@aid) == @cl_still && getd("@cl_pdn_" + .@aid) == @cl_still) {
		if (.@sec < getd("@cl_prx_" + .@aid))
			return;
		setd "@cl_pdn_" + .@aid, 0;
		setd "@cl_ps_" + .@aid, -1;	// planned afresh just below
		stand @cl_nm$[getarg(0)];
	}
	if (getd("@cl_ps_" + .@aid) != @cl_still) {
		// A new stop (or another round from L_Restless): plan the round.
		setd "@cl_ps_" + .@aid, @cl_still;
		if (getd("@cl_pdn_" + .@aid) == @cl_still)
			return;	// this stop's rounds are done
		setd "@cl_pd_" + .@aid, (rand(2) ? 1 : -1);
		setd "@cl_pdia_" + .@aid, rand(2);	// straight or diagonal spots
		setd "@cl_prt_" + .@aid, 4 + rand(2) * 4;	// one or two rounds
		setd "@cl_pl_" + .@aid, 0;
		setd "@cl_pw_" + .@aid, rand(4);
		setd "@cl_pph_" + .@aid, 1;	// "on watch" for a moment: the first walk in 1-3 s
		setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec + 1 + rand(3);
		return;
	}
	if (getd("@cl_pdn_" + .@aid) == @cl_still)
		return;
	if (getd("@cl_pph_" + .@aid) == 1) {
		// On watch until @cl_pn_; then the next spot, or done.
		if (.@sec < getd("@cl_pn_" + .@aid))
			return;
		if (getd("@cl_pl_" + .@aid) >= getd("@cl_prt_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_pdn_" + .@aid, @cl_still;
			setd "@cl_prx_" + .@aid, .@sec + 90 + rand(31);	// rest, then the next round
			if (rand(100) < 40)
				callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_OK;	// all clear
			return;
		}
		// The next spot round: about 6 cells out, closer if the ground there is a
		// wall, water or someone's spot. Four tries round the circle.
		.@dir = getd("@cl_pd_" + .@aid);
		.@w = getd("@cl_pw_" + .@aid);
		// Clockwise: the diagonal spots, or straight north, east, south, west.
		if (getd("@cl_pdia_" + .@aid)) {
			setarray .@sx, 4, 4, -4, -4;
			setarray .@sy, 4, -4, -4, 4;
		} else {
			setarray .@sx, 0, 6, 0, -6;
			setarray .@sy, 6, 0, -6, 0;
		}
		for (.@t = 0; .@t < 4; .@t++) {
			.@w = (.@w + .@dir + 4) % 4;
			setd "@cl_pl_" + .@aid, getd("@cl_pl_" + .@aid) + 1;
			for (.@s = 10; .@s >= 5; .@s--) {	// 10/10 down to 5/10 of the way out
				.@x = .@ox + .@sx[.@w] * .@s / 10;
				.@y = .@oy + .@sy[.@w] * .@s / 10;
				if (checkcell(.@m$, .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
					break;
			}
			if (.@s < 5)
				continue;
			setd "@cl_pw_" + .@aid, .@w;
			setd "@cl_ptx_" + .@aid, .@x;
			setd "@cl_pty_" + .@aid, .@y;
			setd "@cl_pdl_" + .@aid, .@sec + 8;
			setd "@cl_pph_" + .@aid, 0;
			stand @cl_nm$[getarg(0)];	// a walk fails on a sitter
			break;
		}
		if (.@t >= 4) {
			setd "@cl_pdn_" + .@aid, @cl_still;	// nowhere to go round here
			setd "@cl_prx_" + .@aid, .@sec + 90 + rand(31);
			return;
		}
	}
	// Walking: there yet? (The user's test: a spot behind a building could only be
	// reached the long way round, and on the 8 s deadline the scout stood watch
	// wherever it was, far from the party.) More than 9 cells from you: the round
	// ends, it comes back. Not there by the deadline: that spot is skipped.
	.@tx = getd("@cl_ptx_" + .@aid);
	.@ty = getd("@cl_pty_" + .@aid);
	if (max(.@ox - .@cx, .@cx - .@ox, .@oy - .@cy, .@cy - .@oy) > 9) {
		setd "@cl_pdn_" + .@aid, @cl_still;
		setd "@cl_prx_" + .@aid, .@sec + 90 + rand(31);
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_F, 1, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;	// ends it (sc_end can't)
		return;
	}
	if (max(.@tx - .@cx, .@cx - .@tx, .@ty - .@cy, .@cy - .@ty) > 1 && .@sec >= getd("@cl_pdl_" + .@aid)) {
		setd "@cl_pph_" + .@aid, 1;
		setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec;	// on to the next spot
		return;
	}
	if (max(.@tx - .@cx, .@cx - .@tx, .@ty - .@cy, .@cy - .@ty) <= 1) {
		// On watch: held here about 10 s, looking about.
		.@hold = 9 + rand(4);
		sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_F, .@hold * 1000 + 500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
		setd "@cl_pph_" + .@aid, 1;
		setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec + .@hold;
		if (rand(100) < 15 && .@sec >= @cl_talk && callsub(L_Calm))
			callsub L_SpeakNow, getarg(0), "patrol", "", ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		else if (rand(100) < 45)
			callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		return;
	}
	// Still on the way: let the idle Holds go (Entry_Queue_Apply_Delay), and ask for
	// the walk again only when it stopped getting closer (the party's follow pulled
	// it off, or it's stuck): asked while still walking, the game refuses it with a
	// warning ("already been forced to walk").
	sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, 2500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
	.@d = max(.@tx - .@cx, .@cx - .@tx, .@ty - .@cy, .@cy - .@ty);
	if (.@d >= getd("@cl_pdist_" + .@aid)) {	// (the first second of a leg too: the last leg ended at 1)
		unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitwalk .@aid, .@tx, .@ty;
	}
	setd "@cl_pdist_" + .@aid, .@d;
	return;

// One companion that has sat a while gets up for a moment (the tick says
// when): it moves to a new spot by its favourite (L_Fav), or turns to look at
// you, at whoever spoke last, at a friend or about. You at getarg(1),
// getarg(2) on map getarg(0). The idle_sit rule sits it down again once the
// marker from L_FaceWalk runs out.
L_Restless:
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	.@still = .@sec - @cl_still;
	.@ox = getarg(1);
	.@oy = getarg(2);
	.@w = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_rst_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;	// down, resting to get HP/SP back, or off roaming: leave it be
		if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3 && .patrol && .idlesit)
			continue;	// 3.9.1: a scout keeps its own rhythm, rounds and rests (L_Patrol)
		.@thr = 60;
		if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3)
			.@thr = 120;	// scouts without the lookout rounds
		if (.@still < .@thr + 10)
			continue;	// not sitting yet
		if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 4 && .@sec - @cl_hurt_t < 60)
			continue;	// a Healer stays ready while someone's hurt
		.@wc[.@w] = .@i;
		.@w++;
	}
	if (!.@w)
		return;
	.@i = .@wc[0];
	if (.@w > 1)
		.@i = .@wc[rand(.@w)];	// rand(1) logs an error on this server
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(0))
		return;
	// Who it's fond of, and where they are.
	.@f = callsub(L_Fav, .@i);
	.@fx = .@ox;
	.@fy = .@oy;
	// (Two ifs: the script's && reads both sides, and @cl_aid[-1] when the
	// favourite is you stopped the tick with your character still attached,
	// which froze you: no walking, no NPCs, until a relog.)
	if (.@f >= 0) {
		if (getmapxy(.@fm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@f]) == 0) {
			.@fx = .@px;
			.@fy = .@py;
		}
	}
	stand @cl_nm$[.@i];

	// A new spot (not the Rogue, it stays where it sat): a free cell next to
	// its favourite, or anywhere near you, and it sits facing them.
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] != 6 && rand(100) < 40) {
		.@nb = 1;
		.@bx[0] = .@ox;
		.@by[0] = .@oy;
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
			if (.@k == .@i)
				continue;
			if (getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
				.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
				.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
				.@nb++;
			}
			if (getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@k]) == @cl_still) {
				.@bx[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gx_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
				.@by[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gy_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
				.@nb++;
			}
		}
		.@nc = 0;
		for (.@t = 0; .@t < 20 && !.@nc; .@t++) {
			if (.@t < 8) {
				// next to its favourite: the 8 cells round them, from a varying start
				.@x = .@fx + .rx[(.@t + .@i) % 8];
				.@y = .@fy + .ry[(.@t + .@i) % 8];
			} else {
				.@x = .@ox + rand(-.near, .near);
				.@y = .@oy + rand(-.near, .near);
			}
			if ((.@x == .@cx && .@y == .@cy) || max(.@x - .@ox, .@ox - .@x, .@y - .@oy, .@oy - .@y) > .near)
				continue;
			if (!checkcell(getarg(0), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
				continue;
			for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
				if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
					break;
			}
			if (.@q < .@nb)
				continue;
			.@nc = 1;
		}
		if (.@nc) {
			setd "@cl_gd_" + .@aid, @cl_still;
			setd "@cl_gx_" + .@aid, .@x;
			setd "@cl_gy_" + .@aid, .@y;
			if (rand(100) < 30)
				callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_SLEEPY;	// a stretch
			callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, getarg(0), .@cx, .@cy, .@x, .@y, .@fx, .@fy, 6000;
			return;
		}
	}

	// A glance: a step back and onto its spot again, which turns it to face
	// whoever spoke just now, else you, a friend, or somewhere out there.
	.@tx = .@ox;
	.@ty = .@oy;
	.@em = 0;
	.@r = rand(100);
	if (@cl_said && @cl_said != .@aid && gettimetick(0) - @cl_saidt < 10000
			&& getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_said) == 0 && .@pm$ == getarg(0)) {
		.@tx = .@px;
		.@ty = .@py;
	} else if (.@r < 50) {
		if (rand(100) < 30)
			.@em = ET_STARE;
	} else if (.@r < 80 && @cl_n > 1) {
		.@j = .@f;
		if (.@j < 0 || .@j == .@i) {
			.@j = 0;
			if (@cl_n > 2)
				.@j = rand(@cl_n - 1);	// rand(1) logs an error on this server
			if (.@j >= .@i)
				.@j++;
		}
		if (getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@j]) == 0) {
			.@tx = .@px;
			.@ty = .@py;
			if (rand(100) < 25)
				.@em = ET_SMILE;
		}
	} else {
		.@k = rand(8);
		.@tx = .@cx + .rx[.@k] * 5;
		.@ty = .@cy + .ry[.@k] * 5;
		.@em = ET_STARE_ABOUT;
	}
	if (.@em)
		callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, .@em;
	callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, getarg(0), .@cx, .@cy, .@cx, .@cy, .@tx, .@ty, 3000;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) goes to sit by the one it is most comfortable with
// (L_Fav): a free cell next to them, within 4 cells of you (getarg(2),
// getarg(3) on map getarg(1)). Where it ends up is kept in @cl_gx_/gy_<aid>
// for the ones after it; the emote for the bond comes once it's there.
L_SitBy:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	.@ox = getarg(2);
	.@oy = getarg(3);
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(1))
		return;
	setd "@cl_gx_" + .@aid, .@cx;
	setd "@cl_gy_" + .@aid, .@cy;
	// The Rogue, a lone wolf: about a third of the time it sits apart, where it is.
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 6 && rand(100) < 35) {
		callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, ET_COOL;
		return;
	}
	.@f = callsub(L_Fav, .@i);
	.@em = callsub(L_BondEmote, .@i, .@f);
	if (.@f < 0) {
		.@tx = .@ox;
		.@ty = .@oy;
	} else {
		.@faid = @cl_aid[.@f];
		if (getd("@cl_gd_" + .@faid) == @cl_still) {
			// It already went to sit. If by me, I stay: it came to me.
			if (callsub(L_Fav, .@f) == .@i) {
				callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, .@em;
				return;
			}
			.@tx = getd("@cl_gx_" + .@faid);
			.@ty = getd("@cl_gy_" + .@faid);
		} else if (getmapxy(.@fm$, .@tx, .@ty, BL_PC, .@faid) != 0)
			return;
	}
	// Already beside them.
	if (max(.@cx - .@tx, .@tx - .@cx, .@cy - .@ty, .@ty - .@cy) == 1) {
		callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, .@em;
		return;
	}
	// Taken: where the others stand, and where the ones that went to sit went.
	.@nb = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
		if (.@k == .@i)
			continue;
		if (getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
			.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
			.@nb++;
		}
		if (getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@k]) == @cl_still) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gx_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
			.@by[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gy_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
			.@nb++;
		}
	}
	// The free cell next to them that is nearest to it.
	.@bd = 99;
	for (.@dx = -1; .@dx <= 1; .@dx++) {
		for (.@dy = -1; .@dy <= 1; .@dy++) {
			.@x = .@tx + .@dx;
			.@y = .@ty + .@dy;
			if ((.@dx == 0 && .@dy == 0) || (.@x == .@ox && .@y == .@oy))
				continue;
			if (max(.@x - .@ox, .@ox - .@x, .@y - .@oy, .@oy - .@y) > .near)
				continue;
			if (!checkcell(getarg(1), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
				continue;
			for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
				if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
					break;
			}
			if (.@q < .@nb)
				continue;
			.@d = max(.@x - .@cx, .@cx - .@x, .@y - .@cy, .@cy - .@y);
			if (.@d < .@bd) {
				.@bd = .@d;
				.@gx = .@x;
				.@gy = .@y;
			}
		}
	}
	if (.@bd == 99)
		return;	// no room next to them: it sits where it is
	setd "@cl_gx_" + .@aid, .@gx;
	setd "@cl_gy_" + .@aid, .@gy;
	// It sits down facing the one it came to sit with.
	callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, getarg(1), .@cx, .@cy, .@gx, .@gy, .@tx, .@ty, 5000;
	callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, .@em;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) at getarg(2),getarg(3) on map getarg(1) walks to
// getarg(4),getarg(5) and ends up facing getarg(6),getarg(7). No script command
// turns a player-type unit, but one faces the way its last step went: so it
// walks to the cell just behind the spot first (seen from the target), and
// steps onto the spot on the next tick. Marker for getarg(8) ms, which holds
// off the spacing Hold and the idle_sit rule meanwhile.
L_FaceWalk:
	.@aid = getarg(0);
	.@gx = getarg(4);
	.@gy = getarg(5);
	.@sx = (getarg(6) > .@gx) - (getarg(6) < .@gx);
	.@sy = (getarg(7) > .@gy) - (getarg(7) < .@gy);
	.@px = .@gx - .@sx;
	.@py = .@gy - .@sy;
	sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, getarg(8), 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
	unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
	.@here = (.@gx == getarg(2) && .@gy == getarg(3));
	// Already right behind it, no facing wanted, or no room behind: straight
	// there (a glance on its own spot then just doesn't happen).
	if ((.@sx == 0 && .@sy == 0) || (.@px == getarg(2) && .@py == getarg(3))
			|| !checkcell(getarg(1), .@px, .@py, cell_chkpass)) {
		if (!.@here)
			unitwalk .@aid, .@gx, .@gy;
		return;
	}
	if (!unitwalk(.@aid, .@px, .@py)) {
		if (!.@here)
			unitwalk .@aid, .@gx, .@gy;
		return;
	}
	.@d = max(.@px - getarg(2), getarg(2) - .@px, .@py - getarg(3), getarg(3) - .@py);
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_fa);
	@cl_fa[.@q] = .@aid;
	@cl_fx[.@q] = .@gx;
	@cl_fy[.@q] = .@gy;
	@cl_ft[.@q] = gettimetick(0) + 400 + .@d * 200;
	@cl_fs[.@q] = @cl_still;
	return;

// An emote (getarg(1), 0 = none) from getarg(0) in about 4 s, once it got there.
L_EmoteLater:
	if (!getarg(1))
		return;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_pea);
	@cl_pea[.@q] = getarg(0);
	@cl_pee[.@q] = getarg(1);
	@cl_pet[.@q] = gettimetick(2) + 4;
	return;

// Bonds. Who companion getarg(0) is most comfortable with: -1 you, or the
// index of another companion here. Ties go to you, the leader.
//   with you:  5 (you hired it) + 1 a day together + 1 per 100 monsters
//              + 2 per MVP + 1 per 10 places seen (memories, m.c<id>.*);
//              Guardian x1.5 (its oath), Rookie x1.25 (looks up to you)
//   with another companion: 1 per talk they've shared (r.c<lo>_<hi>, as
//              their friendship; 3 for a heartfelt one, 0 for an argument);
//              0 while they sulk, first while they've just flirted (3.9.1)
//   a Healer: +5 to whoever was hurt last, in the last 5 minutes
L_Fav:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@best = -1;
	.@bv = callsub(L_PlayerBond, .@i);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < @cl_n; .@j++) {
		if (.@j == .@i || @cl_hp[.@j] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@v = callsub(L_PairBond, .@i, .@j);
		if (.@v > .@bv) {
			.@bv = .@v;
			.@best = .@j;
		}
	}
	return .@best;

L_PlayerBond:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
	.@b = 5;
	if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".met"))
		.@b += (gettimetick(2) - modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", gettimetick(2))) / 86400;
	.@b += modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 0) / 100 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 0) * 2
		+ modstore_count("char", .@p$ + ".maps") / 10;
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 1)
		.@b = .@b * 3 / 2;
	else if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 0)
		.@b = .@b * 5 / 4;
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 4 && @cl_hurt_aid == getcharid(3) && gettimetick(2) - @cl_hurt_t < 300)
		.@b += 5;
	return .@b;

// Bonds (3.7.0): points from the memories of companion char id getarg(0). Days
// together 5, 20 kills 1, an MVP 10, a new place 2, a gift 5, a camp night 15,
// 10 minutes together 1 (3.10.2: a pop quiz of theirs you got right 20). With the tiers in .bt_need, a party that played a week
// together is Trusted.
L_BondPts:
	.@p$ = "m.c" + getarg(0);
	.@pt = 0;
	.@met = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", 0);
	if (.@met > 0)
		.@pt = (gettimetick(2) - .@met) / 86400 * 5;
	.@pt += modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 0) / 20 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 0) * 10
		+ modstore_count("char", .@p$ + ".maps") * 2 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".gifts", 0) * 5
		+ modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".camps", 0) * 15 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) / 10
		+ modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".moments", 0) * 8	// 3.9.1: moments you answered
		+ modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".quiz", 0) * 20;	// 3.10.2: their pop quizzes you got right
	return .@pt;

// The tier (1 Acquaintance .. 5 Kindred) for getarg(0) points.
L_BondTier:
	for (.@t = 4; .@t > 0; .@t--) {
		if (getarg(0) >= .bt_need[.@t])
			return .@t + 1;
	}
	return 1;

// A tier's max HP/SP bonus on companion getarg(0) (an index), tier getarg(1):
// 2% at Companion and Trusted, 4% Sworn, 6% Kindred. Six minutes, renewed every minute.
L_BondBuff:
	setarray .@pc, 0, 0, 2, 2, 4, 6;
	.@v = .@pc[getarg(1)];
	if (!.@v)
		return;
	bonus_script "bonus bMaxHPrate," + .@v + "; bonus bMaxSPrate," + .@v + ";", 360, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, EFST_BLANK, @cl_cid[getarg(0)];
	return;

L_PairBond:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@j = getarg(1);
	.@t = modstore_get("char", callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@i], @cl_cid[.@j]), 0);
	// 3.9.1: no more x1.5 for a Merchant (sociable): with the Merchant's extra talks
	// it made the Whitesmith everyone's favourite. Sulking: nobody sits by the one they
	// argued with; just flirted: they sit together a while (L_ConvEnd).
	.@sk$ = callsub(L_SulkKey, @cl_cid[.@i], @cl_cid[.@j]);
	if (gettimetick(2) < getd("@cl_sk" + .@sk$))
		return 0;
	if (gettimetick(2) < getd("@cl_fl" + .@sk$))
		.@t += 100000;
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 4 && @cl_hurt_aid == @cl_aid[.@j] && gettimetick(2) - @cl_hurt_t < 300)
		.@t += 5;
	return .@t;

// The store key for two companions' talks: "r.c<lower char id>_<higher>".
// (Plain numbers in: min()/max() given an array element read the whole rest
// of the array, which before 2.3.2 put pairs under the wrong key.)
L_PairKey:
	.@x = getarg(0);
	.@y = getarg(1);
	return "r.c" + min(.@x, .@y) + "_" + max(.@x, .@y);

// The emote for companion getarg(0) sitting by getarg(1) (-1 you): a smile for
// friends (6 talks, or a bond of 12 with you), a heart for close friends (20
// talks), a big heart for a bond of 30 with you.
L_BondEmote:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@f = getarg(1);
	if (.@f < 0) {
		.@b = callsub(L_PlayerBond, .@i);
		if (.@b >= 30)
			return ET_BIGTHROB;
		if (.@b >= 12)
			return ET_SMILE;
		return 0;
	}
	.@t = modstore_get("char", callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@i], @cl_cid[.@f]), 0);
	if (.@t >= 20)
		return ET_THROB;
	if (.@t >= 6)
		return ET_SMILE;
	return 0;

// Speak over a unit's head, as players do. Skips one that left or fell.
L_SayAid:
	if (!unitexists(getarg(0)) || getd("@cl_ph_" + getarg(0)) == 1)
		return;
	unittalk getarg(0), getarg(1) + " : " + getarg(2);
	return;

L_EmoteIdx:
	callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_aid[getarg(0)], getarg(1);
	return;

// An emote, at most one per companion every 12 s and one on screen every 2.5 s.
L_EmoteAid:
	if (!.emotes || !unitexists(getarg(0)))
		return;
	.@now = gettimetick(0);
	if (.@now < @cl_eall || .@now < getd("@cl_ecd_" + getarg(0)))
		return;
	setd "@cl_ecd_" + getarg(0), .@now + 12000;
	@cl_eall = .@now + 2500;
	emotion getarg(1), getarg(0);
	return;

// town / field / dungeon / "" (indoors, castles, arenas: say nothing).
L_MapKind:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	if (.@m$ == "")
		return "";	// between maps (getmapflag on "" returns nothing and broke the tick, 3.1.0)
	// Towns by name first: Morroc lacks the town flag in pre-renewal.
	setarray .@towns$, "prontera", "izlude", "geffen", "payon", "morocc", "alberta", "aldebaran", "yuno", "comodo", "umbala", "amatsu", "gonryun", "louyang", "ayothaya", "jawaii", "xmas", "niflheim", "einbroch", "einbech", "lighthalzen", "hugel", "rachel", "veins", "moscovia", "brasilis", "manuk", "splendide", "dewata", "malangdo", "malaya", "eclage", "mora", "dicastes01";
	if (inarray(.@towns$, .@m$) >= 0 || getmapflag(.@m$, MF_TOWN))
		return "town";
	if (compare(.@m$, "fild"))
		return "field";
	if (compare(.@m$, "sphinx"))
		return "dungeon";
	// "in_" only at the start (in_orcs01): odin_tem and ein_dun contain it too.
	if (compare(.@m$, "_in") || substr(.@m$, 0, 2) == "in_" || compare(.@m$, "cas0") || compare(.@m$, "pvp") || compare(.@m$, "job") || compare(.@m$, "que_") || compare(.@m$, "_vs"))
		return "";
	return "dungeon";
}


// Campfire nights (3.0.0): the bonfire. Never placed itself: L_CampStart makes a
// copy next to you with duplicate_dynamic (only you see it), and puts it out with
// unloadnpc. Clicking it: sit a while, or put it out. Sprite 19420 is our own id for
// the client's bonfire (System/jobname.lub): this client has no row for 4_BONFIRE's id.
// Putting it out only marks it (@cl_cout): unloading this NPC while its own dialog was
// still open left you "talking" to an NPC that no longer existed, and no other NPC
// would talk to you until a relog. The camp tick puts it out once the dialog is shut.
-	script	CampFire	19420,{
	mes "The fire crackles warmly. Your companions are gathered around it.";
	if (select("Sit a while longer:Put out the fire") == 2)
		@cl_cout = 1;
	close;
}

// Warp Portal (3.3.0): never placed itself: L_WarpOpen makes a copy next to you with
// duplicate_dynamic (only you see it). The copy keeps this header's touch area: 0,0 =
// its own cell, like the real skill. Clicking it is a "talk", which runs this script
// from the top from anywhere on screen: then you're walked onto the cell instead
// (3.3.3: before, a click warped you from where you stood), and the step takes you
// through. The talk cursor over it is the client's for every NPC (roBrowser TYPE_NPC);
// a mod can't change it. Companions are
// players too, so they can touch it: only the one whose @cl_wp$ names this copy goes.
// It never unloads itself (rAthena refuses a self-unload): the tick closes it.
-	script	CpoWarpPortal	111,0,0,{
OnTouch:
	if (@cl_wp$ != strnpcinfo(3) || @cl_wpdm$ == "")
		end;
	getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC);
	getmapxy(.@nm$, .@nx, .@ny, BL_NPC);
	if (.@px != .@nx || .@py != .@ny) {
		unitwalk getcharid(3), .@nx, .@ny;	// a click from afar: walk in
		end;
	}
	@cl_wpend = 0;	// the tick closes it after you
	warp @cl_wpdm$, @cl_wpdx, @cl_wpdy;
	end;
}

// 3.9.0: a pet crying beside its fallen companion (L_PetMournStart). Never placed
// themselves: a copy of one goes next to the body (only you see it), with the pet's
// look and name; the tick takes it away. Six, as rAthena gives each player one copy
// per source NPC.
